We evaluate Bi-HIL on unimanual and bimanual real-robot tasks, demonstrating consistent improvements over flat and ablated variants. The results highlight the importance of explicitly modeling subtask progression together with force-aware control for robust long-horizon manipulation. For additional material, please check: https://mertcookimg.github.io/bi-hil

</details>


### [83] [STL-SVPIO: Signal Temporal Logic guided Stein Variational Path Integral Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13333)
*Hongrui Zheng,Zirui Zang,Ahmad Amine,Cristian Ioan Vasile,Rahul Mangharam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: STL-SVPIO：一种将信号时序逻辑转化为全局信息、可微分奖励塑形机制的方法，通过Stein变分梯度下降和可微分物理引擎，解决复杂STL规范下的长时域连续控制轨迹合成问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在合成复杂STL规范的长时域连续控制轨迹时面临根本性挑战：基于求解器的方法（如MILP）存在指数级扩展问题，而采样方法（如MPPI）难以处理稀疏、长时域的成本函数。需要一种能够有效处理STL嵌套鲁棒性目标结构的方法。

Method: 提出STL-SVPIO方法，将STL重构为全局信息、可微分的奖励塑形机制。利用Stein变分梯度下降和可微分物理引擎，将相互排斥的控制粒子群传输到高鲁棒性区域。该方法将稀疏的逻辑满足转化为可处理的变分推断，缓解了标准基于梯度方法的严重局部极小值陷阱。

Result: STL-SVPIO在传统STL任务的鲁棒性和效率方面显著优于现有方法。能够解决复杂的长时域任务，包括具有同步和排队功能的多智能体协调，而基线方法要么无法发现可行解，要么计算上不可行。此外，在具有非线性动力学的敏捷机器人运动规划任务中（如7自由度操作和猎豹后空翻）展示了算法的泛化能力。

Conclusion: STL-SVPIO通过将STL转化为变分推断问题，有效解决了复杂STL规范下长时域连续控制轨迹合成的挑战，在鲁棒性、效率和泛化能力方面均表现出色，为机器人任务规划提供了强大的形式化规范工具。

Abstract: Signal Temporal Logic (STL) enables formal specification of complex spatiotemporal constraints for robotic task planning. However, synthesizing long-horizon continuous control trajectories from complex STL specifications is fundamentally challenging due to the nested structure of STL robustness objectives. Existing solver-based methods, such as Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP), suffer from exponential scaling, whereas sampling methods, such as Model-Predictive Path Integral control (MPPI), struggle with sparse, long-horizon costs. We introduce Signal Temporal Logic guided Stein Variational Path Integral Optimization (STL-SVPIO), which reframes STL as a globally informative, differentiable reward-shaping mechanism. By leveraging Stein Variational Gradient Descent and differentiable physics engines, STL-SVPIO transports a mutually repulsive swarm of control particles toward high robustness regions. Our method transforms sparse logical satisfaction into tractable variational inference, mitigating the severe local minima traps of standard gradient-based methods. We demonstrate that STL-SVPIO significantly outperforms existing methods in both robustness and efficiency for traditional STL tasks. Moreover, it solves complex long-horizon tasks, including multi-agent coordination with synchronization and queuing while baselines either fail to discover feasible solutions, or become computationally intractable. Finally, we use STL-SVPIO in agile robotic motion planning tasks with nonlinear dynamics, such as 7-DoF manipulation and half cheetah back flips to show the generalizability of our algorithm.

</details>


### [84] [Spatially Grounded Long-Horizon Task Planning in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13433)
*Sehun Jung,HyunJee Song,Dong-Hee Kim,Reuben Tan,Jianfeng Gao,Yong Jae Lee,Donghyun Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出GroundedPlanBench基准和V2GP框架，用于评估和改进视觉语言模型在机器人操作中的空间可执行规划能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉语言模型在机器人操作中主要用于高层次推理和动作规划，但现有基准无法评估这些规划是否在空间上可执行，即机器人能否在实际物理环境中执行这些动作，这限制了真实世界操作能力的评估

Method: 1) 定义空间接地规划新任务；2) 构建GroundedPlanBench基准，联合评估分层子动作规划和空间动作接地；3) 提出V2GP框架，利用真实世界机器人视频演示自动生成数据以改进空间接地长时程规划

Result: 评估表明空间接地长时程规划是当前视觉语言模型的主要瓶颈。V2GP框架在基准测试和真实世界机器人操作实验中均能显著提升动作规划和空间接地性能

Conclusion: 空间接地规划是机器人操作的关键挑战，GroundedPlanBench基准和V2GP框架为解决这一问题提供了系统评估方法和有效改进途径，推动了空间可执行规划的发展

Abstract: Recent advances in robot manipulation increasingly leverage Vision-Language Models (VLMs) for high-level reasoning, such as decomposing task instructions into sequential action plans expressed in natural language that guide downstream low-level motor execution. However, current benchmarks do not assess whether these plans are spatially executable, particularly in specifying the exact spatial locations where the robot should interact to execute the plan, limiting evaluation of real-world manipulation capability. To bridge this gap, we define a novel task of grounded planning and introduce GroundedPlanBench, a newly curated benchmark for spatially grounded long-horizon action planning in the wild. GroundedPlanBench jointly evaluates hierarchical sub-action planning and spatial action grounding (where to act), enabling systematic assessment of whether generated sub-actions are spatially executable for robot manipulation. We further introduce Video-to-Spatially Grounded Planning (V2GP), an automated data generation framework that leverages real-world robot video demonstrations to improve spatially grounded long-horizon planning. Our evaluations reveal that spatially grounded long-horizon planning remains a major bottleneck for current VLMs. Our results demonstrate that V2GP provides a promising approach for improving both action planning and spatial grounding performance, validated on our benchmark as well as through real-world robot manipulation experiments, advancing progress toward spatially actionable planning.

</details>


### [85] [Safety-guaranteed and Goal-oriented Semantic Sensing, Communication, and Control for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13502)
*Wenchao Wu,Shutong Chen,Wenjie Liu,Zhibo Pang,Yansha Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向无线连接机器人系统的安全保证与目标导向语义通信框架，在满足安全要求的前提下最大化任务效能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有目标导向语义通信方法主要优化效能指标而忽视安全要求，而安全在实际机器人系统中应作为最高优先级

Method: 1) 总结典型机器人任务的安全要求和效能指标；2) 系统分析无线连接机器人系统在感知、通信和控制中的安全与效能挑战；3) 提出安全保证与目标导向的感知、通信和控制解决方案；4) 通过无人机目标跟踪案例验证

Result: 提出的GSC解决方案能显著提升安全率和跟踪成功率，分别提高2倍以上和4.5倍以上

Conclusion: 安全保证与目标导向语义通信框架能有效解决无线连接机器人系统中安全与效能的平衡问题，为实际应用提供可靠解决方案

Abstract: Wirelessly-connected robotic system empowers robots with real-time intelligence by leveraging remote computing resources for decision-making. However, the data exchange between robots and base stations often overwhelms communication links, introducing latency that undermines real-time response. To tackle this, goal-oriented semantic communication (GSC) has been introduced into wirelessly-connected robotic systems to extract and transmit only goal-relevant semantic representations, enhancing communication efficiency and task effectiveness. However, existing GSC approaches focused primarily on optimizing effectiveness metrics while overlooking safety requirements, which should be treated as the top priority in real-world robotic systems. To bridge this gap, we propose safety-guaranteed and goal-oriented semantic communication for wirelessly-connected robotic system, aiming to maximize the robotic task effectiveness subject to practical operational safety requirements. We first summarize the general safety requirements and effectiveness metrics across typical robotic tasks, including robot arm grasping, unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV)-assisted tasks, and multi-robot exploration. We then systematically analyze the unique safety and effectiveness challenges faced by wirelessly-connected robotic system in sensing, communication, and control. Based on these, we further present potential safety-guaranteed and goal-oriented sensing, communication, and control solutions. Finally, a UAV target tracking case study validates that our proposed GSC solutions can significantly improve safety rate and tracking success rate by more than 2 times and 4.5 times, respectively.

</details>


### [86] [Learning Actionable Manipulation Recovery via Counterfactual Failure Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13528)
*Dayou Li,Jiuzhou Lei,Hao Wang,Lulin Liu,Yunhao Yang,Zihan Wang,Bangya Liu,Minghui Zheng,Zhiwen Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Dream2Fix框架通过生成对抗性失败回放来训练视觉语言模型，实现机器人执行错误的自主恢复，无需真实失败数据或模拟器，将纠正准确率从19.7%提升至81.3%


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人基础模型在自主恢复执行错误方面存在不足，现有失败学习范式依赖成本高且不安全的真实数据收集或存在严重模拟-现实差距的模拟器扰动，现有视觉分析器主要输出粗糙的二元诊断而非可执行的轨迹级纠正

Method: Dream2Fix框架从成功的真实演示中直接合成逼真的反事实失败回放，通过在生成世界模型中扰动动作来创建配对的失败-纠正数据，无需模拟器；实施结构化验证机制，严格筛选回放的任务有效性、视觉连贯性和运动学安全性；使用超过12万配对样本的高保真数据集微调视觉语言模型，联合预测失败类型和精确恢复轨迹

Result: 广泛的真实机器人实验表明，该方法实现了最先进的纠正准确率，从先前基线的19.7%提升至81.3%，并成功在物理部署中实现零样本闭环失败恢复

Conclusion: Dream2Fix通过生成对抗性失败回放和结构化验证，成功弥合了失败诊断与可执行恢复之间的差距，为机器人自主错误恢复提供了一种高效且安全的解决方案，显著提升了机器人系统的鲁棒性和自主性

Abstract: While recent foundation models have significantly advanced robotic manipulation, these systems still struggle to autonomously recover from execution errors. Current failure-learning paradigms rely on either costly and unsafe real-world data collection or simulator-based perturbations, which introduce a severe sim-to-real gap. Furthermore, existing visual analyzers predominantly output coarse, binary diagnoses rather than the executable, trajectory-level corrections required for actual recovery. To bridge the gap between failure diagnosis and actionable recovery, we introduce Dream2Fix, a framework that synthesizes photorealistic, counterfactual failure rollouts directly from successful real-world demonstrations. By perturbing actions within a generative world model, Dream2Fix creates paired failure-correction data without relying on simulators. To ensure the generated data is physically viable for robot learning, we implement a structured verification mechanism that strictly filters rollouts for task validity, visual coherence, and kinematic safety. This engine produces a high-fidelity dataset of over 120k paired samples. Using this dataset, we fine-tune a vision-language model to jointly predict failure types and precise recovery trajectories, mapping visual anomalies directly to corrective actions. Extensive real-world robotic experiments show our approach achieves state-of-the-art correction accuracy, improving from 19.7% to 81.3% over prior baselines, and successfully enables zero-shot closed-loop failure recovery in physical deployments.

</details>


### [87] [Fabric Pneumatic Artificial Muscle-Based Head-Neck Exosuit: Design, Modeling, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13531)
*Katalin Schäffer,Ian Bales,Haohan Zhang,Margaret McGuinness*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于织物气动人工肌肉的头颈支撑外骨骼服，通过物理模型分析重力补偿和运动范围，验证了软体驱动在头颈辅助中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统刚性外骨骼虽然能提供头颈活动能力，但笨重且限制性强，需要更轻便舒适的软体驱动方案。针对神经疾病患者的头颈支撑需求，开发基于织物气动人工肌肉的柔性外骨骼服。

Method: 设计织物气动人工肌肉原型并建立物理模型，推导重力补偿所需的气压；进行运动范围和工作空间分析；引入颈部压缩力作为舒适度指标；比较不同执行器布局的扭矩输出以优化设计；通过测试假人和真人演示验证功能。

Result: 模型分析显示：执行器长度限制导致工作空间覆盖率为83%，重力补偿限制更显著（43%覆盖率）；模型正确预测了测量数据趋势；优化设计在侧向偏转稳定性和轴向旋转扭矩方面表现良好；演示验证了外骨骼服能提供功能性头颈支撑和轨迹跟踪。

Conclusion: 基于织物气动人工肌肉的柔性外骨骼服能够有效提供头颈支撑和运动辅助，验证了软体驱动在头颈活动辅助中的潜力，为神经疾病患者的康复和日常活动提供了更舒适轻便的解决方案。

Abstract: Wearable exosuits assist human movement in tasks ranging from rehabilitation to daily activities; specifically, head-neck support is necessary for patients with certain neurological disorders. Rigid-link exoskeletons have shown to enable head-neck mobility compared to static braces, but their bulkiness and restrictive structure inspire designs using "soft" actuation methods. In this paper, we propose a fabric pneumatic artificial muscle-based exosuit design for head-neck support. We describe the design of our prototype and physics-based model, enabling us to derive actuator pressures required to compensate for gravitational load. Our modeled range of motion and workspace analysis indicate that the limited actuator lengths impose slight limitations (83% workspace coverage), and gravity compensation imposes a more significant limitation (43% workspace coverage). We introduce compression force along the neck as a novel, potentially comfort-related metric. We further apply our model to compare the torque output of various actuator placement configurations, allowing us to select a design with stability in lateral deviation and high axial rotation torques. The model correctly predicts trends in measured data where wrapping the actuators around the neck is not a significant factor. Our test dummy and human user demonstration confirm that the exosuit can provide functional head support and trajectory tracking, underscoring the potential of artificial muscle-based soft actuation for head-neck mobility assistance.

</details>


### [88] [End-to-End O-RAN Testbed for Edge-AI-Enabled 5G/6G Connected Industrial Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13567)
*Sasa Talosi,Vladimir Vincan,Srdjan Sobot,Goran Martic,Vladimir Morosev,Vukan Ninkovic,Dragisa Miskovic,Dejan Vukobratovic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于5G/6G O-RAN的端到端测试平台，将边缘AI即服务(E-AIaaS)集成到工业机器人应用中，通过自主焊接场景展示了该平台在机器人感知、控制和通信方面的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业机器人系统需要高可靠、高吞吐量、低延迟的无线连接来支持移动性和计算密集型AI模型的卸载。传统有线连接限制了机器人系统的移动性，而边缘AI即服务(E-AIaaS)能够简化现场维护、降低运营成本，并支持灵活的AI模型生命周期管理和功能升级。

Method: 设计并部署了一个基于开放技术和接口的通用实验平台，采用5G/6G O-RAN架构构建端到端测试床。通过自主焊接场景进行演示，研究不同数据采集、边缘处理和实时流媒体方法之间的权衡，同时支持语义通信和目标导向通信等新兴范式。

Result: 成功开发了一个集成E-AIaaS的5G/6G O-RAN测试平台，能够支持工业机器人应用的端到端验证。该平台为研究机器人感知、边缘计算和实时通信之间的权衡提供了实验环境，并展示了在自主焊接场景中的实际应用能力。

Conclusion: 基于5G/6G O-RAN的E-AIaaS测试平台为连接机器人系统提供了有效的解决方案，支持移动性、AI模型卸载和灵活的功能升级。该平台为研究边缘AI服务、机器人感知和新兴通信范式提供了重要实验基础，推动了工业环境中智能机器人系统的发展。

Abstract: Connected robotics is one of the principal use cases driving the transition towards more intelligent and capable 6G mobile cellular networks. Replacing wired connections with highly reliable, high-throughput, and low-latency 5G/6G radio interfaces enables robotic system mobility and the offloading of compute-intensive artificial intelligence (AI) models for robotic perception and control to servers located at the network edge. The transition towards Edge AI as a Service (E-AIaaS) simplifies on-site maintenance of robotic systems and reduces operational costs in industrial environments, while supporting flexible AI model life-cycle management and seamless upgrades of robotic functionalities over time. In this paper, we present a 5G/6G O-RAN-based end-to-end testbed that integrates E-AIaaS for connected industrial robotic applications. The objective is to design and deploy a generic experimental platform based on open technologies and interfaces, demonstrated through an E-AIaaS-enabled autonomous welding scenario. Within this scenario, the testbed is used to investigate trade-offs among different data acquisition, edge processing, and real-time streaming approaches for robotic perception, while supporting emerging paradigms such as semantic and goal-oriented communications.

</details>


### [89] [Creating manufacturable blueprints for coarse-grained virtual robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13582)
*Zihan Guo,Muhan Li,Shuzhe Zhang,Sam Kriegman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 自动化从可进化简化设计空间到可直接制造蓝图的映射，加速新型机器人设计


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去三十年，大量虚拟智能体在计算机模拟中进化，但极少能转化为物理机器人，因为进化在便于自由形态生成的抽象层次进行，忽略了功能性部件的物理细节，导致设计粗糙且不确定，需要大量专业工作才能转化为可制造格式

Method: 开发自动化管道，将简化设计空间映射到完整制造蓝图，通过嵌入电机、电子元件、电池和布线的结构和功能语义到抽象虚拟设计中，逐步解决制造约束；管道可接受用户定义或AI生成的"草图"作为输入

Result: 创建了从可进化抽象设计到可直接制造蓝图的自动化转换框架，为加速新型机器人设计提供了多功能解决方案

Conclusion: 该自动化管道解决了虚拟进化设计到物理实现的关键障碍，通过嵌入制造约束语义，使抽象设计可直接转化为可制造蓝图，显著加速了新型机器人的设计过程

Abstract: Over the past three decades, countless embodied yet virtual agents have freely evolved inside computer simulations, but vanishingly few were realized as physical robots. This is because evolution was conducted at a level of abstraction that was convenient for freeform body generation (creation, mutation, recombination) but swept away almost all of the physical details of functional body parts. The resulting designs were crude and underdetermined, requiring considerable effort and expertise to convert into a manufacturable format. Here, we automate this mapping from simplified design spaces that are readily evolvable to complete blueprints that can be directly followed by a builder. The pipeline incrementally resolves manufacturing constraints by embedding the structural and functional semantics of motors, electronics, batteries, and wiring into the abstract virtual design. In lieu of evolution, a user-defined or AI-generated ``sketch'' of a body plan can also be fed as input to the pipeline, providing a versatile framework for accelerating the design of novel robots.

</details>


### [90] [Sonar-MASt3R: Real-Time Opti-Acoustic Fusion in Turbid, Unstructured Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13585)
*Amy Phung,Richard Camilli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Sonar-MASt3R方法，通过融合光学相机和声呐数据，在浑浊水下环境中实现实时密集3D重建


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水下干预感知系统依赖光学相机数据，在能见度差或光照条件不佳时性能受限。现有光声融合方法无法实现实时密集3D重建，且缺乏在浑浊环境中的验证结果

Method: 提出Sonar-MASt3R方法，使用MASt3R从光学相机数据中实时提取密集对应关系，并结合声呐3D重建的几何线索，确保在浑浊条件下的鲁棒性

Result: 在浊度值范围<0.5到>12 NTU的浑浊环境中记录的实验数据显示，该方法相对于基线方法在浑浊条件下的鲁棒性显著提高

Conclusion: Sonar-MASt3R方法通过有效融合光学和声呐数据，解决了水下浑浊环境中实时密集3D重建的挑战，为水下干预操作提供了更可靠的感知能力

Abstract: Underwater intervention is an important capability in several marine domains, with numerous industrial, scientific, and defense applications. However, existing perception systems used during intervention operations rely on data from optical cameras, which limits capabilities in poor visibility or lighting conditions. Prior work has examined opti-acoustic fusion methods, which use sonar data to resolve the depth ambiguity of the camera data while using camera data to resolve the elevation angle ambiguity of the sonar data. However, existing methods cannot achieve dense 3D reconstructions in real-time, and few studies have reported results from applying these methods in a turbid environment. In this work, we propose the opti-acoustic fusion method Sonar-MASt3R, which uses MASt3R to extract dense correspondences from optical camera data in real-time and pairs it with geometric cues from an acoustic 3D reconstruction to ensure robustness in turbid conditions. Experimental results using data recorded from an opti-acoustic eye-in-hand configuration across turbidity values ranging from <0.5 to >12 NTU highlight this method's improved robustness to turbidity relative to baseline methods.

</details>


### [91] [Beyond Binary Success: Sample-Efficient and Statistically Rigorous Robot Policy Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13616)
*David Snyder,Apurva Badithela,Nikolai Matni,George Pappas,Anirudha Majumdar,Masha Itkina,Haruki Nishimura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于安全任意时间有效推断(SAVI)的机器人策略评估框架，实现样本高效、统计严谨的评估，支持多种评价指标，相比传统方法减少70%评估负担


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人操作策略能力不断提升，但真实世界硬件测试资源有限，需要更信息化的性能度量和可靠高效的评估方法来准确评估模型能力和领域进展

Method: 基于安全任意时间有效推断(SAVI)的序列测试框架，支持早期停止，当积累足够统计证据时即可在预定置信水平下做出决策，统一处理从离散部分任务进展到连续奖励等多种指标

Result: 相比标准批量方法减少70%评估负担，相比最先进的二元结果序列方法减少50%评估负担，且不损失统计严谨性；使用细粒度任务进展比二元成功指标能更快区分竞争策略

Conclusion: 提出的SAVI框架为机器人策略比较提供了样本高效、统计严谨且广泛适用的评估方法，能显著减少评估负担并支持更信息化的性能度量，有助于推动机器人学习领域的进展

Abstract: Generalist robot manipulation policies are becoming increasingly capable, but are limited in evaluation to a small number of hardware rollouts. This strong resource constraint in real-world testing necessitates both more informative performance measures and reliable and efficient evaluation procedures to properly assess model capabilities and benchmark progress in the field. This work presents a novel framework for robot policy comparison that is sample-efficient, statistically rigorous, and applicable to a broad set of evaluation metrics used in practice. Based on safe, anytime-valid inference (SAVI), our test procedure is sequential, allowing the evaluator to stop early when sufficient statistical evidence has accumulated to reach a decision at a pre-specified level of confidence. Unlike previous work developed for binary success, our unified approach addresses a wide range of informative metrics: from discrete partial credit task progress to continuous measures of episodic reward or trajectory smoothness, spanning both parametric and nonparametric comparison problems. Through extensive validation on simulated and real-world evaluation data, we demonstrate up to 70% reduction in evaluation burden compared to standard batch methods and up to 50% reduction compared to state-of-the-art sequential procedures designed for binary outcomes, with no loss of statistical rigor. Notably, our empirical results show that competing policies can be separated more quickly when using fine-grained task progress than binary success metrics.

</details>


### [92] [SAATT Nav: a Socially Aware Autonomous Transparent Transportation Navigation Framework for Wheelchairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13698)
*Yutong Zhang,Shaiv Y. Mehra,Bradley S. Duerstock,Juan P. Wachs*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SAATT框架利用LLM实现社交感知的轮椅自主导航，通过意图预测和透明决策提升用户信任和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动轮椅虽减轻体力负担但增加认知负荷，现有自主系统缺乏社交感知和决策透明度，导致用户和他人感知的安全性和信任度降低。

Method: 提出SAATT导航框架，使用LLM作为决策器，结合用户意图预测和他人意图预测，实现社交情境检测与导航，并通过文本方式透明展示决策推理过程。

Result: 在三种不同社交水平的模拟环境中，SAATT在多数社交情境中表现优于标准全局规划器、代表性竞争模型和消融研究，在安全、社交合规、效率和舒适度八个指标上整体表现更优或相当。

Conclusion: SAATT框架展示了社交感知和透明自主导航系统在辅助轮椅用户方面的潜力，能有效处理社交情境并提升用户信任。

Abstract: While powered wheelchairs reduce physical fatigue as opposed to manual wheelchairs for individuals with mobility impairment, they demand high cognitive workload due to information processing, decision making and motor coordination. Current autonomous systems lack social awareness in navigation and transparency in decision-making, leading to decreased perceived safety and trust from the user and others in context. This work proposes Socially Aware Autonomous Transparent Transportation (SAATT) Navigation framework for wheelchairs as a potential solution. By implementing a Large Language Model (LLM) informed of user intent and capable of predicting other peoples' intent as a decision-maker for its local controller, it is able to detect and navigate social situations, such as passing pedestrians or a pair conversing. Furthermore, the LLM textually communicates its reasoning at each waypoint for transparency. In this experiment, it is compared against a standard global planner, a representative competing social navigation model, and an Ablation study in three simulated environments varied by social levels in eight metrics categorized under Safety, Social Compliance, Efficiency, and Comfort. Overall, SAATT Nav outperforms in most social situations and equivalently or only slightly worse in the remaining metrics, demonstrating the potential of a socially aware and transparent autonomous navigation system to assist wheelchair users.

</details>


### [93] [REFINE-DP: Diffusion Policy Fine-tuning for Humanoid Loco-manipulation via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13707)
*Zhaoyuan Gu,Yipu Chen,Zimeng Chai,Alfred Cueva,Thong Nguyen,Yifan Wu,Huishu Xue,Minji Kim,Isaac Legene,Fukang Liu,Matthew Kim,Ayan Barula,Yongxin Chen,Ye Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: REFINE-DP：一种通过强化学习微调扩散策略的分层框架，用于人形机器人运动操作任务，解决了离线训练的运动规划器与低级控制器解耦导致的跟踪不良和分布偏移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人的运动操作需要高层运动规划与低层全身控制的协调，但现有的扩散策略在部署到人形机器人时面临关键挑战：离线训练的运动规划器与低级控制器解耦，导致命令跟踪不良、分布偏移累积和任务失败。扩大演示数据规模对于高维人形系统成本过高。

Method: 提出REFINE-DP分层框架，联合优化扩散策略高层规划器和基于强化学习的低层运动操作控制器。通过PPO-based扩散策略梯度微调扩散策略以提高任务成功率，同时更新控制器以准确跟踪规划器演化的命令分布，减少导致运动质量下降的分布不匹配。

Result: 在模拟环境中，REFINE-DP在人形机器人执行门穿越和长时程物体运输等运动操作任务中实现了超过90%的成功率，即使在预训练数据中未见过的分布外情况下也能成功。在真实世界动态环境中实现了平滑的自主任务执行。

Conclusion: REFINE-DP显著优于预训练的扩散策略基线，证明了强化学习微调是实现可靠人形机器人运动操作的关键。该方法通过联合优化高层规划和低层控制，有效解决了分布不匹配问题，提高了系统的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Humanoid loco-manipulation requires coordinated high-level motion plans with stable, low-level whole-body execution under complex robot-environment dynamics and long-horizon tasks. While diffusion policies (DPs) show promise for learning from demonstrations, deploying them on humanoids poses critical challenges: the motion planner trained offline is decoupled from the low-level controller, leading to poor command tracking, compounding distribution shift, and task failures. The common approach of scaling demonstration data is prohibitively expensive for high-dimensional humanoid systems. To address this challenge, we present REFINE-DP (REinforcement learning FINE-tuning of Diffusion Policy), a hierarchical framework that jointly optimizes a DP high-level planner and an RL-based low-level loco-manipulation controller. The DP is fine-tuned via a PPO-based diffusion policy gradient to improve task success rate, while the controller is simultaneously updated to accurately track the planner's evolving command distribution, reducing the distributional mismatch that degrades motion quality. We validate REFINE-DP on a humanoid robot performing loco-manipulation tasks, including door traversal and long-horizon object transport. REFINE-DP achieves an over $90\%$ success rate in simulation, even in out-of-distribution cases not seen in the pre-trained data, and enables smooth autonomous task execution in real-world dynamic environments. Our proposed method substantially outperforms pre-trained DP baselines and demonstrates that RL fine-tuning is key to reliable humanoid loco-manipulation. https://refine-dp.github.io/REFINE-DP/

</details>


### [94] [LPV-MPC for Lateral Control in Full-Scale Autonomous Racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13732)
*Hassan Jardali,Ihab S. Mohamed,Durgakant Pushp,Lantao Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出线性参数变化模型预测控制器（LPV-MPC）用于高速自动驾驶赛车横向控制，在IAC AV-24平台上实现超过160 mph（71.5 m/s）的稳定性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶赛车需要选择在车载系统计算限制内运行且满足有限赛道时间和高成本等操作约束的最优控制器

Method: 设计线性参数变化模型预测控制器（LPV-MPC），详细说明控制器设计、模型参数提取方法以及关键系统级和实施考虑因素

Result: 在IAC AV-24平台上实现稳定性能，速度超过160 mph（71.5 m/s），提供了最终比赛运行结果，包括车辆动力学和控制器性能的全面分析

Conclusion: LPV-MPC控制器在高速自动驾驶赛车横向控制中表现出稳定性能，框架的Python实现已公开可用

Abstract: Autonomous racing has attracted significant attention recently, presenting challenges in selecting an optimal controller that operates within the onboard system's computational limits and meets operational constraints such as limited track time and high costs. This paper introduces a Linear Parameter-Varying Model Predictive Controller (LPV-MPC) for lateral control. Implemented on an IAC AV-24, the controller achieved stable performance at speeds exceeding 160 mph (71.5 m/s). We detail the controller design, the methodology for extracting model parameters, and key system-level and implementation considerations. Additionally, we report results from our final race run, providing a comprehensive analysis of both vehicle dynamics and controller performance. A Python implementation of the framework is available at: https://tinyurl.com/LPV-MPC-acados

</details>


### [95] [Implicit Maximum Likelihood Estimation for Real-time Generative Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13733)
*Grayson Lee,Minh Bui,Shuzi Zhou,Yankai Li,Mo Chen,Ke Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: IMLE作为轨迹规划的生成模型替代方案，相比扩散模型实现两个数量级的推理速度提升，更适合实时MPC应用


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在轨迹规划中表现出色但推理速度慢，不适用于需要快速生成和持续适应变化环境的实时应用如闭环MPC

Method: 研究隐式最大似然估计（IMLE）作为轨迹规划的生成建模方法，该方法提供良好的模态覆盖同时实现快速推理

Result: IMLE在标准离线强化学习基准测试中达到与扩散模型相当的性能，同时在开环和闭环设置中显著提升规划速度，在实时人类导航场景中验证了其快速自适应规划能力

Conclusion: IMLE是轨迹规划中扩散模型的有效替代方案，特别适用于需要快速推理的实时MPC应用，在保持性能的同时大幅提升计算效率

Abstract: Diffusion-based models have recently shown strong performance in trajectory planning, as they are capable of capturing diverse, multimodal distributions of complex behaviors. A key limitation of these models is their slow inference speed, which results from the iterative denoising process. This makes them less suitable for real-time applications such as closed-loop model predictive control (MPC), where plans must be generated quickly and adapted continuously to a changing environment. In this paper, we investigate Implicit Maximum Likelihood Estimation (IMLE) as an alternative generative modeling approach for planning. IMLE offers strong mode coverage while enabling inference that is two orders of magnitude faster, making it particularly well suited for real-time MPC tasks. Our results demonstrate that IMLE achieves competitive performance on standard offline reinforcement learning benchmarks compared to the standard diffusion-based planner, while substantially improving planning speed in both open-loop and closed-loop settings. We further validate IMLE in a closed-loop human navigation scenario, operating in real-time, demonstrating how it enables rapid and adaptive plan generation in dynamic environments.

</details>


### [96] [Multi-Robot Coordination for Planning under Context Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13748)
*Pulkit Rustagi,Kyle Hollins Wray,Sandhya Saisubramanian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种多机器人上下文不确定随机最短路径问题(MR-CUSSP)的解决方案，通过两阶段方法协调机器人收集信息推断上下文，然后基于上下文优先级进行路径规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中机器人操作环境通常存在未知的上下文(context)，这些上下文决定了目标优先级。如果基于错误的上下文行动，可能导致行为不协调和不安全。多机器人需要协调收集信息来推断真实上下文，然后根据上下文诱导的优先级优化任务特定目标。

Method: 提出两阶段解决方案：1) CIMOP(协调推理多目标规划)计算引导机器人前往信息丰富地标以高效推断真实上下文的计划；2) LCBS(词典冲突搜索)用于基于上下文诱导的词典目标优先级进行无碰撞多机器人路径规划。

Result: 在三个模拟领域评估了算法，并在五个移动机器人的salp领域设置中展示了实际应用性。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了MR-CUSSP问题的形式化定义和两阶段解决方案，能够有效处理多机器人在未知上下文环境中的协调推理和优先级路径规划问题。

Abstract: Real-world robots often operate in settings where objective priorities depend on the underlying context of operation. When the underlying context is unknown apriori, multiple robots may have to coordinate to gather informative observations to infer the context, since acting based on an incorrect context can lead to misaligned and unsafe behavior. Once the underlying true context is inferred, the robots optimize their task-specific objectives in the preference order induced by the context. We formalize this problem as a Multi-Robot Context-Uncertain Stochastic Shortest Path (MR-CUSSP), which captures context-relevant information at landmark states through joint observations. Our two-stage solution approach is composed of: (1) CIMOP (Coordinated Inference for Multi-Objective Planning) to compute plans that guide robots toward informative landmarks to efficiently infer the true context, and (2) LCBS (Lexicographic Conflict-Based Search) for collision-free multi-robot path planning with lexicographic objective preferences, induced by the context. We evaluate the algorithms using three simulated domains and demonstrate its practical applicability using five mobile robots in the salp domain setup.

</details>


### [97] [Exploration-assisted Bottleneck Transition Toward Robust and Data-efficient Deformable Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13756)
*Yujiro Onishi,Ryo Takizawa,Yoshiyuki Ohmura,Yasuo Kuniyoshi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ExBot框架通过引入瓶颈状态和双动作原语，解决了可变形物体操作中的分布外状态问题，实现了数据高效性和对严重OOD场景的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习在机器人操作中表现优异，但在可变形物体操作中面临分布外状态挑战。DOM的近乎无限配置使得全面数据收集不可行，现有方法需要大量数据或高精度感知，这在DOM中不切实际。

Method: 提出ExBot框架：1) 引入瓶颈状态作为任务执行的标准化起点，将OOD问题转化为将多样初始状态转移到瓶颈状态；2) 基于可识别性划分OOD状态空间，采用双动作原语处理可识别和不可识别状态，无需精确感知。

Result: 在绳子和布料操作的真实世界实验中，ExBot成功从包括严重自遮挡在内的多种OOD状态完成任务，展示了数据效率和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: ExBot通过瓶颈状态转换和探索辅助的感知条件改变，有效解决了DOM中的OOD挑战，减少了演示需求并增强了对感知缺陷的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Imitation learning has demonstrated impressive results in robotic manipulation but fails under out-of-distribution (OOD) states. This limitation is particularly critical in Deformable Object Manipulation (DOM), where the near-infinite possible configurations render comprehensive data collection infeasible. Although several methods address OOD states, they typically require exhaustive data or highly precise perception. Such requirements are often impractical for DOM owing to its inherent complexities, including self-occlusion. To address the OOD problem in DOM, we propose a novel framework, Exploration-assisted Bottleneck Transition for Deformable Object Manipulation (ExBot), which addresses the OOD challenge through two key advantages. First, we introduce bottleneck states, standardized configurations that serve as starting points for task execution. This enables the reconceptualization of OOD challenges as the problem of transitioning diverse initial states to these bottleneck states, significantly reducing demonstration requirements. Second, to account for imperfect perception, we partition the OOD state space based on recognizability and employ dual action primitives. This approach enables ExBot to manipulate even unrecognizable states without requiring accurate perception. By concentrating demonstrations around bottleneck states and leveraging exploration to alter perceptual conditions, ExBot achieves both data efficiency and robustness to severe OOD scenarios. Real-world experiments on rope and cloth manipulation demonstrate successful task completion from diverse OOD states, including severe self-occlusions.

</details>


### [98] [KoopmanFlow: Spectrally Decoupled Generative Control Policy via Koopman Structural Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13781)
*Chengsi Yao,Ge Wang,Kai Kang,Shenhao Yan,Jiahao Yang,Fan Feng,Honghao Cai,Xianxian Zeng,Rongjun Chen,Yiming Zhao,Yatong Han,Xi Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: KoopmanFlow是一种参数高效的生成控制策略，通过Koopman启发的结构归纳偏置，在统一多模态潜在空间中分离宏观和瞬态分支，实现稳定全局运动和高频局部修正的协同建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成控制策略在机器人操作中具有巨大潜力，但难以同时建模稳定的全局运动和高频的局部修正。现有方法使用均匀时间积分方案，在实时滚动时域控制中压缩为单步时，会平滑掉稀疏高频瞬态与低频稳态之间的纠缠动态。

Method: 提出KoopmanFlow：1）在统一多模态潜在空间中进行视觉条件化；2）在终端阶段分叉生成：宏观分支通过单步一致性训练锚定慢变轨迹，瞬态分支使用流匹配隔离由突发视觉线索（如接触或遮挡）刺激的高频残差；3）采用显式谱先验和新型非对称一致性目标优化；4）建立融合协同训练机制，避免多阶段误差累积。

Result: 在需要敏捷干扰抑制的接触丰富任务中，KoopmanFlow显著优于最先进的基线方法。通过用延迟缓冲换取更丰富的结构先验，在实时部署限制内实现了优越的控制保真度和参数效率。

Conclusion: KoopmanFlow通过解耦宏观和瞬态动态，实现了生成控制策略在稳定全局运动和高频局部修正方面的协同建模，为实时机器人操作提供了参数高效且性能优越的解决方案。

Abstract: Generative Control Policies (GCPs) show immense promise in robotic manipulation but struggle to simultaneously model stable global motions and high-frequency local corrections. While modern architectures extract multi-scale spatial features, their underlying Probability Flow ODEs apply a uniform temporal integration schedule. Compressed to a single step for real-time Receding Horizon Control (RHC), uniform ODE solvers mathematically smooth over sparse, high-frequency transients entangled within low-frequency steady states. To decouple these dynamics without accumulating pipelined errors, we introduce KoopmanFlow, a parameter-efficient generative policy guided by a Koopman-inspired structural inductive bias. Operating in a unified multimodal latent space with visual context, KoopmanFlow bifurcates generation at the terminal stage. Because visual conditioning occurs before spectral decomposition, both branches are visually guided yet temporally specialized. A macroscopic branch anchors slow-varying trajectories via single-step Consistency Training, while a transient branch uses Flow Matching to isolate high-frequency residuals stimulated by sudden visual cues (e.g., contacts or occlusions). Guided by an explicit spectral prior and optimized via a novel asymmetric consistency objective, KoopmanFlow establishes a fused co-training mechanism. This allows the variant branch to absorb localized dynamics without multi-stage error accumulation. Extensive experiments show KoopmanFlow significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in contact-rich tasks requiring agile disturbance rejection. By trading a surplus latency buffer for a richer structural prior, KoopmanFlow achieves superior control fidelity and parameter efficiency within real-time deployment limits.

</details>


### [99] [Your Vision-Language-Action Model Already Has Attention Heads For Path Deviation Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13782)
*Jaehwan Jeong,Evelyn Zhu,Jinying Lin,Emmanuel Jaimes,Tuan-Anh Vu,Jungseock Joo,Sangpil Kim,M. Khalid Jawed*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练的异常检测框架，通过监控VLA模型中少数导航注意力头来实时检测视觉推理幻觉导致的路径偏差，并触发轻量RL策略进行最短路径回滚恢复


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在导航任务中表现出色，但存在视觉推理幻觉导致轨迹偏差的问题。传统方法需要训练外部评判模块或依赖复杂不确定性启发式方法，计算开销大。本文发现通过监控冻结VLA模型中的少数注意力头即可准确检测路径偏差，无需额外计算开销。

Method: 1. 发现并识别VLA模型中的"导航头"——这些注意力头固有地捕捉历史视觉序列与语言指令之间的时空因果关系；2. 提出无需训练的异常检测框架，实时监控这些头的信号来检测幻觉；3. 检测到偏差后，绕过重型VLA模型，触发轻量强化学习策略执行最短路径回滚恢复；4. 将整个检测到恢复的流水线集成到物理机器人上验证实用性。

Result: 1. 在超过一千个注意力头中，仅需组合3个头即可实现44.6%的偏差检测率，假阳性率仅为11.7%；2. 检测框架无需训练，计算开销低；3. 成功将检测到恢复的完整流水线集成到物理机器人上，证明了实际鲁棒性；4. 所有源代码将公开可用。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种新颖的、无需训练的方法来解决VLA模型中的视觉推理幻觉问题。通过识别和监控固有的导航注意力头，实现了高效的路径偏差检测，并结合轻量RL策略进行恢复。该方法计算效率高，无需额外训练，在实际机器人导航中表现出良好的鲁棒性，为VLA模型的可靠性提升提供了新思路。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong potential for predicting semantic actions in navigation tasks, demonstrating the ability to reason over complex linguistic instructions and visual contexts. However, they are fundamentally hindered by visual-reasoning hallucinations that lead to trajectory deviations. Addressing this issue has conventionally required training external critic modules or relying on complex uncertainty heuristics. In this work, we discover that monitoring a few attention heads within a frozen VLA model can accurately detect path deviations without incurring additional computational overhead. We refer to these heads, which inherently capture the spatiotemporal causality between historical visual sequences and linguistic instructions, as Navigation Heads. Using these heads, we propose an intuitive, training-free anomaly-detection framework that monitors their signals to detect hallucinations in real time. Surprisingly, among over a thousand attention heads, a combination of just three is sufficient to achieve a 44.6 % deviation detection rate with a low false-positive rate of 11.7 %. Furthermore, upon detecting a deviation, we bypass the heavy VLA model and trigger a lightweight Reinforcement Learning (RL) policy to safely execute a shortest-path rollback. By integrating this entire detection-to-recovery pipeline onto a physical robot, we demonstrate its practical robustness. All source code will be publicly available.

</details>


### [100] [Robust Sim-to-Real Cloth Untangling through Reduced-Resolution Observations via Adaptive Force-Difference Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13785)
*Yoshihisa Tsurumine,Yuki Kadokawa,Kohei Hayashi,Christian Diehm,Takamitsu Matsubara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出ADQ方法，通过自适应力差量化来改善布料解缠任务的模拟到真实转移，强调粗粒度力变化模式而非精确力值，提高策略鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 布料解缠任务需要适应变化的接触和张力条件，但大规模真实世界训练不切实际（布料损坏、硬件磨损）。模拟到真实转移是可行方案，但布料操作对交互动力学高度敏感，依赖精确力值的策略因现实差距而失败。作者观察到解缠主要由定性张力转变而非精确力值表征，直接最小化原始力测量的模拟-真实差距不一定与任务结构对齐。

Method: 提出自适应力差量化（ADQ）方法：1）通过将力输入表示为离散化的时间差来降低观测分辨率；2）自适应学习状态依赖的量化阈值。这种表示方法减轻了对环境特定力特征的过拟合，促进直接的模拟到真实转移。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界布料解缠实验中，ADQ相比使用原始力输入的策略实现了更高的成功率，并在模拟到真实转移中表现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 强调粗粒度力变化模式同时抑制细粒度环境依赖变化能提高模拟到真实转移的鲁棒性。ADQ通过自适应力差量化有效实现这一目标，为布料操作等对交互动力学敏感的任务提供了更稳健的模拟到真实转移方案。

Abstract: Robotic cloth untangling requires progressively disentangling fabric by adapting pulling actions to changing contact and tension conditions. Because large-scale real-world training is impractical due to cloth damage and hardware wear, sim-to-real policy transfer is a promising solution. However, cloth manipulation is highly sensitive to interaction dynamics, and policies that depend on precise force magnitudes often fail after transfer because similar force responses cannot be reproduced due to the reality gap. We observe that untangling is largely characterized by qualitative tension transitions rather than exact force values. This indicates that directly minimizing the sim-to-real gap in raw force measurements does not necessarily align with the task structure. We therefore hypothesize that emphasizing coarse force-change patterns while suppressing fine environment-dependent variations can improve robustness of sim-to-real transfer. Based on this insight, we propose Adaptive Force-Difference Quantization (ADQ), which reduces observation resolution by representing force inputs as discretized temporal differences and learning state-dependent quantization thresholds adaptively. This representation mitigates overfitting to environment-specific force characteristics and facilitates direct sim-to-real transfer. Experiments in both simulation and real-world cloth untangling demonstrate that ADQ achieves higher success rates and exhibits greater robustness in sim-to-real transfer than policies using raw force inputs. Supplementary video is available at https://youtu.be/ZeoBs-t0AWc

</details>


### [101] [ST-VLA: Enabling 4D-Aware Spatiotemporal Understanding for General Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13788)
*You Wu,Zixuan Chen,Cunxu Ou,Wenxuan Wang,Wenbo Huang,Lin Cao,Yangtao Chen,Weichao Qiu,Xingyue Quan,Jieqi Shi,Jing Huo,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ST-VLA是一个分层视觉-语言-动作框架，使用统一的3D-4D表示来桥接感知和动作，通过将2D指导转换为3D轨迹并生成平滑空间掩码来提升开放世界机器人操作的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分层VLA框架通常使用2D表示连接高层推理和低层控制，但缺乏深度感知和时间一致性，限制了在复杂3D场景中的鲁棒性。需要一种能够处理语义、几何和长时程动作动态的统一表示方法。

Method: 提出ST-VLA框架，使用统一的3D-4D表示。将2D指导转换为3D轨迹，生成平滑空间掩码捕获4D时空上下文。引入ST-Human数据集（14个任务，30万片段），通过半自动流程标注2D、3D和4D监督。训练ST-VLM时空视觉语言模型，生成空间基础和时序一致的3D表示来指导策略执行。

Result: 在RLBench和真实世界操作任务上的实验显示，ST-VLA显著优于现有基线方法，零样本成功率分别提升44.6%和30.3%。平滑空间掩码专注于任务相关几何并稳定潜在表示，支持在线重新规划和长时程推理。

Conclusion: 将时空推理卸载到具有统一3D-4D表示的VLM中，显著提高了开放世界机器人操作的鲁棒性和泛化能力。统一的时空表示为语义推理和连续控制提供了稳定接口。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation in open-world environments requires reasoning across semantics, geometry, and long-horizon action dynamics. Existing hierarchical Vision-Language-Action (VLA) frameworks typically use 2D representations to connect high-level reasoning with low-level control, but lack depth awareness and temporal consistency, limiting robustness in complex 3D scenes. We propose ST-VLA, a hierarchical VLA framework using a unified 3D-4D representation to bridge perception and action. ST-VLA converts 2D guidance into 3D trajectories and generates smooth spatial masks that capture 4D spatio-temporal context, providing a stable interface between semantic reasoning and continuous control. To enable effective learning of such representations, we introduce ST-Human, a large-scale human manipulation dataset with 14 tasks and 300k episodes, annotated with 2D, 3D, and 4D supervision via a semi-automated pipeline. Using ST-Human, we train ST-VLM, a spatio-temporal vision-language model that generates spatially grounded and temporally coherent 3D representations to guide policy execution. The smooth spatial masks focus on task-relevant geometry and stabilize latent representations, enabling online replanning and long-horizon reasoning. Experiments on RLBench and real-world manipulation tasks show that \method significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, improving zero-shot success rates by 44.6% and 30.3%. These results demonstrate that offloading spatio-temporal reasoning to VLMs with unified 3D-4D representations substantially improves robustness and generalization for open-world robotic manipulation. Project website: https://oucx117.github.io/ST-VLA/.

</details>


### [102] [Building Explicit World Model for Zero-Shot Open-World Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13825)
*Xiaotong Li,Gang Chen,Javier Alonso-Mora*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于显式世界模型的开放世界物体操纵框架，通过构建物理基础的数字孪生环境实现零样本泛化，无需任务特定的动作演示数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型严重依赖大规模机器人动作演示数据，这些数据收集成本高且可能阻碍分布外泛化能力。需要一种不依赖大量演示数据就能实现开放世界操纵的方法。

Method: 构建基于显式世界模型的框架，包含开放集感知、数字孪生重建、交互策略采样与评估。通过构建环境的物理基础数字孪生，在物理模拟器中高效探索和评估操纵策略，然后将选定策略可靠部署到真实世界。

Result: 该框架能够在没有任何任务特定动作演示的情况下执行多个开放集操纵任务，在任务和物体层面都表现出强大的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 基于显式世界模型和数字孪生的方法为开放世界机器人操纵提供了一种有前景的替代方案，减少了对大规模演示数据的依赖，同时保持了强大的泛化能力。

Abstract: Open-world object manipulation remains a fundamental challenge in robotics. While Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated promising results, they rely heavily on large-scale robot action demonstrations, which are costly to collect and can hinder out-of-distribution generalization. In this paper, we propose an explicit-world-model-based framework for open-world manipulation that achieves zero-shot generalization by constructing a physically grounded digital twin of the environment. The framework integrates open-set perception, digital-twin reconstruction, sampling and evaluation of interaction strategies. By constructing a digital twin of the environment, our approach efficiently explores and evaluates manipulation strategies in physic-enabled simulator and reliably deploys the chosen strategy to the real world. Experimentally, the proposed framework is able to perform multiple open-set manipulation tasks without any task-specific action demonstrations, proving strong zero-shot generalization on both the task and object levels. Project Page: https://bojack-bj.github.io/projects/thesis/

</details>


### [103] [GraspADMM: Improving Dexterous Grasp Synthesis via ADMM Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13832)
*Liangwang Ruan,Jiayi Chen,He Wang,Baoquan Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GraspADMM框架，通过ADMM分解目标接触点与手部实际接触位置，在保持采样多样性的同时提升灵巧抓取的动力学稳定性和运动学可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在局限性：Dexonomy框架依赖固定接触点限制了手部可达性，无法优化动力学稳定性；纯梯度优化器能最大化动力学稳定性但使用简化接触近似导致物理穿透。需要同时解决多样性、运动学可行性和动力学稳定性问题。

Method: 提出GraspADMM框架，使用交替方向乘子法(ADMM)将抓取细化阶段分解：1) 更新目标物体接触点以直接最大化动态抓取指标；2) 调整手部姿态以物理上到达这些目标点，同时严格尊重碰撞边界。这种分解允许交替优化动力学稳定性和运动学可行性。

Result: GraspADMM显著优于现有基线：在类型无关合成中实现接近15%的绝对抓取成功率提升，在类型感知合成中实现约100%的相对改进。即使在极端低摩擦条件下也能保持稳健、物理合理的抓取生成。

Conclusion: GraspADMM通过ADMM分解成功桥接了采样多样性与优化稳定性之间的差距，在保持多样性的同时显著提升了灵巧抓取的运动学可行性和动力学稳定性，为机器人灵巧操作提供了更有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Synthesizing high-quality dexterous grasps is a fundamental challenge in robot manipulation, requiring adherence to diversity, kinematic feasibility (valid hand-object contact without penetration), and dynamic stability (secure multi-contact forces). The recent framework Dexonomy successfully ensures broad grasp diversity through dense sampling and improves kinematic feasibility via a simulator-based refinement method that excels at resolving exact collisions. However, its reliance on fixed contact points restricts the hand's reachability and prevents the optimization of grasp metrics for dynamic stability. Conversely, purely gradient-based optimizers can maximize dynamic stability but rely on simplified contact approximations that inevitably cause physical penetrations. To bridge this gap, we propose GraspADMM, a novel grasp synthesis framework that preserves sampling-based diversity while improving kinematic feasibility and dynamic stability. By formulating the refinement stage using the Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM), we decouple the target contact points on the object from the actual contact locations on the hand. This decomposition allows the pipeline to alternate between updating the target object points to directly maximize dynamic grasp metrics, and adjusting the hand pose to physically reach these targets while strictly respecting collision boundaries. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GraspADMM significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving a nearly 15\% absolute improvement in grasp success rate for type-unaware synthesis and roughly a 100\% relative improvement in type-aware synthesis. Furthermore, our approach maintains robust, physically plausible grasp generation even under extreme low-friction conditions.

</details>


### [104] [ImagiNav: Scalable Embodied Navigation via Generative Visual Prediction and Inverse Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13833)
*Jie Chen,Yuxin Cai,Yizhuo Wang,Ruofei Bai,Yuhong Cao,Jun Li,Yau Wei Yun,Guillaume Sartoretti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ImagiNav提出了一种新颖的模块化视觉语言导航范式，通过解耦视觉规划和机器人执行，利用野外导航视频实现零样本机器人导航，无需机器人演示数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉语言导航依赖昂贵的机器人特定数据训练端到端策略，而基于模拟的预训练模型受限于场景多样性和视觉保真度。需要一种能够利用丰富野外导航视频并实现零样本迁移到机器人导航的解决方案。

Method: 提出模块化分层框架：1) 视觉语言模型将指令分解为文本子目标；2) 微调生成视频模型想象朝向子目标的未来视频轨迹；3) 逆动力学模型从想象视频中提取轨迹，由底层控制器跟踪执行。同时开发了通过逆动力学和预训练视觉语言模型自动标注野外导航视频的可扩展数据管道。

Result: ImagiNav展示了强大的零样本迁移能力，能够在无需机器人演示的情况下实现机器人导航，为直接从无标注的开放世界数据学习导航的通用机器人铺平道路。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种创新的模块化视觉语言导航范式，通过解耦视觉规划和机器人执行，实现了利用野外导航视频进行零样本机器人导航，解决了现有方法对昂贵机器人数据和模拟场景局限性的依赖问题。

Abstract: Enabling robots to navigate open-world environments via natural language is critical for general-purpose autonomy. Yet, Vision-Language Navigation has relied on end-to-end policies trained on expensive, embodiment-specific robot data. While recent foundation models trained on vast simulation data show promise, the challenge of scaling and generalizing due to the limited scene diversity and visual fidelity in simulation persists. To address this gap, we propose ImagiNav, a novel modular paradigm that decouples visual planning from robot actuation, enabling the direct utilization of diverse in-the-wild navigation videos. Our framework operates as a hierarchy: a Vision-Language Model first decomposes instructions into textual subgoals; a finetuned generative video model then imagines the future video trajectory towards that subgoal; finally, an inverse dynamics model extracts the trajectory from the imagined video, which can then be tracked via a low-level controller. We additionally develop a scalable data pipeline of in-the-wild navigation videos auto-labeled via inverse dynamics and a pretrained Vision-Language Model. ImagiNav demonstrates strong zero-shot transfer to robot navigation without requiring robot demonstrations, paving the way for generalist robots that learn navigation directly from unlabeled, open-world data.

</details>


### [105] [Fine-tuning is Not Enough: A Parallel Framework for Collaborative Imitation and Reinforcement Learning in End-to-end Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13842)
*Zhexi Lian,Haoran Wang,Xuerun Yan,Weimeng Lin,Xianhong Zhang,Yongyu Chen,Jia Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PaIR-Drive提出了一种并行协作的模仿学习与强化学习框架，用于端到端自动驾驶，通过并行分支设计避免策略漂移，并引入树形轨迹神经采样器增强探索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前端到端自动驾驶主要基于模仿学习，但受限于人类演示质量。现有方法通过顺序微调结合强化学习存在策略漂移和性能瓶颈问题，需要更有效的协作优化框架。

Method: 提出PaIR-Drive并行框架：1) 将IL和RL分离为两个并行分支，避免训练目标冲突；2) 推理时RL利用IL策略进一步优化最终规划；3) 引入树形轨迹神经采样器进行分组相对策略优化(GRPO)增强探索能力。

Result: 在NAVSIMv1和v2基准测试中，基于Transfuser和DiffusionDrive IL基线，PaIR-Drive达到91.2 PDMS和87.9 EPDMS的竞争性性能，优于现有RL微调方法，甚至能纠正人类专家的次优行为。

Conclusion: PaIR-Drive通过并行协作框架有效解决了IL与RL结合时的策略漂移问题，实现了超越IL先验知识的性能，为端到端自动驾驶提供了更优的训练范式。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving is typically built upon imitation learning (IL), yet its performance is constrained by the quality of human demonstrations. To overcome this limitation, recent methods incorporate reinforcement learning (RL) through sequential fine-tuning. However, such a paradigm remains suboptimal: sequential RL fine-tuning can introduce policy drift and often leads to a performance ceiling due to its dependence on the pretrained IL policy. To address these issues, we propose PaIR-Drive, a general Parallel framework for collaborative Imitation and Reinforcement learning in end-to-end autonomous driving. During training, PaIR-Drive separates IL and RL into two parallel branches with conflict-free training objectives, enabling fully collaborative optimization. This design eliminates the need to retrain RL when applying a new IL policy. During inference, RL leverages the IL policy to further optimize the final plan, allowing performance beyond prior knowledge of IL. Furthermore, we introduce a tree-structured trajectory neural sampler to group relative policy optimization (GRPO) in the RL branch, which enhances exploration capability. Extensive analysis on NAVSIMv1 and v2 benchmark demonstrates that PaIR-Drive achieves Competitive performance of 91.2 PDMS and 87.9 EPDMS, building upon Transfuser and DiffusionDrive IL baselines. PaIR-Drive consistently outperforms existing RL fine-tuning methods, and could even correct human experts' suboptimal behaviors. Qualitative results further confirm that PaIR-Drive can effectively explore and generate high-quality trajectories.

</details>


### [106] [LDHP: Library-Driven Hierarchical Planning for Non-prehensile Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13844)
*Tierui He,Chao Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LDHP（库驱动分层规划器），通过将可执行性作为首要设计目标，解决非抓取操作中物理不可行和泛化性差的问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在局限性：基于规划和搜索的方法依赖手动设计或忽略关键夹爪属性导致物理不可行；基于训练的方法数据密集且难以泛化到新任务。需要一种能保证可执行性并具有良好泛化能力的方法

Method: LDHP采用分层规划：顶层接触状态规划器使用MoveObject原语规划物体姿态路径；底层抓取规划器使用AdjustGrasp原语合成可行抓取序列。通过碰撞检测和准静态力学验证可行性，接触敏感段通过有界二分细化恢复。夹爪感知分解将物体运动与抓取可行性解耦

Result: 在零移动性提升和槽插入任务上的真实机器人实验表明，该方法具有一致的执行能力，并对形状和环境变化具有鲁棒性。实现了跨操作任务和几何变化的任务无关流程，无需重新设计

Conclusion: LDHP通过将可执行性作为核心设计原则，解决了非抓取操作中的关键挑战。其分层架构、夹爪感知分解和可行性验证机制提供了可泛化的解决方案，并为可选学习先验提供了清晰的接口

Abstract: Non-prehensile manipulation is essential for handling thin, large, or otherwise ungraspable objects in unstructured settings. Prior planning and search-based methods often rely on ad-hoc manual designs or generate physically unrealizable motions by ignoring critical gripper properties, while training-based approaches are data-intensive and struggle to generalize to novel, out-of-distribution tasks. We propose a library-driven hierarchical planner (LDHP) that makes executability a first-class design goal: a top-tier contact-state planner proposes object-pose paths using MoveObject primitives, and a bottom-tier grasp planner synthesizes feasible grasp sequences with AdjustGrasp primitives; feasibility is certified by collision checks and quasi-static mechanics, and contact-sensitive segments are recovered via a bounded dichotomy refinement. This gripper-aware decomposition decouples object motion from grasp realizability, yields a task-agnostic pipeline that transfers across manipulation tasks and geometric variations without re-design, and exposes clean hooks for optional learned priors. Real-robot studies on zero-mobility lifting and slot insertion demonstrate consistent execution and robustness to shape and environment changes.

</details>


### [107] [TransDex: Pre-training Visuo-Tactile Policy with Point Cloud Reconstruction for Dexterous Manipulation of Transparent Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13869)
*Fengguan Li,Yifan Ma,Chen Qian,Wentao Rao,Weiwei Shang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TransDex：基于点云重建预训练的3D视觉-触觉融合运动策略，用于解决灵巧操作中透明物体的自遮挡、深度噪声和信息丢失问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作支持复杂任务，但在操作透明物体时面临自遮挡、严重深度噪声和深度信息丢失等问题，需要更鲁棒的感知和控制系统

Method: 1. 基于Transformer的自监督点云重建预训练方法，从灵巧手交互点云中准确恢复物体3D结构；2. TransDex采用细粒度分层感知编码和多轮注意力机制，自适应融合机械臂和灵巧手特征以实现差异化运动预测

Result: 在真实机器人系统上进行的透明物体操作实验表明，TransDex优于现有基线方法，进一步分析验证了其泛化能力和各组件有效性

Conclusion: TransDex通过点云重建预训练和分层注意力融合机制，有效解决了透明物体灵巧操作中的感知挑战，为复杂操作任务提供了鲁棒的解决方案

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation enables complex tasks but suffers from self-occlusion, severe depth noise, and depth information loss when manipulating transparent objects. To solve this problem, this paper proposes TransDex, a 3D visuo-tactile fusion motor policy based on point cloud reconstruction pre-training. Specifically, we first propose a self-supervised point cloud reconstruction pre-training approach based on Transformer. This method accurately recovers the 3D structure of objects from interactive point clouds of dexterous hands, even when random noise and large-scale masking are added. Building on this, TransDex is constructed in which perceptual encoding adopts a fine-grained hierarchical scheme and multi-round attention mechanisms adaptively fuse features of the robotic arm and dexterous hand to enable differentiated motion prediction. Results from transparent object manipulation experiments conducted on a real robotic system demonstrate that TransDex outperforms existing baseline methods. Further analysis validates the generalization capabilities of TransDex and the effectiveness of its individual components.

</details>


### [108] [Path-conditioned Reinforcement Learning-based Local Planning for Long-Range Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13888)
*Mateo Haro,Julia Richter,Fan Yang,Cesar Cadena,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于强化学习的局部导航策略，利用路径信息作为上下文指导，在高质量路径下提升导航效率，在路径信息退化时仍保持基准性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分层导航系统对全局路径质量敏感，不准确的遥感数据会导致局部不可行的路径点，降低局部执行效率；同时局部规划器有限的全局上下文限制了长距离导航效率

Method: 提出基于强化学习的局部导航策略，以参考路径观测为条件进行训练，奖励函数主要基于目标到达目标，没有显式的路径跟随奖励，通过隐式条件学习机会主义地利用路径信息

Result: 实验结果表明，当高质量路径可用时，该方法显著提高导航效率；当路径观测严重退化甚至不存在时，仍保持基准水平的性能

Conclusion: 该方法特别适合长距离导航场景，其中高层规划是近似的，局部执行必须保持对不确定性的适应性，通过隐式条件学习实现路径信息的鲁棒利用

Abstract: Long-range navigation is commonly addressed through hierarchical pipelines in which a global planner generates a path, decomposed into waypoints, and followed sequentially by a local planner. These systems are sensitive to global path quality, as inaccurate remote sensing data can result in locally infeasible waypoints, which degrade local execution. At the same time, the limited global context available to the local planner hinders long-range efficiency. To address this issue, we propose a reinforcement learning-based local navigation policy that leverages path information as contextual guidance. The policy is conditioned on reference path observations and trained with a reward function mainly based on goal-reaching objectives, without any explicit path-following reward. Through this implicit conditioning, the policy learns to opportunistically exploit path information while remaining robust to misleading or degraded guidance. Experimental results show that the proposed approach significantly improves navigation efficiency when high-quality paths are available and maintains baseline-level performance when path observations are severely degraded or even non-existent. These properties make the method particularly well-suited for long-range navigation scenarios in which high-level plans are approximate and local execution must remain adaptive to uncertainty.

</details>


### [109] [LineMaster Pro: A Low-Cost Intelligent Line Following Robot with PID Control and Ultrasonic Obstacle Avoidance for Educational Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13907)
*Jeni Shahi,Abhishek Shah,A. S. M. Ahsanul Sarkar Akib*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LineMaster Pro是一款基于Arduino Nano的低成本智能循线机器人，集成了红外循线传感器、超声波避障传感器、PID控制和有限状态机，成本仅28.5美元，相比商业方案降低94%，为资源受限环境提供实用的机器人教育平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业循线机器人价格昂贵（150-300美元）且缺乏避障功能，无法满足实际应用需求。需要开发低成本、功能完整的解决方案，以促进资源受限环境下的机器人教育普及。

Method: 基于Arduino Nano平台，集成双TCRT5000红外传感器用于精确循线，HC-SR04超声波传感器用于实时避障。采用Ziegler-Nichols优化的数字PID控制器和分层有限状态机实现鲁棒避障。开发了四阶段传感器校准方法确保不同光照和表面条件下的可靠运行。

Result: 200次控制试验和72小时连续运行验证：平均循线精度1.18cm（速度0.4m/s），障碍物检测可靠性96.7%（10-40cm范围，误报率0.7%），路径偏差恢复成功率94%。PID控制相比传统开关控制提升43%（p<0.001）。总硬件成本28.5美元，相比商业方案成本降低94%。

Conclusion: LineMaster Pro成功实现了低成本、高性能的智能循线机器人设计，为资源受限环境下的机器人教育建立了实用基准，证明了通过系统化设计和优化可以在极低成本下实现接近商业产品的性能。

Abstract: Line following robots are fundamental platforms in robotics education, yet commercially available solutions remain prohibitively expensive ($150-300$) while lacking integrated obstacle detection capabilities essential for real-world applications. This paper presents LineMaster Pro, an intelligent low-cost line following robot implemented on an Arduino Nano platform that integrates dual TCRT5000 infrared sensors for precision line tracking, an HC-SR04 ultrasonic sensor for real-time obstacle detection, a digitally tuned PID controller with Ziegler-Nichols optimization, and a hierarchical finite state machine for robust obstacle avoidance. A systematic four-phase sensor calibration methodology ensures reliable operation across varying lighting and surface conditions. Experimental validation through 200 controlled trials and 72-hour continuous operation demonstrates mean tracking accuracy of 1.18 cm at 0.4 m/s (95\% CI [1.06, 1.30]), obstacle detection reliability of 96.7\% within 10-40 cm range with 0.7\% false positive rate, and 94\% successful recovery from path deviations. The PID implementation achieves 43\% improvement over conventional on-off control ($p<0.001$). At a total hardware cost of \$28.50 based on verified Bangladesh market prices, LineMaster Pro achieves a 94\% cost reduction compared to commercial alternatives, establishing a practical benchmark for accessible robotics education in resource-constrained environments.

</details>


### [110] [Data-Driven Autoregressive Power Prediction for GTernal Robots in the Robotarium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13908)
*Yassin Abdelmeguid,Ammar Hasan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种轻量级自回归预测器，用于GTernal移动机器人平台的能耗预测，通过分析48,000个样本发现能耗具有强时间自相关性，7,041参数的MLP模型在未见运动模式上达到R²=0.90，并在7个机器人上验证了零样本迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多机器人系统的能量感知算法依赖运动学近似，无法捕捉真实硬件的复杂动态特性，需要更准确的能耗模型来支持能量感知的多机器人算法开发。

Method: 提出基于多层感知机(MLP)的轻量级自回归预测器，利用能耗的时间自相关性，通过分析48,000个样本数据训练7,041参数模型，在GTernal移动机器人平台上部署验证。

Result: 模型在保留运动模式上达到R²=0.90，接近测量噪声限制的理论预测上限；在7个机器人的碰撞避免场景中平均R²=0.87，展示了零样本迁移能力；单次推理仅需224μs，比平台30Hz控制频率快150倍。

Conclusion: 能耗预测主要依赖时间自相关性而非运动学效应，提出的轻量级自回归预测器能准确预测机器人能耗并实现零样本迁移，为能量感知的多机器人算法开发提供了实用工具和数据集。

Abstract: Energy-aware algorithms for multi-robot systems require accurate power consumption models, yet existing approaches rely on kinematic approximations that fail to capture the complex dynamics of real hardware. We present a lightweight autoregressive predictor for the GTernal mobile robot platform deployed in the Georgia Tech Robotarium. Through analysis of 48,000 samples collected across six motion trials, we discover that power consumption exhibits strong temporal autocorrelation ($ρ_1 = 0.95$) that dominates kinematic effects. A 7,041-parameter multi-layer perceptron (MLP) achieves $R^2 = 0.90$ on held-out motion patterns by conditioning on recent power history, reaching the theoretical prediction ceiling imposed by measurement noise. Physical validation across seven robots in a collision avoidance scenario yields mean $R^2 = 0.87$, demonstrating zero-shot transfer to unseen robots and behaviors. The predictor runs in 224 $μ$s per inference, enabling real-time deployment at 150$\times$ the platform's 30 Hz control rate. We release the trained model and dataset to support energy-aware multi-robot algorithm development.

</details>


### [111] [SmoothVLA: Aligning Vision-Language-Action Models with Physical Constraints via Intrinsic Smoothness Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13925)
*Jiashun Li,Xiaoyu Shi,Hong Xie,Mingsheng Shang,Yun Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SmoothVLA：通过结合任务奖励和轨迹平滑度奖励的强化学习微调框架，解决VLA模型在稳定性和探索性之间的权衡问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型微调方法面临稳定性与探索性的两难困境：监督微调受限于演示质量且缺乏泛化能力，而强化学习虽能提升探索性但常产生违反物理约束的抖动轨迹

Method: 提出SmoothVLA框架，采用物理信息混合奖励函数，将稀疏任务奖励与基于轨迹jerk的连续密集奖励相结合；使用GRPO算法将轨迹平滑度作为显式优化先验

Result: 在LIBERO基准测试中，SmoothVLA相比标准RL平滑度提升13.8%，在多样化任务中的泛化能力显著超越监督微调

Conclusion: SmoothVLA通过内在奖励优化提供了一种可扩展的方法，使VLA模型更好地与物理世界约束对齐

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a powerful paradigm for robotic manipulation. However, existing post-training methods face a dilemma between stability and exploration: Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) is constrained by demonstration quality and lacks generalization, whereas Reinforcement Learning (RL) improves exploration but often induces erratic, jittery trajectories that violate physical constraints. To bridge this gap, we propose SmoothVLA, a novel reinforcement learning fine-tuning framework that synergistically optimizes task performance and motion smoothness. The technical core is a physics-informed hybrid reward function that integrates binary sparse task rewards with a continuous dense term derived from trajectory jerk. Crucially, this reward is intrinsic, that computing directly from policy rollouts, without requiring extrinsic environment feedback or laborious reward engineering. Leveraging the Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), SmoothVLA establishes trajectory smoothness as an explicit optimization prior, guiding the model toward physically feasible and stable control. Extensive experiments on the LIBERO benchmark demonstrate that SmoothVLA outperforms standard RL by 13.8\% in smoothness and significantly surpasses SFT in generalization across diverse tasks. Our work offers a scalable approach to aligning VLA models with physical-world constraints through intrinsic reward optimization.

</details>


### [112] [ToMPC: Task-oriented Model Predictive Control via ADMM for Safe Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13944)
*Xinyu Jia,Wenxin Wang,Jun Yang,Yongping Pan,Haoyong Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向任务的模型预测控制框架，用于开放工作空间中安全高效的机器人操作，统一了无碰撞运动和机器人-环境交互，并引入面向任务的避障方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在开放工作空间中实现安全高效的机器人操作需要同时处理无碰撞运动和机器人-环境交互，传统方法难以统一处理这些多样化场景，且需要提高在障碍环境中的操作效率。

Method: 提出任务导向模型预测控制框架，引入面向任务的避障方法，利用运动学冗余性；采用ADMM算法将复杂优化问题分解为两个子问题，分别用DDP和QP求解。

Result: 在Franka Panda机械臂的仿真和硬件实验中验证了有效性，能够实时规划运动和/或力轨迹，在避免障碍的同时最大化操作范围，并严格遵守安全相关的硬约束。

Conclusion: 该框架成功统一了无碰撞运动和机器人-环境交互，通过任务导向的避障方法提高了操作效率，ADMM分解策略实现了复杂优化问题的实时求解，为开放工作空间中的机器人操作提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes a task-oriented model predictive control (ToMPC) framework for safe and efficient robotic manipulation in open workspaces. The framework unifies collision-free motion and robot-environment interaction to address diverse scenarios. Additionally, it introduces task-oriented obstacle avoidance that leverages kinematic redundancy to enhance manipulation efficiency in obstructed environments. This complex optimization problem is solved by the alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM), which decomposes the problem into two subproblems tackled by differential dynamic programming (DDP) and quadratic programming (QP), respectively. The effectiveness of this approach is validated in simulation and hardware experiments on a Franka Panda robotic manipulator. Results demonstrate that the framework can plan motion and/or force trajectories in real time, maximize the manipulation range while avoiding obstacles, and strictly adhere to safety-related hard constraints.

</details>


### [113] [Vision-guided Autonomous Dual-arm Extraction Robot for Bell Pepper Harvesting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13987)
*Kshitij Madhav Bhat,Tom Gao,Abhishek Mathur,Rohit Satishkumar,Francisco Yandun,Dominik Bauer,Nancy Pollard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VADER是一个用于户外甜椒自主采摘的双臂移动机器人系统，通过视觉感知和双臂协调规划实现采摘，在真实环境中达到60%以上的采摘成功率，并贡献了包含3200多张图像的分层数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业机器人是解决劳动力短缺和采摘成本上升的关键方案。甜椒采摘是劳动密集型任务，占生产成本的50%。现有自动化方案在受控温室环境中表现良好，但在非结构化户外农场中仍面临环境变化和遮挡等挑战。

Method: 提出VADER系统：1）集成鲁棒感知流水线；2）双臂规划框架协调抓取臂和切割臂进行采摘；3）基于GELLO遥操作框架的故障安全机制；4）贡献包含3200多张图像的分层数据集，采用从粗到细的训练策略，从果实检测到高精度姿态估计。

Result: 在各种真实条件下验证系统，采摘成功率超过60%，每个果实采摘周期时间低于100秒。系统具备基于GELLO的遥操作故障安全机制确保鲁棒性。

Conclusion: VADER系统展示了在户外环境中自主采摘甜椒的可行性，为农业机器人领域提供了实用的解决方案和宝贵的数据资源，代码和数据集将在接受后公开。

Abstract: Agricultural robotics has emerged as a critical solution to the labor shortages and rising costs associated with manual crop harvesting. Bell pepper harvesting, in particular, is a labor-intensive task, accounting for up to 50% of total production costs. While automated solutions have shown promise in controlled greenhouse environments, harvesting in unstructured outdoor farms remains an open challenge due to environmental variability and occlusion. This paper presents VADER (Vision-guided Autonomous Dual-arm Extraction Robot), a dual-arm mobile manipulation system designed specifically for the autonomous harvesting of bell peppers in outdoor environments. The system integrates a robust perception pipeline coupled with a dual-arm planning framework that coordinates a gripping arm and a cutting arm for extraction. We validate the system through trials in various realistic conditions, demonstrating a harvest success rate exceeding 60% with a cycle time of under 100 seconds per fruit, while also featuring a teleoperation fail-safe based on the GELLO teleoperation framework to ensure robustness. To support robust perception, we contribute a hierarchically structured dataset of over 3,200 images spanning indoor and outdoor domains, pairing wide-field scene images with close-up pepper images to enable a coarse-to-fine training strategy from fruit detection to high-precision pose estimation. The code and dataset will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [114] [URDF-Anything+: Autoregressive Articulated 3D Models Generation for Physical Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14010)
*Zhuangzhe Wu,Yue Xin,Chengkai Hou,Minghao Chen,Yaoxu Lyu,Jieyu Zhang,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出端到端自回归框架，直接从视觉观测生成可执行的铰接物体URDF模型，实现真实到仿真的数字孪生迁移


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铰接物体在机器人、物理仿真和虚拟环境中至关重要，但从视觉输入重建铰接物体仍然具有挑战性，需要同时推断部件几何和运动学结构

Method: 端到端自回归框架，基于图像和物体级3D线索，顺序生成部件几何和对应的关节参数，自动推断完整几何和运动学结构，生成完整URDF模型

Result: 在大规模铰接物体基准测试和真实世界机器人任务中，在几何重建质量、关节参数精度和物理可执行性方面优于先前方法

Conclusion: 该方法实现了Real-Follow-Sim新范式，通过视觉观测构建的高保真数字孪生，使纯仿真训练的策略无需在线适应即可迁移到真实机器人

Abstract: Articulated objects are fundamental for robotics, simulation of physics, and interactive virtual environments. However, reconstructing them from visual input remains challenging, as it requires jointly inferring both part geometry and kinematic structure. We present, an end-to-end autoregressive framework that directly generates executable articulated object models from visual observations. Given image and object-level 3D cues, our method sequentially produces part geometries and their associated joint parameters, resulting in complete URDF models without reliance on multi-stage pipelines. The generation proceeds until the model determines that all parts have been produced, automatically inferring complete geometry and kinematics. Building on this capability, we enable a new Real-Follow-Sim paradigm, where high-fidelity digital twins constructed from visual observations allow policies trained and tested purely in simulation to transfer to real robots without online adaptation. Experiments on large-scale articulated object benchmarks and real-world robotic tasks demonstrate that outperforms prior methods in geometric reconstruction quality, joint parameter accuracy, and physical executability.

</details>


### [115] [Amortizing Trajectory Diffusion with Keyed Drift Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14056)
*Gokul Puthumanaillam,Melkior Ornik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: KDP是一种单步推理的轨迹规划方法，通过键空间距离匹配和漂移场目标实现类似扩散模型的多模态规划效果，大幅降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在离线强化学习中能生成丰富的多模态动作序列，但其迭代去噪过程导致推理时间成本高，在严格计算预算下闭环规划缓慢。需要实现类似扩散模型的轨迹规划行为，同时保持单步推理、多样候选计划生成和当前状态条件化的能力。

Method: 提出键控漂移策略(KDP)，核心观察是条件轨迹生成在朴素分布匹配目标下会失败，因为相似性度量被无约束的未来维度主导。关键见解是需要条件感知的邻域概念：使用反映条件的紧凑键空间中的距离计算轨迹更新，同时在完整轨迹空间中应用更新。采用漂移场目标训练，吸引生成轨迹向条件匹配的数据集窗口，排斥附近生成样本，使用停止梯度的漂移目标将迭代优化摊销到训练中。

Result: 在标准RL基准测试和实时硬件部署中，KDP通过单步推理实现强大性能，规划延迟显著低于扩散采样方法。

Conclusion: KDP成功实现了扩散式轨迹规划的单步推理替代方案，通过条件感知的键空间距离匹配和漂移场训练，在保持多样性和条件化能力的同时大幅降低推理延迟，适用于实时控制场景。

Abstract: Diffusion-based trajectory planners can synthesize rich, multimodal action sequences for offline reinforcement learning, but their iterative denoising incurs substantial inference-time cost, making closed-loop planning slow under tight compute budgets. We study the problem of achieving diffusion-like trajectory planning behavior with one-step inference, while retaining the ability to sample diverse candidate plans and condition on the current state in a receding-horizon control loop. Our key observation is that conditional trajectory generation fails under naïve distribution-matching objectives when the similarity measure used to align generated trajectories with the dataset is dominated by unconstrained future dimensions. In practice, this causes attraction toward average trajectories, collapses action diversity, and yields near-static behavior. Our key insight is that conditional generative planning requires a conditioning-aware notion of neighborhood: trajectory updates should be computed using distances in a compact key space that reflects the condition, while still applying updates in the full trajectory space. Building on this, we introduce Keyed Drifting Policies (KDP), a one-step trajectory generator trained with a drift-field objective that attracts generated trajectories toward condition-matched dataset windows and repels them from nearby generated samples, using a stop-gradient drifted target to amortize iterative refinement into training. At inference, the resulting policy produces a full trajectory window in a single forward pass. Across standard RL benchmarks and real-time hardware deployments, KDP achieves strong performance with one-step inference and substantially lower planning latency than diffusion sampling. Project website, code and videos: https://keyed-drifting.github.io/

</details>


### [116] [Stiffness Copilot: An Impedance Policy for Contact-Rich Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14068)
*Yeping Wang,Zhengtong Xu,Pornthep Preechayasomboon,Ben Abbatematteo,Amirhossein H. Memar,Nick Colonnese,Sonny Chan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Stiffness Copilot：基于视觉的共享控制遥操作系统，操作员控制机器人位姿，策略在线调整机器人阻抗，实现安全与效率的平衡


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在接触丰富的遥操作任务中，机器人阻抗选择至关重要但困难。机器人需要柔顺以避免损坏环境，又需要刚硬以保持响应性并在需要时施加力。传统方法难以在安全与效率间取得平衡。

Method: 首先在仿真中使用特权接触信息推断方向依赖的刚度矩阵，然后用这些矩阵监督轻量级视觉策略，该策略从腕部摄像头图像预测机器人刚度，并在运行时零样本迁移到真实图像。

Result: 在人体实验中，Stiffness Copilot实现了与恒定低刚度相当的安全性，同时匹配了恒定高刚度的效率。

Conclusion: Stiffness Copilot通过视觉策略在线调整阻抗，有效解决了遥操作中安全与效率的权衡问题，实现了零样本迁移到真实环境。

Abstract: In teleoperation of contact-rich manipulation tasks, selecting robot impedance is critical but difficult. The robot must be compliant to avoid damaging the environment, but stiff to remain responsive and to apply force when needed. In this paper, we present Stiffness Copilot, a vision-based policy for shared-control teleoperation in which the operator commands robot pose and the policy adjusts robot impedance online. To train Stiffness Copilot, we first infer direction-dependent stiffness matrices in simulation using privileged contact information. We then use these matrices to supervise a lightweight vision policy that predicts robot stiffness from wrist-camera images and transfers zero-shot to real images at runtime. In a human-subject study, Stiffness Copilot achieved safety comparable to using a constant low stiffness while matching the efficiency of using a constant high stiffness.

</details>


### [117] [GelSphere: An Omnidirectional Rolling Vision-Based Tactile Sensor for Online 3D Reconstruction and Normal Force Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14104)
*Seoyeon Lee,Mohammad Amin Mirzaee,Wenzhen Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GelSphere是一种球形视觉触觉传感器，可实现全向滚动连续表面扫描，克服了传统传感器局部感知、易损坏和单向滚动的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉触觉传感器存在三个主要问题：1）只能局部感知；2）在表面滑动时容易损坏；3）圆柱形传感器只能沿固定方向滚动。需要一种能够全向滚动、连续扫描表面且不易损坏的触觉传感器。

Method: 设计了球形视觉触觉传感器，内部采用钢球形成轴承层，位于凝胶和刚性外壳之间，实现全向滚动运动。传感器通过Wi-Fi流式传输触觉图像，具备在线大面积重建能力。

Result: 传感器在多向滚动下保持几何保真度和高重建精度，实现了不间断表面扫描。定量结果显示重建精度和图像融合性能良好。

Conclusion: GelSphere传感器成功实现了全向滚动连续表面扫描，克服了传统触觉传感器的局限性，为实时大面积触觉感知提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: We present GelSphere, a spherical vision-based tactile sensor designed for real-time continuous surface scanning. Unlike traditional vision-based tactile sensors that can only sense locally and are damaged when slid across surfaces, and cylindrical tactile sensors that can only roll along a fixed direction, our design enables omnidirectional rolling on surfaces. We accomplish this through our novel sensing system design, which has steel balls inside the sensor, forming a bearing layer between the gel and the rigid housing that allows rolling motion in all axes. The sensor streams tactile images through Wi-Fi, with online large-surface reconstruction capabilities. We present quantitative results for both reconstruction accuracy and image fusion performance. The results show that our sensor maintains geometric fidelity and high reconstruction accuracy even under multi-directional rolling, enabling uninterrupted surface scanning.

</details>


### [118] [H-RINS: Hierarchical Tightly-coupled Radar-Inertial Navigation via Smoothing and Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14109)
*Ali Alridha Abdulkarim,Mikhail Litvinov,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出分层雷达-惯性因子图框架，通过解耦高频率重置图与持久全局图，解决雷达-惯性状态估计中的漂移问题，实现高精度、低延迟的实时定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波雷达在视觉退化环境中提供鲁棒感知，但雷达-惯性状态估计易受漂移影响。雷达仅提供稀疏的体坐标系速度测量，对绝对姿态约束弱，导致IMU偏置在滑动窗口滤波器的短时间范围内可观测性差。

Method: 提出紧耦合分层雷达-惯性因子图框架：1) 高频率重置图融合IMU预积分、雷达速度和自适应零速度更新，生成平滑低延迟里程计；2) 持久全局图为全状态因子图，通过惯性数据与关键帧几何建图、闭环检测融合，维护完整位姿、速度和偏置信息；3) 使用增量平滑与建图技术，避免显式边缘化变量；4) 概率紧耦合机制将持久图优化的偏置及其协方差持续注入重置图先验。

Result: 系统在27倍实时执行速度下实现高精度、漂移减少的估计。通过解耦架构解决了偏置可观测性挑战，同时满足实时控制需求。

Conclusion: 分层因子图框架有效解决了雷达-惯性状态估计中的漂移问题，通过持久图确保长期偏置可观测性，重置图提供实时低延迟里程计，概率紧耦合机制将两者有机结合，实现了鲁棒高效的定位系统。

Abstract: Millimeter-wave radar provides robust perception in visually degraded environments. However, radar-inertial state estimation is inherently susceptible to drift. Because radar yields only sparse, body-frame velocity measurements, it provides weak constraints on absolute orientation. Consequently, IMU biases remain poorly observable over the short time horizons typical of sliding-window filters. To address this fundamental observability challenge, we propose a tightly coupled, hierarchical radar-inertial factor graph framework. Our architecture decouples the estimation problem into a high-rate resetting graph and a persistent global graph. The resetting graph fuses IMU preintegration, radar velocities, and adaptive Zero-Velocity Updates (ZUPT) to generate the smooth, low-latency odometry required for real-time control. Concurrently, the persistent graph is a full-state factor graph maintaining the complete information of poses, velocities, and biases by fusing inertial data with keyframe-based geometric mapping and loop closures. Leveraging Incremental Smoothing and Mapping, the persistent graph can operate without explicit marginalization of variables, preserving their information while ensuring long-term bias observability. The cornerstone of our approach is a probabilistic tight-coupling mechanism: fully observable, optimized biases and their exact covariances are continuously injected from the persistent graph into the resetting graph's prior, effectively anchoring the high-rate estimator against integration drift. Extensive evaluations demonstrate our system achieves high accuracy with drift-reduced estimation at 27x real-time execution speeds. We release the implementation code and datasets upon the acceptance of the paper.

</details>


### [119] [See, Learn, Assist: Safe and Self-Paced Robotic Rehabilitation via Video-Based Learning from Demonstration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14160)
*Ali Alabbas,Camillo Murgia,Joanne Regan,Philip Long*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个远程治疗师教学框架，通过RGB-D视频编码康复训练为6-DoF轨迹，使用解耦混合控制架构实现空间合规虚拟隧道和基于努力的时序扩展，支持三种康复模式，并通过GMR模型确保安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决远程康复治疗中治疗师无法现场指导的问题，需要系统能够准确复现治疗师演示的训练动作，同时适应不同患者的解剖结构差异，并在各种康复模式下保证患者安全。

Method: 使用RGB-D视频编码治疗师演示为6-DoF身体中心轨迹（Cartesian DMPs），采用解耦混合控制架构构建空间合规虚拟隧道和基于努力的时序扩展机制，支持被动、主动辅助和主动抵抗三种康复模式，通过GMR模型实时学习患者肢体特征以检测异常交互力。

Result: 系统实现高精度轨迹复现（平均误差3.7cm）和关节活动度复现（误差5.5度），动态交互试验证实控制器能有效执行基于努力的进展控制，同时在人类干扰下保持严格的空间路径遵循。

Conclusion: 该框架成功实现了远程治疗师教学和机器人辅助康复训练，通过先进的轨迹编码、混合控制架构和安全机制，为远程康复提供了精确、安全和适应性强的解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a novel framework that allows therapists to teach robot-assisted rehabilitation exercises remotely via RGB-D video. Our system encodes demonstrations as 6-DoF body-centric trajectories using Cartesian Dynamic Movement Primitives (DMPs), ensuring accurate posture-independent spatial generalization across diverse patient anatomies. Crucially, we execute these trajectories through a decoupled hybrid control architecture that constructs a spatially compliant virtual tunnel, paired with an effort-based temporal dilation mechanism. This architecture is applied to three distinct rehabilitation modalities: Passive, Active-Assisted, and Active-Resistive, by dynamically linking the exercise's execution phase to the patient's tangential force contribution.
