Motivation: 基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的机器人操作系统在易混淆物体场景中缺乏鲁棒性，容易产生不可预测的错误。初步分析表明这些失败主要源于VLM固有的捷径学习问题，限制了它们准确区分易混淆特征的能力。

Method: 提出混淆感知上下文学习（CAICL）方法：首先进行混淆定位和分析，识别潜在的混淆源；然后将这些信息作为提示，引导VLM关注最可能导致误识别的特征。

Result: 在VIMA-Bench上的大量实验表明，CAICL有效解决了捷径学习问题，实现了85.5%的成功率，并在不同泛化程度的任务中表现出良好的稳定性。

Conclusion: CAICL方法显著提升了VLM在机器人操作中面对易混淆物体时的性能和鲁棒性，为解决VLM的捷径学习问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have significantly improved the generalization capabilities of robotic manipulation. However, VLM-based systems often suffer from a lack of robustness, leading to unpredictable errors, particularly in scenarios involving confusable objects. Our preliminary analysis reveals that these failures are mainly caused by shortcut learning problem inherently in VLMs, limiting their ability to accurately distinguish between confusable features. To this end, we propose Confusion-Aware In-Context Learning (CAICL), a method that enhances VLM performance in confusable scenarios for robotic manipulation. The approach begins with confusion localization and analysis, identifying potential sources of confusion. This information is then used as a prompt for the VLM to focus on features most likely to cause misidentification. Extensive experiments on the VIMA-Bench show that CAICL effectively addresses the shortcut learning issue, achieving a 85.5\% success rate and showing good stability across tasks with different degrees of generalization.

</details>


### [158] [Master Micro Residual Correction with Adaptive Tactile Fusion and Force-Mixed Control for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15152)
*Xingting Li,Yifan Xie,Han Liu,Wei Hou,Guangyu Chen,Shoujie Li,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: M2-ResiPolicy：一种主-微残差控制架构，通过10Hz主引导策略和60Hz微残差校正器协同工作，结合视觉触觉感知和力反馈，提升机器人精细操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉模仿学习方法在长时程规划方面表现良好，但难以感知摩擦变化、初始滑动等关键交互线索，且难以平衡全局任务连贯性与局部反应性反馈。机器人接触丰富和精细操作面临复杂交互动力学和多时间尺度控制的挑战。

Method: 提出M2-ResiPolicy架构，包含：1）10Hz主引导策略（MGP），使用扩散模型生成时间一致的动作块，通过触觉强度驱动的自适应融合机制动态调节视觉与触觉的感知权重；2）60Hz微残差校正器（MRC），使用轻量级GRU基于TCP力反馈提供实时动作补偿；3）与力混合PBIC执行层集成，有效调节接触力确保交互安全。

Result: 在脆弱物体抓取和精密插入等多个要求苛刻的任务中，M2-ResiPolicy显著优于标准扩散策略（DP）和最先进的反应扩散策略（RDP），在芯片抓取中实现93%的无损伤成功率，并展现出优越的力调节稳定性。

Conclusion: M2-ResiPolicy通过主-微残差控制架构成功解决了机器人精细操作中多时间尺度控制的挑战，实现了视觉与触觉感知的有效融合，为接触丰富和精细操作任务提供了高性能解决方案。

Abstract: Robotic contact-rich and fine-grained manipulation remains a significant challenge due to complex interaction dynamics and the competing requirements of multi-timescale control. While current visual imitation learning methods excel at long-horizon planning, they often fail to perceive critical interaction cues like friction variations or incipient slip, and struggle to balance global task coherence with local reactive feedback. To address these challenges, we propose M2-ResiPolicy, a novel Master-Micro residual control architecture that synergizes high-level action guidance with low-level correction. The framework consists of a Master-Guidance Policy (MGP) operating at 10 Hz, which generates temporally consistent action chunks via a diffusion-based backbone and employs a tactile-intensity-driven adaptive fusion mechanism to dynamically modulate perceptual weights between vision and touch. Simultaneously, a high-frequency (60 Hz) Micro-Residual Corrector (MRC) utilizes a lightweight GRU to provide real-time action compensation based on TCP wrench feedback. This policy is further integrated with a force-mixed PBIC execution layer, effectively regulating contact forces to ensure interaction safety. Experiments across several demanding tasks including fragile object grasping and precision insertion, demonstrate that M2-ResiPolicy significantly outperforms standard Diffusion Policy (DP) and state-of-the-art Reactive Diffusion Policy (RDP), achieving a 93\% damage-free success rate in chip grasping and superior force regulation stability.

</details>


### [159] [ForceVLA2: Unleashing Hybrid Force-Position Control with Force Awareness for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15169)
*Yang Li,Zhaxizhuoma,Hongru Jiang,Junjie Xia,Hongquan Zhang,Jinda Du,Yunsong Zhou,Jia Zeng,Ce Hao,Jieji Ren,Qiaojun Yu,Cewu Lu,Yu Qiao,Jiangmiao Pang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ForceVLA2是一个端到端的视觉-语言-动作框架，为机器人提供混合力-位置控制和显式力感知，显著提升了接触丰富操作的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的具身智能在接触丰富的操作任务中主要依赖位置控制，缺乏对交互力的显式感知和调节，导致在实际任务中的稳定性、精度和鲁棒性受限。

Method: ForceVLA2在VLM专家中引入基于力的提示来构建跨阶段的力感知任务概念，并在动作专家中采用跨尺度混合专家（MoE）自适应融合这些概念与实时交互力，实现闭环混合力-位置调节。同时构建了ForceVLA2-Dataset数据集用于训练和评估。

Result: 在5个接触丰富任务（擦拭、按压、组装等）上的实验表明，ForceVLA2显著提高了成功率和可靠性，分别比pi0和pi0.5高出48.0%和35.0%，并缓解了手臂过载和不稳定接触等常见故障模式。

Conclusion: ForceVLA2通过引入显式力感知和混合力-位置控制，有效推进了VLA中的力感知交互物理智能，为接触丰富的机器人操作提供了更稳定、精确和鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Embodied intelligence for contact-rich manipulation has predominantly relied on position control, while explicit awareness and regulation of interaction forces remain under-explored, limiting stability, precision, and robustness in real-world tasks. We propose ForceVLA2, an end-to-end vision-language-action framework that equips robots with hybrid force-position control and explicit force awareness. ForceVLA2 introduces force-based prompts into the VLM expert to construct force-aware task concepts across stages, and employs a Cross-Scale Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) in the action expert to adaptively fuse these concepts with real-time interaction forces for closed-loop hybrid force-position regulation. To support learning and evaluation, we construct ForceVLA2-Dataset, containing 1,000 trajectories over 5 contact-rich tasks, including wiping, pressing, and assembling, with multi-view images, task prompts, proprioceptive state, and force signals. Extensive experiments show that ForceVLA2 substantially improves success rates and reliability in contact-rich manipulation, outperforming pi0 and pi0.5 by 48.0% and 35.0%, respectively, across the 5 tasks, and mitigating common failure modes such as arm overload and unstable contact, thereby actively advancing force-aware interactive physical intelligence in VLAs. The project page is available at https://sites.google.com/view/force-vla2/home.

</details>


### [160] [What Matters for Scalable and Robust Learning in End-to-End Driving Planners?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15185)
*David Holtz,Niklas Hanselmann,Simon Doll,Marius Cordts,Bernt Schiele*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统性地重新评估了端到端自动驾驶架构中常见设计模式对闭环性能的影响，并基于这些洞察提出了轻量级、高可扩展的BevAD架构，在Bench2Drive基准上取得了72.7%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前端到端自动驾驶架构通常在开环数据集上开发，关注感知和规划模块的中间任务性能，但这些在开环中表现优异的架构设计往往无法有效转化为可扩展的、鲁棒的闭环驾驶学习。需要重新评估常见架构模式对闭环性能的实际影响。

Method: 系统性地重新检验三种常见架构模式对闭环性能的影响：(1)高分辨率感知表示，(2)解耦的轨迹表示，(3)生成式规划。基于这些分析洞察，提出了BevAD架构——一种轻量级、高可扩展的端到端驾驶架构，采用纯模仿学习方法。

Result: BevAD在Bench2Drive基准测试中取得了72.7%的成功率，并展示了强大的数据扩展能力。研究揭示了这些架构模式在组合使用时存在的意外局限性以及尚未充分探索的协同效应。

Conclusion: 端到端自动驾驶架构的设计需要重新思考，开环性能指标不能直接预测闭环驾驶效果。BevAD通过系统性的架构分析，实现了轻量、可扩展且性能优异的闭环驾驶能力，为未来端到端自动驾驶研究提供了新的设计方向。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving has gained significant attention for its potential to learn robust behavior in interactive scenarios and scale with data. Popular architectures often build on separate modules for perception and planning connected through latent representations, such as bird's eye view feature grids, to maintain end-to-end differentiability. This paradigm emerged mostly on open-loop datasets, with evaluation focusing not only on driving performance, but also intermediate perception tasks. Unfortunately, architectural advances that excel in open-loop often fail to translate to scalable learning of robust closed-loop driving. In this paper, we systematically re-examine the impact of common architectural patterns on closed-loop performance: (1) high-resolution perceptual representations, (2) disentangled trajectory representations, and (3) generative planning. Crucially, our analysis evaluates the combined impact of these patterns, revealing both unexpected limitations as well as underexplored synergies. Building on these insights, we introduce BevAD, a novel lightweight and highly scalable end-to-end driving architecture. BevAD achieves 72.7% success rate on the Bench2Drive benchmark and demonstrates strong data-scaling behavior using pure imitation learning. Our code and models are publicly available here: https://dmholtz.github.io/bevad/

</details>


### [161] [NavGSim: High-Fidelity Gaussian Splatting Simulator for Large-Scale Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15186)
*Jiahang Liu,Yuanxing Duan,Jiazhao Zhang,Minghan Li,Shaoan Wang,Zhizheng Zhang,He Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NavGSim是一个基于高斯泼溅的大规模导航环境模拟器，通过分层3D高斯泼溅框架实现数百平方米场景的光线真实渲染，并提供碰撞检测、多GPU开发API等功能，显著提升了VLA模型在导航任务中的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习中的环境模拟面临渲染和物理仿真的重大挑战，特别是在导航任务中，轨迹经常跨越多个房间或整个楼层，需要高保真、大规模的环境模拟器来支持有效的策略训练。

Method: 基于分层3D高斯泼溅框架构建NavGSim模拟器，实现大规模场景的光线真实渲染；提出基于高斯泼溅的切片技术直接从重建的高斯中提取可导航区域进行碰撞模拟；提供全面的API支持多GPU开发，包括自定义场景重建、机器人配置、策略训练和评估工具。

Result: 在NavGSim中收集轨迹训练VLA模型，在模拟和真实环境中评估性能。结果表明NavGSim显著增强了VLA模型的场景理解能力，使策略能够有效处理多样化的导航查询。

Conclusion: NavGSim作为一个基于高斯泼溅的高保真、大规模导航模拟器，有效解决了机器人学习中的环境模拟挑战，通过提供真实感渲染和碰撞模拟，显著提升了导航策略的训练效果和泛化能力。

Abstract: Simulating realistic environments for robots is widely recognized as a critical challenge in robot learning, particularly in terms of rendering and physical simulation. This challenge becomes even more pronounced in navigation tasks, where trajectories often extend across multiple rooms or entire floors. In this work, we present NavGSim, a Gaussian Splatting-based simulator designed to generate high-fidelity, large-scale navigation environments. Built upon a hierarchical 3D Gaussian Splatting framework, NavGSim enables photorealistic rendering in expansive scenes spanning hundreds of square meters. To simulate navigation collisions, we introduce a Gaussian Splatting-based slice technique that directly extracts navigable areas from reconstructed Gaussians. Additionally, for ease of use, we provide comprehensive NavGSim APIs supporting multi-GPU development, including tools for custom scene reconstruction, robot configuration, policy training, and evaluation. To evaluate NavGSim's effectiveness, we train a Vision-Language-Action (VLA) model using trajectories collected from NavGSim and assess its performance in both simulated and real-world environments. Our results demonstrate that NavGSim significantly enhances the VLA model's scene understanding, enabling the policy to handle diverse navigation queries effectively.

</details>


### [162] [Coupled Particle Filters for Robust Affordance Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15223)
*Patrick Lowin,Vito Mengers,Oliver Brock*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种通过耦合两个递归估计器（可抓取区域和可移动区域）来消解机器人感知中视觉、几何和语义歧义的方法，显著提升操作可能性预测精度和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作可能性估计面临视觉、几何和语义等多重歧义的挑战，现有方法难以同时处理这些不确定性，需要一种能够整合互补信号并减少歧义的解决方案。

Method: 设计两个耦合的递归估计器，分别针对操作可能性的两个子方面：可抓取区域和可移动区域。每个估计器编码特定属性的规律性以减少不确定性，通过双向信息交换机制，使注意力聚焦于两个估计器一致的区域，即真正的操作可能性区域。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上评估，相比Where2Act、Hands-as-Probes和HRP三个最新方法，精度分别提升308%、245%和257%，在低光照、杂乱环境等挑战条件下保持鲁棒性。真实世界评估中达到70%的成功率。

Conclusion: 耦合互补估计器的方法能够产生精确、鲁棒且适合具体机器人形态的操作可能性预测，通过双向信息交换机制有效消解感知歧义，为机器人操作提供可靠的环境交互理解。

Abstract: Robotic affordance estimation is challenging due to visual, geometric, and semantic ambiguities in sensory input. We propose a method that disambiguates these signals using two coupled recursive estimators for sub-aspects of affordances: graspable and movable regions. Each estimator encodes property-specific regularities to reduce uncertainty, while their coupling enables bidirectional information exchange that focuses attention on regions where both agree, i.e., affordances. Evaluated on a real-world dataset, our method outperforms three recent affordance estimators (Where2Act, Hands-as-Probes, and HRP) by 308%, 245%, and 257% in precision, and remains robust under challenging conditions such as low light or cluttered environments. Furthermore, our method achieves a 70% success rate in our real-world evaluation. These results demonstrate that coupling complementary estimators yields precise, robust, and embodiment-appropriate affordance predictions.

</details>


### [163] [A Methodology for Dynamic Parameters Identification of 3-DOF Parallel Robots in Terms of Relevant Parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15254)
*Miguel Díaz-Rodríguez,Vicente Mata,Angel Valera,Alvaro Page*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于相关参数的完全并联机器人动力学参数辨识方法，通过模型简化、加权最小二乘辨识和统计模型缩减，确保物理可行性，并在实际3-DOF并联机器人上验证


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械系统动力学参数辨识对于改进基于模型的控制和实现真实动力学仿真至关重要。传统方法通常只能辨识基参数子集，且某些参数由于对机器人动力学贡献小，在测量噪声和建模误差存在时辨识质量下降。因此需要一种针对完全并联机器人的相关参数子集辨识策略。

Method: 从完整动力学模型出发，首先基于每个连杆的几何特性和完全并联机器人腿的对称性进行模型简化。然后采用加权最小二乘法进行参数辨识。接着通过统计考虑缩减模型，直到满足物理可行性条件。同时提出了一种基于相关参数的正向动力学计算方法。

Result: 该方法在实际3-DOF并联机器人的两种不同配置上进行了实验验证。辨识模型的逆动力学和正向动力学响应与实验结果一致，验证了所提策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的基于相关参数的动力学参数辨识策略能够有效处理完全并联机器人的参数辨识问题，通过模型简化和统计缩减确保物理可行性，实验验证表明该方法能获得与实验一致的动力学响应。

Abstract: The identification of dynamic parameters in mechanical systems is important for improving model-based control as well as for performing realistic dynamic simulations. Generally, when identification techniques are applied only a subset of so-called base parameters can be identified. More even, some of these parameters cannot be identified properly given that they have a small contribution to the robot dynamics and hence in the presence of noise in measurements and discrepancy in modeling, their quality of being identifiable decreases. For this reason, a strategy for dynamic parameter identification of fully parallel robots in terms of a subset called relevant parameters is put forward. The objective of the proposed methodology is to start from a full dynamic model, then simplification concerning the geometry of each link and, the symmetry due to legs of fully parallel robots, are carried out. After that, the identification is done by Weighted Least Squares. Then, with statistical considerations the model is reduced until the physical feasibility conditions are met. The application of the propose strategy has been experimentally tested on two difierent configurations of actual 3-DOF parallel robots. The response of the inverse and forward dynamics of the identified models agrees with experiments. In order to evaluate the forward dynamics response, an approach for obtaining the forward dynamics in terms of the relevant parameters is also proposed.

</details>


### [164] [HapticVLA: Contact-Rich Manipulation via Vision-Language-Action Model without Inference-Time Tactile Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15257)
*Konstantin Gubernatorov,Mikhail Sannikov,Ilya Mikhalchuk,Egor Kuznetsov,Makar Artemov,Ogunwoye Faith Ouwatobi,Marcelino Fernando,Artem Asanov,Ziang Guo,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HapticVLA：一种无需部署触觉传感器的触觉感知视觉-语言-动作架构，通过安全感知奖励加权流匹配和触觉蒸馏两阶段实现离线学习触觉感知操作


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉感知对VLA架构至关重要，但专用触觉硬件增加了成本并降低了跨机器人平台的可复现性。作者认为触觉感知操作可以通过离线学习实现，在推理时无需直接触觉反馈。

Method: 提出HapticVLA两阶段方法：1) 安全感知奖励加权流匹配(SA-RWFM)：训练包含预计算安全感知触觉奖励的流匹配动作专家，惩罚过度抓取力和次优抓取轨迹；2) 触觉蒸馏(TD)：将触觉感知能力转移到传统VLA中，从SA-RWFM教师蒸馏紧凑触觉令牌，训练学生VLA从视觉和状态模态预测该令牌，实现无需触觉传感器的触觉感知动作生成。

Result: 在真实世界实验中，HapticVLA达到86.7%的平均成功率，持续优于基线VLA（包括在推理时提供直接触觉反馈的版本）。

Conclusion: HapticVLA能够在VLA中保持接触丰富的触觉感知推理，同时消除部署时对板载触觉传感器的需求，实现了触觉感知操作的离线学习和无传感器部署。

Abstract: Tactile sensing is a crucial capability for Vision-Language-Action (VLA) architectures, as it enables dexterous and safe manipulation in contact-rich tasks. However, reliance on dedicated tactile hardware increases cost and reduces reproducibility across robotic platforms. We argue that tactile-aware manipulation can be learned offline and deployed without direct haptic feedback at inference. To this end, we present HapticVLA, which proceeds in two tightly coupled stages: Safety-Aware Reward-Weighted Flow Matching (SA-RWFM) and Tactile Distillation (TD). SA-RWFM trains a flow-matching action expert that incorporates precomputed, safety-aware tactile rewards penalizing excessive grasping force and suboptimal grasping trajectories. TD further transfers this tactile-aware capability into a conventional VLA: we distill a compact tactile token from the SA-RWFM teacher and train a student VLA to predict that token from vision and state modalities, enabling tactile-aware action generation at inference without requiring on-board tactile sensors. This design preserves contact-rich tactile-aware reasoning within VLA while removing the need for on-board tactile sensors during deployment. On real-world experiments, HapticVLA achieves a mean success rate of 86.7%, consistently outperforming baseline VLAs - including versions provided with direct tactile feedback during inference.

</details>


### [165] [MoE-ACT: Scaling Multi-Task Bimanual Manipulation with Sparse Language-Conditioned Mixture-of-Experts Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15265)
*Kangjun Guo,Haichao Liu,Yanji Sun,Ruhan Zhao,Jinni Zhou,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MoE-ACT：一种轻量级多任务模仿学习框架，通过稀疏专家混合模块增强动作分块Transformer，显著提升双臂操作的多任务性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在真实家庭和工业应用中需要处理多个任务，但任务间的分布外变化常导致任务干扰和负迁移问题，需要开发能统一处理多任务的鲁棒策略

Method: 将稀疏专家混合模块集成到ACT的Transformer编码器中，通过自适应激活解耦多任务动作分布；使用FiLM动态调制动作token；结合多尺度交叉注意力同时关注高低级语义特征；引入文本信息构建视觉中心、语言条件的动作生成系统

Result: MoE-ACT在仿真和真实双臂设置中显著提升多任务性能，平均成功率比vanilla ACT提高33%，展现出更强的鲁棒性和泛化能力

Conclusion: MoE-ACT通过专家混合机制有效缓解多任务学习中的干扰问题，为复杂双臂操作环境提供了更鲁棒和泛化的解决方案

Abstract: The ability of robots to handle multiple tasks under a unified policy is critical for deploying embodied intelligence in real-world household and industrial applications. However, out-of-distribution variation across tasks often causes severe task interference and negative transfer when training general robotic policies. To address this challenge, we propose a lightweight multi-task imitation learning framework for bimanual manipulation, termed Mixture-of-Experts-Enhanced Action Chunking Transformer (MoE-ACT), which integrates sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) modules into the Transformer encoder of ACT. The MoE layer decomposes a unified task policy into independently invoked expert components. Through adaptive activation, it naturally decouples multi-task action distributions in latent space. During decoding, Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM) dynamically modulates action tokens to improve consistency between action generation and task instructions. In parallel, multi-scale cross-attention enables the policy to simultaneously focus on both low-level and high-level semantic features, providing rich visual information for robotic manipulation. We further incorporate textual information, transitioning the framework from a purely vision-based model to a vision-centric, language-conditioned action generation system. Experimental validation in both simulation and a real-world dual-arm setup shows that MoE-ACT substantially improves multi-task performance. Specifically, MoE-ACT outperforms vanilla ACT by an average of 33% in success rate. These results indicate that MoE-ACT provides stronger robustness and generalization in complex multi-task bimanual manipulation environments. Our open-source project page can be found at https://j3k7.github.io/MoE-ACT/.

</details>


### [166] [GNIO: Gated Neural Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15281)
*Dapeng Feng,Yizhen Yin,Zhiqiang Chen,Yuhua Qi,Hongbo Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GNIO是一种基于学习的惯性里程计框架，通过可学习的运动库和门控预测头来减少MEMS传感器的漂移误差，在静止时抑制噪声，在动态运动时增强预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本MEMS传感器存在噪声和偏置不稳定性导致的快速漂移问题。现有数据驱动方法依赖固定窗口回归，难以处理静止时的微漂移和复杂运动转换时的模式融合。

Method: 提出门控神经惯性里程计(GNIO)框架，包含两个关键创新：1)可学习的运动库，从全局运动模式字典中查询语义上下文；2)门控预测头，将位移分解为幅度和方向，作为软可微分的零速度更新机制。

Result: 在四个公开基准测试中，GNIO显著减少了位置漂移。在OxIOD数据集上轨迹误差降低60.21%，在涉及频繁停止和不规则运动速度的挑战性场景中表现出优越的泛化能力。

Conclusion: GNIO通过显式建模运动有效性和上下文，有效解决了MEMS惯性导航中的漂移问题，在静止和动态运动场景中都表现出优于现有CNN和Transformer基线的性能。

Abstract: Inertial navigation using low-cost MEMS sensors is plagued by rapid drift due to sensor noise and bias instability. While recent data-driven approaches have made significant strides, they often struggle with micro-drifts during stationarity and mode fusion during complex motion transitions due to their reliance on fixed-window regression. In this work, we introduce Gated Neural Inertial Odometry (GNIO), a novel learning-based framework that explicitly models motion validity and context. We propose two key architectural innovations: \ding{182} a learnable Motion Bank that queries a global dictionary of motion patterns to provide semantic context beyond the local receptive field, and \ding{183} a Gated Prediction Head that decomposes displacement into magnitude and direction. This gating mechanism acts as a soft, differentiable Zero-Velocity Update (ZUPT), dynamically suppressing sensor noise during stationary periods while scaling predictions during dynamic motion. Extensive experiments across four public benchmarks demonstrate that GNIO significantly reduces position drift compared to state-of-the-art CNN and Transformer-based baselines. Notably, GNIO achieves a $60.21\%$ reduction in trajectory error on the OxIOD dataset and exhibits superior generalization in challenging scenarios involving frequent stops and irregular motion speeds.

</details>


### [167] [User-Tailored Learning to Forecast Walking Modes for Exosuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15329)
*Gabriele Abbate,Enrica Tricomi,Nathalie Gierden,Alessandro Giusti,Lorenzo Masia,Antonio Paolillo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 基于机器学习的感知模块，仅使用两个惯性传感器数据，实时估计穿戴外骨骼用户的三种行走模式（上楼梯、下楼梯、平地行走），支持未来和过去时间步的预测，并实现在线模型自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辅助机器人设备（如下肢软体外骨骼/外骨骼服）需要适应不同用户，但现有系统因重量和穿戴便利性限制而缺乏足够的感知能力。需要开发轻量化的感知系统来准确识别用户行走模式。

Method: 提出基于机器学习的感知模块，仅使用两个惯性传感器的数据。该方法不仅能估计当前行走模式，还能预测未来和回溯过去时间步的模式，支持控制决策。系统具备在线自标记功能，可自动标注采集的数据并针对新用户优化模型。

Result: 在真实数据集上的全面分析表明，该用户定制化感知模块效果显著。系统成功集成到外骨骼服中，通过闭环控制器在在线单受试者实验中验证了性能。

Conclusion: 该研究开发了一种轻量化的机器学习感知系统，仅需两个惯性传感器即可准确识别三种行走模式，支持在线自适应和闭环控制，为外骨骼服的智能感知提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Assistive robotic devices, like soft lower-limb exoskeletons or exosuits, are widely spreading with the promise of helping people in everyday life. To make such systems adaptive to the variety of users wearing them, it is desirable to endow exosuits with advanced perception systems. However, exosuits have little sensory equipment because they need to be light and easy to wear. This paper presents a perception module based on machine learning that aims at estimating 3 walking modes (i.e., ascending or descending stairs and walking on level ground) of users wearing an exosuit. We tackle this perception problem using only inertial data from two sensors. Our approach provides an estimate for both future and past timesteps that supports control and enables a self-labeling procedure for online model adaptation. Indeed, we show that our estimate can label data acquired online and refine the model for new users. A thorough analysis carried out on real-life datasets shows the effectiveness of our user-tailored perception module. Finally, we integrate our system with the exosuit in a closed-loop controller, validating its performance in an online single-subject experiment.

</details>


### [168] [NavThinker: Action-Conditioned World Models for Coupled Prediction and Planning in Social Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15359)
*Tianshuai Hu,Zeying Gong,Lingdong Kong,XiaoDong Mei,Yiyi Ding,Qi Zeng,Ao Liang,Rong Li,Yangyi Zhong,Junwei Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NavThinker是一个面向社会导航的未来感知框架，通过动作条件世界模型与在线强化学习耦合，实现机器人对场景和行人未来演变的预测规划，在多个仿真和真实环境中达到最先进的导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会导航需要机器人在动态人类环境中安全行动。有效行为需要前瞻性思考：推理在不同机器人动作下场景和行人如何演变，而不仅仅是响应当前观测。这形成了预测-规划的耦合挑战，其中机器人动作和人类运动相互影响。

Method: 提出NavThinker框架，包含动作条件世界模型和在线强化学习。世界模型在Depth Anything V2补丁特征空间运行，自回归预测未来场景几何和人类运动；多头解码器生成未来深度图和人类轨迹。策略使用DD-PPO训练，通过两种方式注入世界模型的前瞻信号：(i)动作条件未来特征融合到当前观测嵌入；(ii)基于预测人类轨迹的社会奖励塑形。

Result: 在单机器人和多机器人Social-HM3D实验中达到最先进的导航成功率，零样本迁移到Social-MP3D，并在Unitree Go2机器人上实现真实世界部署，验证了泛化能力和实际应用性。

Conclusion: NavThinker通过耦合动作条件世界模型与强化学习，有效解决了社会导航中的预测-规划耦合挑战，实现了对未来场景演变的推理，在仿真和真实环境中均表现出优越性能。

Abstract: Social navigation requires robots to act safely in dynamic human environments. Effective behavior demands thinking ahead: reasoning about how the scene and pedestrians evolve under different robot actions rather than reacting to current observations alone. This creates a coupled prediction-planning challenge, where robot actions and human motion mutually influence each other. To address this challenge, we propose NavThinker, a future-aware framework that couples an action-conditioned world model with on-policy reinforcement learning. The world model operates in the Depth Anything V2 patch feature space and performs autoregressive prediction of future scene geometry and human motion; multi-head decoders then produce future depth maps and human trajectories, yielding a future-aware state aligned with traversability and interaction risk. Crucially, we train the policy with DD-PPO while injecting world-model think-ahead signals via: (i) action-conditioned future features fused into the current observation embedding and (ii) social reward shaping from predicted human trajectories. Experiments on single- and multi-robot Social-HM3D show state-of-the-art navigation success, with zero-shot transfer to Social-MP3D and real-world deployment on a Unitree Go2, validating generalization and practical applicability. Webpage: https://github.com/hutslib/NavThinker.

</details>


### [169] [Zero-Shot Generalization from Motion Demonstrations to New Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15445)
*Kilian Freitag,Alvin Combrink,Nadia Figueroa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Gaussian Graph框架，将学习到的运动基元重新解释为离散顶点，实现连续控制与离散图搜索的结合，支持从孤立演示中组合新任务


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端模型需要大数据集和重计算，而现有动力学系统学习方法只能建模孤立任务，难以复用演示数据到新行为。需要一种能组合孤立演示以实现泛化的方法

Method: 引入Gaussian Graph，将学习到的运动基元空间组件重新解释为离散顶点及其连接。提出两种框架：Stitching构建时间不变动力学系统，Chaining提供序列化动力学系统用于复杂运动

Result: 仿真和真实机器人实验表明，该方法能成功泛化到新任务，而基线方法失败

Conclusion: Gaussian Graph框架有效桥接了连续控制与离散图搜索，实现了从少量演示中组合新任务的能力，同时保持了收敛性保证

Abstract: Learning motion policies from expert demonstrations is an essential paradigm in modern robotics. While end-to-end models aim for broad generalization, they require large datasets and computationally heavy inference. Conversely, learning dynamical systems (DS) provides fast, reactive, and provably stable control from very few demonstrations. However, existing DS learning methods typically model isolated tasks and struggle to reuse demonstrations for novel behaviors. In this work, we formalize the problem of combining isolated demonstrations within a shared workspace to enable generalization to unseen tasks. The Gaussian Graph is introduced, which reinterprets spatial components of learned motion primitives as discrete vertices with connections to one another. This formulation allows us to bridge continuous control with discrete graph search. We propose two frameworks leveraging this graph: Stitching, for constructing time-invariant DSs, and Chaining, giving a sequence-based DS for complex motions while retaining convergence guarantees. Simulations and real-robot experiments show that these methods successfully generalize to new tasks where baseline methods fail.

</details>


### [170] [RoCo Challenge at AAAI 2026: Benchmarking Robotic Collaborative Manipulation for Assembly Towards Industrial Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15469)
*Haichao Liu,Yuheng Zhou,Zhenyu Wu,Ziheng Ji,Ziyu Shan,Qianzhun Wang,Ruixuan Liu,Zhiyuan Yang,Yejun Gu,Shalman Khan,Shijun Yan,Jun Liu,Haiyue Zhu,Changliu Liu,Jianfei Yang,Jingbing Zhang,Ziwei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoCo挑战赛为工业机器人装配任务建立了一个仿真与真实世界结合的基准测试平台，专注于行星齿轮箱的高精度装配任务，吸引了全球团队参与并验证了双模型框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着具身人工智能的发展，工业机器人操作正从孤立感知向集成连续动作转变。为推进这一能力，需要建立能够同时涵盖仿真和真实世界的基准测试平台，以解决工业装配中的高精度、长时程任务挑战。

Method: 开发了RoCo挑战赛，包含仿真轮和真实世界轮两个阶段。仿真轮在Isaac Sim中建立数据收集、训练和评估系统，定义细粒度任务阶段进行逐步评分；真实世界轮使用物理齿轮箱组件和高质量遥操作数据集，通过双臂机器人进行实际部署。

Result: 吸引了来自10多个国家的60多个团队、170多名参与者参与。结果显示，用于长时程多任务学习的双模型框架非常有效，特别是ARC-VLA和RoboCola解决方案表现突出。从失败中恢复的课程数据策略对成功部署至关重要。

Conclusion: RoCo挑战赛成功建立了工业具身人工智能的基准测试平台，验证了双模型框架在复杂装配任务中的有效性，并为未来工业EAI发展提供了数据集、评估方法和关键见解。

Abstract: Embodied Artificial Intelligence (EAI) is rapidly developing, gradually subverting previous autonomous systems' paradigms from isolated perception to integrated, continuous action. This transition is highly significant for industrial robotic manipulation, promising to free human workers from repetitive, dangerous daily labor. To benchmark and advance this capability, we introduce the Robotic Collaborative Assembly Assistance (RoCo) Challenge with a dataset towards simulation and real-world assembly manipulation. Set against the backdrop of human-centered manufacturing, this challenge focuses on a high-precision planetary gearbox assembly task, a demanding yet highly representative operation in modern industry. Built upon a self-developed data collection, training, and evaluation system in Isaac Sim, and utilizing a dual-arm robot for real-world deployment, the challenge operates in two phases. The Simulation Round defines fine-grained task phases for step-wise scoring to handle the long-horizon nature of the assembly. The Real-World Round mirrors this evaluation with physical gearbox components and high-quality teleoperated datasets. The core tasks require assembling an epicyclic gearbox from scratch, including mounting three planet gears, a sun gear, and a ring gear. Attracting over 60 teams and 170+ participants from more than 10 countries, the challenge yielded highly effective solutions, most notably ARC-VLA and RoboCola. Results demonstrate that a dual-model framework for long-horizon multi-task learning is highly effective, and the strategic utilization of recovery-from-failure curriculum data is a critical insight for successful deployment. This report outlines the competition setup, evaluation approach, key findings, and future directions for industrial EAI. Our dataset, CAD files, code, and evaluation results can be found at: https://rocochallenge.github.io/RoCo2026/.

</details>


### [171] [On the Derivation of Tightly-Coupled LiDAR-Inertial Odometry with VoxelMap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15471)
*Zhihao Zhan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提供了一个紧密耦合的LiDAR-IMU里程计在迭代误差状态卡尔曼滤波框架下的数学公式化表达，使用VoxelMap表示，旨在作为技术参考和入门指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为紧密耦合的LiDAR-IMU里程计系统提供一个清晰、自包含的数学推导，统一几何建模和概率状态估计，使用一致的符号和明确的公式化表达，既作为技术参考又作为系统架构和估计原理的入门指南。

Method: 采用迭代误差状态卡尔曼滤波框架，结合VoxelMap表示，对紧密耦合的LiDAR-IMU里程计进行数学公式化表达，提供从几何建模到概率状态估计的完整推导。

Result: 提供了一个统一的数学框架，将几何建模和概率状态估计通过一致的符号和明确的公式化表达相结合，形成了自包含的技术参考文档。

Conclusion: 该论文成功地为紧密耦合的LiDAR-IMU里程计系统提供了一个清晰的数学公式化表达，统一了几何建模和概率状态估计，可作为技术参考和入门学习材料。

Abstract: This note presents a concise mathematical formulation of tightly-coupled LiDAR-Inertial Odometry within an iterated error-state Kalman filter framework using a VoxelMap representation. Rather than proposing a new algorithm, it provides a clear and self-contained derivation that unifies the geometric modeling and probabilistic state estimation through consistent notation and explicit formulations. The document is intended to serve both as a technical reference and as an accessible entry point for a foundational understanding of the system architecture and estimation principles.

</details>


### [172] [Optimal control of differentially flat underactuated planar robots in the perspective of oscillation mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15528)
*Stefano Lovato,Michele Tonan,Matteo Bottin,Matteo Massaro,Alberto Doria,Giulio Rosati*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了将最优控制与微分平坦控制结合，以改善欠驱动机器人的轨迹跟踪性能，特别关注减少因忽略摩擦引起的残余振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欠驱动机器人具有自由度多于执行器的特点，通过特定质量分布设计可应用微分平坦理论控制。然而，忽略摩擦简化动态模型会导致末端执行器在目标位置产生残余振荡，需要改进控制方法。

Method: 采用最优控制与微分平坦控制相结合的方法，通过形式化分析和数值仿真验证。最优控制用于规划平坦变量，考虑不同的二次性能指标：控制努力（电机扭矩）和欠驱动关节的势能。

Result: 研究表明最优控制可用于规划平坦变量，考虑不同性能指标。特别地，最小化势能可以设计出对欠驱动关节刚度和阻尼变化具有鲁棒性的运动规律，从而在刚度和阻尼不匹配情况下减少振荡。

Conclusion: 最优控制与微分平坦控制的结合能有效改善欠驱动机器人的轨迹跟踪性能，通过最小化势能设计的运动规律对系统参数变化具有鲁棒性，有助于减少残余振荡。

Abstract: Underactuated robots are characterized by a larger number of degrees of freedom than actuators and if they are designed with a specific mass distribution, they can be controlled by means of differential flatness theory. This structural property enables the development of lightweight and cost-effective robotic systems with enhanced dexterity. However, a key challenge lies in managing the passive joints, whose control demands precise and comprehensive dynamic modeling of the system. To simplify dynamic models, particularly for low-speed trajectories, friction is often neglected. While this assumption simplifies analysis and control design, it introduces residual oscillations of the end-effector about the target position. In this paper, the possibility of using optimal control along with differential flatness control is investigated to improve the tracking of the planned trajectories. First, the study was carried out through formal analysis, and then, it was validated by means of numerical simulations. Results highlight that optimal control can be used to plan the flat variables considering different (quadratic) performance indices: control effort, i.e. motor torque, and potential energy of the considered underactuated joint. Moreover, the minimization of potential energy can be used to design motion laws that are robust against variation of the stiffness and damping of the underactuated joint, thus reducing oscillations in the case of stiffness/damping mismatch.

</details>


### [173] [From Passive Observer to Active Critic: Reinforcement Learning Elicits Process Reasoning for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15600)
*Yibin Liu,Yaxing Lyu,Daqi Gao,Zhixuan Liang,Weiliang Tang,Shilong Mu,Xiaokang Yang,Yao Mu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PRIMO R1是一个7B参数的视频多模态大语言模型框架，通过强化学习将被动观察的视频MLLM转变为主动评估的"批评家"，用于长时程机器人操作的过程监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视频MLLM主要采用监督微调范式，只能作为被动的"观察者"识别正在进行的事件，无法评估当前状态相对于最终任务目标的进展，这成为长时程机器人操作过程监督的主要瓶颈。

Method: 1. 使用基于结果的强化学习激励显式的思维链生成以进行进展估计；2. 构建结构化时序输入，通过在初始状态图像和当前状态图像之间明确锚定视频序列；3. 提出PRIMO数据集和基准进行训练和评估。

Result: 1. 7B模型相比专用推理基线的平均绝对误差降低50%；2. 在72B规模通用MLLM上实现显著的相对准确率提升；3. 在RoboFail基准上达到67.0%的准确率，超过OpenAI o1等闭源模型6.0%；4. 在困难故障检测任务上表现出强大的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: PRIMO R1成功将视频MLLM从被动观察者转变为主动批评家，通过强化学习驱动的显式思维链生成和结构化时序输入，在长时程机器人操作过程监督中实现了最先进的性能，并展现出强大的泛化能力。

Abstract: Accurate process supervision remains a critical challenge for long-horizon robotic manipulation. A primary bottleneck is that current video MLLMs, trained primarily under a Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) paradigm, function as passive "Observers" that recognize ongoing events rather than evaluating the current state relative to the final task goal. In this paper, we introduce PRIMO R1 (Process Reasoning Induced Monitoring), a 7B framework that transforms video MLLMs into active "Critics". We leverage outcome-based Reinforcement Learning to incentivize explicit Chain-of-Thought generation for progress estimation. Furthermore, our architecture constructs a structured temporal input by explicitly anchoring the video sequence between initial and current state images. Supported by the proposed PRIMO Dataset and Benchmark, extensive experiments across diverse in-domain environments and out-of-domain real-world humanoid scenarios demonstrate that PRIMO R1 achieves state-of-the-art performance. Quantitatively, our 7B model achieves a 50% reduction in the mean absolute error of specialized reasoning baselines, demonstrating significant relative accuracy improvements over 72B-scale general MLLMs. Furthermore, PRIMO R1 exhibits strong zero-shot generalization on difficult failure detection tasks. We establish state-of-the-art performance on RoboFail benchmark with 67.0% accuracy, surpassing closed-source models like OpenAI o1 by 6.0%.

</details>


### [174] [EAAE: Energy-Aware Autonomous Exploration for UAVs in Unknown 3D Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15604)
*Jacob Elskamp,Moji Shi,Leonard Bauersfeld,Davide Scaramuzza,Marija Popović*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出EAAE框架，将能量作为多旋翼无人机自主3D探索中的显式决策变量，通过能量预测和双层规划减少能耗


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统探索策略仅优化覆盖范围或时间，忽略了能量限制，导致能耗高的机动轨迹，限制了电池供电多旋翼无人机的任务性能

Method: 提出EAAE模块化前沿框架：1) 将前沿聚类为视图一致区域；2) 规划动态可行的候选轨迹；3) 使用离线功率估计循环预测执行能量；4) 通过最小化预测轨迹能量选择目标，同时通过双层规划架构保持探索进度

Result: 在模拟3D环境中评估，相比基于距离和基于信息增益的前沿基线方法，EAAE持续减少总能量消耗，同时保持竞争性的探索时间和可比的地图质量

Conclusion: EAAE为前沿探索提供了实用的即插即用能量感知层，将能量作为显式决策变量，在保持探索性能的同时显著降低能耗

Abstract: Battery-powered multirotor unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) can rapidly map unknown environments, but mission performance is often limited by energy rather than geometry alone. Standard exploration policies that optimise for coverage or time can therefore waste energy through manoeuvre-heavy trajectories. In this paper, we address energy-aware autonomous 3D exploration for multirotor UAVs in initially unknown environments. We propose Energy-Aware Autonomous Exploration (EAAE), a modular frontier-based framework that makes energy an explicit decision variable during frontier selection. EAAE clusters frontiers into view-consistent regions, plans dynamically feasible candidate trajectories to the most informative clusters, and predicts their execution energy using an offline power estimation loop. The next target is then selected by minimising predicted trajectory energy while preserving exploration progress through a dual-layer planning architecture for safe execution. We evaluate EAAE in a full exploration pipeline with a rotor-speed-based power model across simulated 3D environments of increasing complexity. Compared to representative distance-based and information gain-based frontier baselines, EAAE consistently reduces total energy consumption while maintaining competitive exploration time and comparable map quality, providing a practical drop-in energy-aware layer for frontier exploration.

</details>


### [175] [Perception-Aware Autonomous Exploration in Feature-Limited Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15605)
*Moji Shi,Rajitha de Silva,Hang Yu,Riccardo Polvara,Marija Popović*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种感知感知的层次化探索框架，将探索进度与特征可观测性显式耦合，通过特征质量评估和连续偏航优化来提升VIO性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主探索方法主要关注覆盖效率，但忽略了视觉惯性里程计(VIO)性能对鲁棒视觉特征的依赖。这可能导致机器人进入特征稀疏区域，造成跟踪退化、里程计漂移、地图损坏和任务失败

Method: 提出层次化感知感知探索框架：1) 使用全局特征地图评估候选前沿的预期特征质量，优先选择视觉信息丰富的子目标；2) 沿规划运动优化连续偏航轨迹以维持稳定的特征跟踪

Result: 在具有不同纹理水平的仿真环境和真实室内纹理稀疏环境中评估。相比忽略特征质量和/或不优化连续偏航的基线方法，本方法维持了更可靠的特征跟踪，减少了里程计漂移，在里程计误差超过阈值前平均实现了30%更高的覆盖率

Conclusion: 通过显式耦合探索进度与特征可观测性，提出的感知感知探索框架能够显著提升VIO性能，在特征稀疏环境中实现更可靠的自主探索

Abstract: Autonomous exploration in unknown environments typically relies on onboard state estimation for localisation and mapping. Existing exploration methods primarily maximise coverage efficiency, but often overlook that visual-inertial odometry (VIO) performance strongly depends on the availability of robust visual features. As a result, exploration policies can drive a robot into feature-sparse regions where tracking degrades, leading to odometry drift, corrupted maps, and mission failure. We propose a hierarchical perception-aware exploration framework for a stereo-equipped unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) that explicitly couples exploration progress with feature observability. Our approach (i) associates each candidate frontier with an expected feature quality using a global feature map, and prioritises visually informative subgoals, and (ii) optimises a continuous yaw trajectory along the planned motion to maintain stable feature tracks. We evaluate our method in simulation across environments with varying texture levels and in real-world indoor experiments with largely textureless walls. Compared to baselines that ignore feature quality and/or do not optimise continuous yaw, our method maintains more reliable feature tracking, reduces odometry drift, and achieves on average 30\% higher coverage before the odometry error exceeds specified thresholds.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [176] [Entropy Maximization and Weak Gibbsianity of Quasi-Free Fermionic States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14020)
*Vojkan Jakšić,Claude-Alain Pillet,Anna Szczepanek*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该研究解决了晶格费米子系统中熵最大化原理的唯一性和弱吉布斯性两个长期存在的问题，证明了在特定条件下熵最大化的规范不变准自由态是唯一的且具有弱吉布斯性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自1972年Lanford和Robinson研究平衡态方法以来，他们发现晶格费米子的规范不变准自由态在所有具有固定两点函数的平移不变态中最大化熵，但唯一性问题未解决。后续研究中，唯一性问题和这类准自由态是否为弱吉布斯态的问题重新出现，需要理论澄清。

Method: 研究在动量空间两点函数满足0<Ĉ(k)<1且属于布里渊区维纳代数的态类中进行。证明揭示了熵最大化原理和弱吉布斯性都直接来源于晶格费米子的热力学形式体系。

Result: 在该研究设定的条件下，熵最大化的规范不变准自由态是唯一的，并且这些态具有弱吉布斯性质，为正则回答了这两个长期存在的问题。

Conclusion: 晶格费米子系统的熵最大化原理和弱吉布斯性质可以通过热力学形式体系直接推导，为这类量子统计力学问题提供了统一的理论框架，解决了长期存在的理论疑问。

Abstract: In their 1972 study of approach to equilibrium, Lanford and Robinson showed that gauge-invariant quasi-free states of lattice fermions maximize entropy among all translation-invariant states with a fixed two-point function, and suggested that the maximizer is unique. In subsequent work on this topic, the uniqueness question re-emerged, together with the problem of whether such quasi-free states are weak Gibbs states. We provide a positive answer to both questions within a class of states whose momentum-space two-point function $\widehat C$ satisfies $0<\widehat C(k)<1$ and belongs to the Wiener algebra of the Brillouin zone. The proof reveals that both the entropy maximization principle and weak Gibbsianity follow directly from the thermodynamic formalism for lattice fermions.

</details>


### [177] [Spectral Analysis of a Quantum Waveguide with Elliptical Window](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14121)
*H. Najar,F. Chogle*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究椭圆窗口耦合的两个空间波导中的Dirichlet拉普拉斯算子，分析椭圆几何如何破坏旋转对称性并改变低能谱特性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非圆形（椭圆）耦合窗口如何影响波导系统的谱特性，特别是椭圆几何破坏旋转对称性后对模式耦合和离散谱的影响

Method: 采用数学分析方法证明椭圆窗口耦合波导中Dirichlet拉普拉斯算子的谱特性，包括有限离散特征值的存在性及其对椭圆几何参数的依赖关系，并进行数值模拟验证

Result: 证明了在本质谱阈值以下存在有限个离散特征值，椭圆几何导致特征值分裂等谱效应，数值模拟显示了第一特征值和基态随窗口几何的变化

Conclusion: 椭圆窗口耦合波导系统展现出与圆形情况不同的谱特性，几何各向异性显著影响模式耦合和低能谱结构，为波导设计提供了新的理论依据

Abstract: We investigate the Dirichlet Laplacian in two spatial waveguides coupled through an elliptic window. The elliptic geometry breaks rotational symmetry and introduces anisotropy through the semi-axes of the aperture, which modifies the coupling of transverse modes and the low-lying spectrum. We prove that the operator has a finite number of discrete eigenvalues below the threshold of the essential spectrum and study their dependence on the geometric parameters of the ellipse. In contrast to the circular case, the elliptic setting gives rise to spectral effects such as eigenvalue splitting. Numerical simulations illustrate the variation of the first eigenvalues and the ground state with the window geometry.

</details>


### [178] [A new model for the quantum mechanics of the Hydrogen atom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14969)
*Joseph Bernstein,Eyal Subag*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于四维洛伦兹二次空间锥体的氢原子新量子力学模型，使用代数微分算子表示可观测量，在Schwartz子空间中计算谱与物理谱一致


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建氢原子的新量子力学模型，旨在使用代数微分算子避免奇异性，通过锥体配置空间获得更大的几何对称群，并消除对特定边界条件的依赖

Method: 从四维洛伦兹二次空间(V,q)出发，以锥体C为配置空间，Hilbert空间H由锥体上的L^2函数构成，可观测量用代数微分算子代数D(C)表示，引入特殊的Schwartz子空间H^∞作为D(C)-模，Schrödinger算子表示为D(C)中的Schrödinger算子族

Result: 在Schwartz空间H^∞中计算Schrödinger算子族的谱，证明与物理谱完全一致，且H^∞中的解对应于通常的物理解

Conclusion: 该模型使用锥体配置空间获得O(3,1)对称性，仅使用无奇异性的代数算子，通过Schwartz空间编码所有边界条件，为氢原子提供了新的数学框架

Abstract: In this paper we introduce a new model for the quantum-mechanical system of the hydrogen atom.
  We start with a four-dimensional Lorentzian quadratic space $(V,q)$ and let $C \subset V$ be the corresponding cone.
  The Hilbert space of our model, denoted by $H$, consists of $L^2$ functions on the cone, and observables are represented by operators in the algebra $D(C)$ of algebraic differential operators on $C$. We introduce a distinguished Schwartz subspace $H^{\infty}$ of $H$ that is naturally a $D(C)$-module. The Schrödinger operator in our system is represented by a Schrödinger family of operators in $D(C)$.
  We compute the spectrum of the Schrödinger family in the Schwartz space $H^{\infty}$ and show that it coincides with the spectrum in physics, and that solutions in $H^{\infty}$ correspond to the usual solutions in physics.
  The main differences from the standard model are as follows. First, we use the cone $C$ instead of $\mathbb{R}^3$ as our configuration space. As a result, the group of geometric symmetries of our configuration space is $O(q)\simeq O(3,1)$ rather than $O(3)\ltimes \mathbb{R}^3$. Second, we use only algebraic operators with no singularities. Third, we do not impose any specific boundary conditions on solutions of our equations; these are all encoded in the Schwartz space $H^{\infty}$.

</details>


### [179] [The Zak phase in topologically insulating chains: invariants and quaternionic constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14995)
*Federico Manzoni,Domenico Monaco,Gabriele Peluso*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了具有时间反演、粒子-空穴和/或手性对称性的一维平移不变拓扑绝缘体中Zak相的拓扑内容，扩展了Monaco等人(2023)的结果，分析了Zak相在所有Altland-Zirnbauer-Cartan对称类中的拓扑捕获能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是深入理解Zak相在一维拓扑绝缘体中的拓扑内容，特别是在具有各种离散对称性（时间反演、粒子-空穴、手性对称）的系统中。需要扩展先前研究结果，系统分析Zak相在所有AZC对称类中的拓扑表征能力，并探索在具有四元数结构的对称类中Zak相的额外约束。

Method: 基于纤维化哈密顿量和谱投影框架，构造适应系统离散对称性的对称Bloch基。定义从阿贝尔Zak相获得的Z₂值拓扑不变量I^(AZC-class)(H)。分析具有四元数结构（反幺正对称性平方为负单位）的对称类中Zak相的额外约束条件。

Result: 证明了在具有四元数结构的对称类中，Zak相受到进一步约束，导致上述Z₂不变量的消失。这突显了Zak相对占据能态流形额外几何结构的敏感性。作为应用，讨论了具有任意有限程跳跃和单/多手性通道的广义Kitaev链情况。

Conclusion: 该研究系统分析了Zak相在所有AZC对称类中的拓扑表征能力，揭示了在具有四元数结构的对称类中Zak相的额外约束特性。通过构造对称Bloch基和定义Z₂拓扑不变量，为理解一维拓扑绝缘体的拓扑性质提供了新的理论框架，并展示了在广义Kitaev链等具体系统中的应用价值。

Abstract: In this work we investigate the topological content of the Zak phase in one-dimensional translation-invariant topological insulators endowed with time-reversal, particle-hole and/or chiral symmetries, extending results from \cite{Monaco_2023}. We analyze the extent to which the Zak phase captures the topology of all Altland--Zirnbauer--Cartan (AZC) symmetry classes in $1$D. Building on the framework of fibered Hamiltonians and spectral projections, we construct symmetric Bloch bases adapted to the discrete symmetries of the system and define a $\mathbb{Z}_2$-valued topological invariant $\mathrm{I}^{(\mathrm{AZC-class})}(H)$ obtained from the abelian Zak phase. Moreover, we demonstrate that in symmetry classes admitting a quaternionic structure, i.e. anti-unitary symmetries squaring to minus the identity, the Zak phase is further constrained, leading to the vanishing of the $\mathbb{Z}_2$ invariant mentioned above. This highlights the sensitivity of the Zak phase to additional geometric structures of the manifold of occupied energy states. As an application, we discuss the case of generalized Kitaev chains with arbitrary finite-range hopping and single or multiple chiral channels.

</details>


### [180] [On uniform large genus asymptotics of Witten's intersection numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15233)
*Jindong Guo,Di Yang,Don Zagier*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了模空间上原始psi类相交数的大亏格渐近行为，给出了统一渐近公式，并扩展到包含零点插入的情况，应用于Painlevé I方程的形式解，同时给出了多项式猜想的新证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究稳定代数曲线模空间上原始psi类相交数的大亏格渐近行为，扩展相关结果，并将其应用于Painlevé I方程，同时为多项式猜想提供新证明。

Method: 基于文献[14]的思想，给出原始psi类相交数的统一大亏格渐近公式，以特定统一方式扩展到包含零点插入的情况，并应用[14]的方法证明多项式猜想。

Result: 获得了原始psi类相交数的统一大亏格渐近公式，成功扩展到包含零点插入的情况，给出了Painlevé I方程特定形式解的应用，并为多项式猜想提供了新证明。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了psi类相交数的大亏格渐近统一理论，扩展了相关结果的应用范围，为Painlevé I方程提供了新见解，并通过新方法验证了多项式猜想的正确性。

Abstract: Following ideas from [14], we give a uniform large genus asymptotics for primitive psi-class intersection numbers on the moduli space of stable algebraic curves, and extend this result including insertions of zeros in a certain uniform way. Application to a particular formal solution of the Painlevé I equation is given. We also use a method from [14] to give a new proof of the polynomiality conjecture on large genus asymptotic expansions of psi-class intersection numbers.

</details>


### [181] [On Csanyi's and Arias' Functional for Ground States Energy of Multi-Particle Fermion Systems: Asymptotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15301)
*Heinz Siedentop*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: Csanyi和Arias的约化单粒子密度矩阵能量泛函被证明在Müller泛函和Hartree-Fock泛函之间，利用此性质推导出该泛函基态能量的渐近展开，与量子能量展开至三阶一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Csanyi和Arias提出的约化单粒子密度矩阵能量泛函的性质，特别是其与已知泛函（Müller泛函和Hartree-Fock泛函）的关系，并验证其在量子化学计算中的准确性。

Method: 通过数学分析证明Csanyi-Arias泛函的下界由Müller泛函给出，上界由Hartree-Fock泛函给出。利用这一界限关系，推导该泛函基态能量的渐近展开，并与量子能量的渐近展开进行比较。

Result: 证明了Csanyi-Arias泛函满足：Müller泛函 ≤ Csanyi-Arias泛函 ≤ Hartree-Fock泛函。基于此界限，推导出的基态能量渐近展开与量子能量的渐近展开在前三阶完全一致。

Conclusion: Csanyi-Arias泛函在Müller泛函和Hartree-Fock泛函之间提供了一个有理论保证的能量估计，其基态能量展开至三阶与精确量子结果相符，表明该泛函在量子化学计算中具有良好理论基础和准确性。

Abstract: We show that Csanyi's and Arias' energy functional of the reduced one-particle density matrix is bounded from below by the Müller functional and bounded from above by the Hartree-Fock functional. We use this fact to derive an asymptotic expansion of the ground state energy of this functional which agrees with the quantum energy to third order.

</details>


### [182] [Ruijsenaars-van Diejen-Takemura Hamiltonians as rational Heun operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15595)
*Satoshi Tsujimoto,Luc Vinet,Alexei Zhedanov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: Ruijsenaars-van Diejen-Takemova Hamiltonians被表征为Heun算子，即具有在Askey-Wilson格上极点有理函数提升作用的二阶q-差分算子


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立Ruijsenaars-van Diejen-Takemura Hamiltonians与Heun算子之间的精确对应关系，揭示其作为二阶q-差分算子的代数结构

Method: 将Hamiltonians定义为Heun算子，研究其在Askey-Wilson格上有理函数空间上的作用，特别是提升作用性质

Result: 证明了最一般的Ruijsenaars-van Diejen-Takemura Hamiltonians可精确表征为具有特定提升作用的Heun算子

Conclusion: 该工作建立了可积系统与特殊函数理论之间的深刻联系，为理解这些Hamiltonians的代数结构提供了新视角

Abstract: The most general Ruijsenaars-van Diejen-Takemura Hamiltonians are characterized as Heun operators defined as second order $q$-difference operators with a raising action on elementary rational functions with poles on the Askey-Wilson grid.

</details>

### [183] [Hierarchical Latent Space Item Response Model for Analyzing Mental Health Vulnerability of Elementary School Students in South Korea](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13677)
*Soyeon Park,Seoyoung Shin,Minjeong Jeon,Hyoun Kyoung Kim,Ick Hoon Jin*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 提出分层潜在空间项目反应模型（HLSIRM），用于分析韩国小学生心理健康数据，识别学校特定脆弱性模式，并生成统一交互地图以指导针对性干预策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 韩国小学生心理健康问题日益严重，但现有分析工具难以从项目反应数据中识别学校特定的脆弱性模式，需要更精细的分析方法来指导针对性干预。

Method: 提出分层潜在空间项目反应模型（HLSIRM），该模型包含分层受访者效应和带符号的受访者-项目关联的内积潜在交互，生成统一交互地图分离学校/个体主效应与学校/个体-项目交互。

Result: 应用于仁川35所学校2210名小学生的心理健康数据，通过方向相似性聚类识别出四个经验性脆弱性领域；学校层面分析显示缺乏咨询经验是主要脆弱性领域，而压力、抑郁和智能手机依赖集中在特定学校；校内分析展示了个体学生在交互地图中的位置如何转化为针对学校特定需求的干预策略。

Conclusion: HLSIRM提供了一种统一框架来分析心理健康脆弱性数据，能够识别学校和个体层面的特定模式，为制定针对性干预策略提供数据支持，有助于解决韩国小学生心理健康这一公共卫生问题。

Abstract: Mental health difficulties among elementary school students represent a growing public health concern in South Korea, yet analytical tools for identifying school-specific vulnerability patterns from item response data remain limited. We propose the hierarchical latent space item response model (HLSIRM), which adds hierarchical respondent effects and an inner-product latent interaction for signed respondent-item associations, yielding a unified interaction map that separates school, individual main effects from school/individual-item interactions. We apply HLSIRM to mental health vulnerability data from 2,210 elementary school students across 35 schools in Incheon, South Korea. Clustering item vectors by directional similarity identifies four empirically derived vulnerability domains. School-level analysis reveals that the absence of counseling experience is the primary vulnerability domain aligned with most school vectors, while stress, depression, and smartphone dependency concentrate in specific schools. Within-school analysis demonstrates how individual student positions in the interaction map translate into targeted intervention strategies that address school-specific needs.

</details>


### [184] [When Does Agroforestry Income Reduce Deforestation? Evidence from a Natural Experiment in Madagascar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13706)
*Camille DeSisto,Ranaivo Rasolofoson,Michelle Foley,Harsh Parikh*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 利用2015年全球香草价格飙升作为外生收入冲击，研究发现收入增加在农业机会成本低的地区减少森林砍伐，但在农业潜力高的地区反而增加砍伐


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热带森林砍伐与农村贫困密切相关，但收入对森林损失的因果效应难以识别。研究旨在通过外生收入冲击来厘清收入变化对森林砍伐的因果影响方向

Method: 采用匹配增强合成控制设计，利用2015年全球香草价格飙升（由食品行业转向天然调味料引发）作为外生收入冲击，分析马达加斯加主要香草产区

Result: 收入增加使2017年年均森林砍伐率降低1.7个百分点（约701公顷避免的森林损失）。但效应存在强烈异质性：在较干燥、交通便利的市镇减少砍伐，而在较湿润、低海拔、农业潜力高的地区反而增加砍伐

Conclusion: 收入同时缓解了依赖森林的生存压力，并在农业回报高的地区提高了保护的机会成本。基于商品的农林业可以在农业机会成本低的条件下协调减贫与森林保护，但政策必须预见收入增加在农业适宜地区可能加剧森林砍伐的情况

Abstract: Tropical deforestation and rural poverty are deeply intertwined, yet isolating the causal effect of income on forest loss remains challenging. We use the 2015 global vanilla price boom, triggered by food-industry shifts toward natural flavoring, as an exogenous income shock affecting Madagascar's primary vanilla-producing region. Using a matching-augmented synthetic control design, we estimate that income gains reduced annual deforestation by 1.7 percentage points in 2017, equivalent to approximately 701 hectares of avoided forest loss. Under a monotonicity assumption linking the price boom to farmers' income, the sign of this reduced-form effect is informative about the causal direction of income on deforestation. However, effects were strongly heterogeneous: higher incomes reduced deforestation in drier, more accessible municipalities but increased clearing in wetter, low-elevation areas with high agricultural potential. These divergent patterns suggest that income simultaneously relaxes subsistence pressures driving forest dependence and raises the opportunity cost of conservation where agricultural returns are high. Our findings indicate that commodity-based agroforestry can align poverty alleviation with forest conservation under conditions of low agricultural opportunity cost. Still, policies must anticipate contexts where rising incomes amplify deforestation in agriculturally suitable land. The strategic targeting of livelihood interventions based on local agricultural potential may help reconcile development and conservation objectives in tropical forest frontiers.

</details>


### [185] [Prediction-based Inference in Electronic Health Record (EHR)-linked Biobanks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14356)
*Xingran Chen,Cheng-Han Yang,Zhenke Wu,Bhramar Mukherjee*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 该论文系统评估了在电子健康记录(EHR)关联生物样本库数据中，基于预测的(PB)方法处理生物标志物缺失值的效果，发现PB方法在满足缺失假设时能显著提高统计功效，但需要比完全案例分析(CCA)更强的假设来控制I类错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录(EHR)关联生物样本库数据中的生物标志物存在异质性缺失，当这些标志物作为表型进行分析时，缺失可能导致分析偏差。虽然最近提出的基于预测的(PB)方法通过机器学习模型填补缺失值来提高关联分析的统计功效和估计精度，但在临床信息性观察过程下，这些方法是否仍然优于传统方法尚不清楚，缺乏对现有PB方法在真实EHR缺失机制下的全面比较评估和理论理解。

Method: 1. 对8种最近开发的PB方法进行结构化综述，并根据其框架进行分类；2. 在10种不同的结果观察过程模型下，系统评估9种方法（包括4种PB方法和5种传统缺失数据方法的基线方法），针对连续和二元结果在不同缺失假设下进行评估；3. 提供理论结果来刻画CCA或PB方法可能保持有效的场景；4. 在"All of Us"数据中应用CCA、加权CCA和两种PB方法进行六种实验室生物标志物的全基因组关联研究(GWAS)。

Result: 1. PB方法在其缺失假设成立时能显著提高统计功效和估计效率；2. 但PB方法需要比CCA更强的假设来控制I类错误；3. 理论分析揭示了CCA或PB方法保持有效的具体场景；4. 在"All of Us"数据应用中，PB方法能够复制已知的遗传关联，同时相对于(加权)CCA提高了效率，并将推断结果扩展到更具代表性的研究人群。

Conclusion: 基于预测的方法在电子健康记录关联生物样本库数据分析中具有潜力，能够提高统计功效和效率，但需要仔细考虑其缺失假设。当PB方法的假设成立时，它们可以显著改善关联分析，但研究者需要权衡统计功效提升与I类错误控制之间的平衡。实际应用中，PB方法能够扩展推断结果到更广泛的人群，提高研究的代表性。

Abstract: Electronic health record (EHR)-linked biobank data facilitate large-scale scientific discoveries such as genome-wide association study (GWAS) on a multitude of phenotypic traits and biomarkers routinely captured in EHR. However, heterogeneous missingness in biomarkers may bias analyses when used as phenotypes. Recently proposed prediction-based (PB) inference methods incorporate external machine ML models to impute missing biomarkers and thereby improve the statistical power and estimation precision in association analyses. Yet, it remains unclear that if these methods are still preferable when the outcome $Y$ is generated under a clinically informative observation process. A comprehensive comparative evaluations and theoretical understanding of the existing PB methods under such realistic EHR missingness mechanisms are lacking. In this paper, we conduct a structured review of 8 recently developed PB methods and categorize them based on their frameworks. We systematically evaluate 9 methods, including 4 PB methods and 5 baseline methods from the traditional missing-data approaches, under 10 different outcome observation process models across different missing assumptions for continuous and binary outcomes. Our results show that PB methods can substantially improve statistical power and estimation efficiency when their missingness assumptions hold, but they may require stronger assumptions than CCA to control type I error. We provide theoretical results to characterize the scenarios under which CCA or PB methods may remain valid. Finally, we apply CCA, weighted CCA, and two PB methods to perform GWAS of six laboratory biomarkers in the All of Us data. The results demonstrate that PB methods can replicate known genetic associations while improving efficiency relative to (weighted) CCA. Furthermore, they extend inferential results to a more representative study population.

</details>


### [186] [Risk-Calibrated Process Capability Approval with Finite Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14479)
*Fei Jiang,Lei Yang*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于风险校准的过程能力指数决策框架，通过考虑估计不确定性和非对称操作损失，改进了传统的确定性阈值规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的过程能力指数（如C_pk）决策通常基于确定性阈值规则，但由于样本估计存在不确定性，当真实能力接近批准阈值时，决策具有随机性。现有方法未能充分考虑估计不确定性和非对称操作损失。

Method: 将能力批准问题建模为二元统计决策问题，提出风险校准决策规则：Ĉ_pk ≥ C_0 + k·SE(Ĉ_pk)。校准常数k可通过可容忍失败概率或误接受/误拒绝成本比确定。该框架统一了确定性阈值、置信下限和概率基批准规则，并扩展到基于非对称操作损失的成本敏感决策规则。

Result: 仿真实验和工业案例研究表明：风险校准主要影响接近阈值的决策，提高了批准稳定性，当误接受成本高于误拒绝成本时，能显著降低预期操作损失。

Conclusion: 提出的风险校准决策框架为过程能力批准提供了更稳健的方法，通过显式考虑估计不确定性和非对称操作损失，改进了传统决策规则，特别适用于真实能力接近批准阈值的情况。

Abstract: Process capability indices such as $C_{pk}$ are widely used in manufacturing to support supplier qualification, pilot-build release, and production approval. In practice, approval decisions are often based on deterministic threshold rules of the form $\widehat{C}_{pk} \ge C_0$. Because $\widehat{C}_{pk}$ is estimated from finite samples, however, such decisions are inherently stochastic, especially when the true capability lies near the approval threshold. This paper develops a risk-calibrated decision framework for process capability approval that explicitly accounts for estimation uncertainty and asymmetric operational loss. Capability approval is formulated as a binary statistical decision problem, leading to a rule of the form $\widehat{C}_{pk} \ge C_0 + k\,SE(\widehat{C}_{pk})$, where the calibration constant $k$ is determined either by a tolerable failure probability or by a false-accept/false-reject cost ratio. The resulting formulation unifies several commonly used procedures, including deterministic thresholding, lower confidence bound rules, and probability-based approval rules, and naturally extends them to cost-sensitive decision rules derived from asymmetric operational loss. Simulation experiments and an industrial case study show that risk calibration primarily affects near-threshold decisions, improves approval stability, and can substantially reduce expected operational loss when false acceptance is more costly than false rejection.

</details>


### [187] [Genetic Algorithms in Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14801)
*Mo Li,QiQi Lu,Robert Lund,Xueheng Shi*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: GAReg是一个统一的遗传算法包，用于处理离散优化回归问题，在标准算法不适用时表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多统计问题涉及对具有未知维度的离散参数空间进行优化。在这些情况下，基于梯度的方法经常失败，因为目标函数不可微分，或者搜索空间非凸或巨大，目标函数具有许多局部极值。

Method: GAReg提供紧凑的染色体表示，支持回归样条的最优节点放置、最佳子集回归变量选择及相关问题。该包允许均匀初始化、保持约束的交叉和变异、稳态替换，以及可选的海岛模型并行化。

Result: GAReg能够高效搜索高维模型空间，在穷举枚举或整数/动态规划方法不可行的情况下提供接近最优的解决方案。

Conclusion: GAReg为离散优化回归问题提供了一个有效的遗传算法解决方案，特别适用于标准算法不适用或失败的情况。

Abstract: Many statistical problems involve optimization over a discrete parameter space having an unknown dimension. In such settings, gradient-based methods often fail due to the non-differentiability of the objective function or a non-convex or massive search space with an objective function having many local maxima/minima. This paper presents GAReg, a unified genetic algorithm package that handles discrete optimization regression problems, which works well when standard algorithms are unjustified. GAReg provides a compact chromosome representation supporting optimal knot placement for regression splines, best-subset regression variable selection, and related problems. The package allows for uniform initialization, constraint-preserving crossover and mutation, steady-state replacement, and an optional island-model parallelization. GAReg efficiently searches high-dimensional model spaces, providing near-optimal solutions in settings where exhaustive enumeration or integer or dynamic programming approaches are infeasible.

</details>


### [188] [vPET-ABC: Fast Voxelwise Approximate Bayesian Inference for Kinetic Modeling in PET](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14859)
*Qinlin Gu,Gaelle M. Emvalomenos,Evan D. Morris,Clara Grazian,Steven R. Meikle*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 提出vPET-ABC方法，用于全身PET动力学建模的体素级贝叶斯推断，无需似然函数，通过前向模拟和差异测试实现快速、可扩展的不确定性量化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全身PET数据量大，传统贝叶斯方法（如MCMC）计算不可行，而深度学习方法需要重新训练和验证，且不保证推理速度提升，需要一种快速、可扩展、模型无关的体素级不确定性量化方法

Method: 提出向量化体素级近似贝叶斯计算（vPET-ABC），这是一种无似然、模型无关的后验推断框架，用前向模拟和差异测试替代显式似然评估，通过完全向量化将体素级推断转化为适合现代GPU的并行工作负载

Result: 在模拟中，vPET-ABC的后验摘要与顺序蒙特卡洛基线差异小，后验均值估计比非负最小二乘法更准确；在模型选择中，vPET-ABC在高噪声下保持高灵敏度，而NNLS+BIC几乎零灵敏度；在人类吸烟数据中，vPET-ABC产生更密集的概率激活图；在50分钟全身FDG研究中，在单GPU上快速生成高质量Ki参数图像，比NNLS更好地保持局部空间相关性

Conclusion: vPET-ABC提供快速、无需训练、考虑不确定性的推断方法，可扩展到全身PET，且在不同示踪剂和动力学模型间保持可移植性

Abstract: Dynamic PET kinetic modeling increasingly demands voxelwise uncertainty quantification and robust model selection. Yet total-body PET (TB-PET) data volumes make conventional Bayesian approaches, such as per-voxel MCMC, computationally impractical, while deep models typically require retraining and careful revalidation when tracers, protocols, or kinetic models change, without necessarily improving inference speed.
  Vectorized voxelwise approximate Bayesian computation (vPET-ABC) is introduced as a likelihood-free, model-agnostic posterior inference framework for dynamic PET kinetic modeling at total-body scale. The method replaces explicit likelihood evaluation with forward simulations and a discrepancy test, then exploits full vectorization to transform voxelwise inference into an embarrassingly parallel workload suited to modern GPUs.
  In simulation, vPET-ABC produced posterior summaries with small divergence from sequential Monte Carlo baselines, and posterior mean estimates significantly more accurate than non-negative least squares (NNLS). For model selection between the linear parametric neurotransmitter model (lp-ntPET) and the multilinear reference tissue model, vPET-ABC maintained high sensitivity under high noise with moderate loss of specificity, whereas NNLS+Bayesian information criteria exhibited the opposite trade-off with near-zero sensitivity. In a human cigarette smoking dataset, vPET-ABC yielded denser probabilistic activation maps than lp-ntPET with effective number of parameters. On a 50 min total-body [18F]FDG study, vPET-ABC generated high quality whole volume K_i parametric images within practical runtimes on a single GPU, while also preserved local spatial correlation better than NNLS.
  Overall, vPET-ABC delivers fast, training-free, uncertainty-aware inference that scales to TB-PET and remains portable across tracers and kinetic models.

</details>


### [189] [Benchmarking Formula 1 results using a normal model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15192)
*John Fry,Silvio Fanzon,Mark Austin,Tom Brighton*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 使用单变量和双变量正态模型量化F1赛车中车手和车队层面的表现预期，区分精英与非精英车队


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: F1赛车中长期存在区分技能与运气影响的需求，特别是区分车辆水平和车手水平的影响。目前四支精英车队主导F1，过去四年赢得所有主要比赛，需要量化车手和车队层面的合理表现预期。

Method: 采用单变量和双变量正态模型来量化车手和车队层面的表现预期，区分精英与非精英车队，并以2025赛季完整数据作为应用案例。

Result: 论文展示了如何应用该方法量化F1赛车中车手和车队层面的表现预期，区分精英与非精英车队的差异。

Conclusion: 提出的统计方法能够有效区分F1赛车中的车手技能和车辆性能影响，为评估车手和车队表现提供了量化框架。

Abstract: There is enduring interest in disentangling the effects of skill and luck in sport. A key issue in Formula 1 is distinguishing between car-level and driver-level effects. Four elite teams currently dominate Formula 1 and have won every major race for the last four years. In this paper we use univariate and bivariate normal models to quantify reasonable performance expectations at both driver and team levels, distinguishing between elite and non-elite teams. We illustrate our approach with an application to the last fully completed 2025 season.

</details>


<div id='hep-ex'></div>

# hep-ex [[Back]](#toc)

### [190] [Measurement of the $t$-channel single top quark cross section in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 5.02 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13592)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: CMS首次在5.02 TeV能量下测量了单顶夸克t道产生截面，测得tq+反tq联合截面为25.4 pb，并提取了CKM矩阵元|V_tb|值


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LHC的5.02 TeV能量下首次测量单顶夸克t道产生截面，验证标准模型预测，并提取Cabibbo-Kobayashi-Maskawa矩阵元|V_tb|，这对理解顶夸克与弱相互作用耦合至关重要

Method: 使用CMS探测器在2017年采集的302 pb⁻¹数据，分析包含一个电子或μ子以及至少两个喷注的事件，其中至少一个喷注被识别为来自b夸克碎裂。通过t道单顶夸克产生过程进行测量

Result: 测得tq+反tq联合截面为25.4^{+3.6}_{-3.5}(统计)^{+4.2}_{-3.9}(系统)±0.5(亮度) pb；tq截面为17.6^{+2.8}_{-2.7}(统计)^{+2.6}_{-2.4}(系统)±0.3(亮度) pb；反tq截面为6.6^{+2.4}_{-1.6}(统计)^{+2.1}_{-2.5}(系统)±0.1(亮度) pb；截面比为2.7^{+1.5}_{-0.8}(统计)^{+1.3}_{-0.3}(系统)；CKM矩阵元|f_LV V_tb| = 0.92 ± 0.09(实验) ± 0.01(理论)

Conclusion: 测量结果与量子色动力学次次领头阶精度的标准模型预测良好一致，首次在5.02 TeV能量下完成了CMS对单顶夸克t道产生过程的测量，为顶夸克物理提供了重要数据

Abstract: The single top quark $t$-channel production cross section is measured in proton-proton collisions at the CERN LHC at $\sqrt{s}$ = 5.02 TeV, using data recorded with the CMS detector in 2017, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 302 pb$^{-1}$, and resulting in the first CMS measurement of the process at that energy. Events with one electron or muon and two or more jets, among which at least one is identified as originating from a b quark fragmentation, are analyzed. The combined cross section of single top quark (tq) and single top antiquark ($\mathrm{\bar{t}q}$) production is $σ_{\mathrm{tq+\bar{t}q}}$ = 25.4$^{+3.6}_{-3.5}$ (stat) $^{+4.2}_{-3.9}$ (syst) $\pm$ 0.5 (lumi) pb. The individual cross sections are measured to be $σ_{\mathrm{tq}}$ = 17.6$^{+2.8}_{-2.7}$ (stat) $^{+2.6}_{-2.4}$ (syst) $\pm$ 0.3 (lumi) pb and $σ_{\mathrm{\bar{t}q}}$ = 6.6$^{+2.4}_{-1.6}$ (stat) $^{+2.1}_{-2.5}$ (syst) $\pm$ 0.1 (lumi) pb. Their ratio is measured to be $\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{t-ch}}$ = 2.7$^{+1.5}_{-0.8}$ (stat) $^{+1.3}_{-0.3}$(syst). The absolute value of the Cabibbo$-$Kobayashi$-$Maskawa matrix element is found to be $\lvert f_{\mathrm{LV}}V_\mathrm{tb}\rvert$ = 0.92 $\pm$ 0.09 (exp) $\pm$ 0.01 (thy). The measurements are in good agreement with the standard model predictions at next-to-next-to-leading order accuracy in quantum chromodynamics.

</details>

TL;DR: MicroBooNE实验测量了电子中微子在氩上的不带π介子末态的电荷交换截面，发现与多数模型在轻子运动学上吻合，但在强子系统特别是质子角度建模上存在差异


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 使用MicroBooNE探测器收集的完整Booster中微子束数据集（1.3×10²¹质子打靶），分析有无质子超过动能可见阈值的两种事件，提取质子和电子运动学的微分截面，包括能量和相对于中微子束方向的角度，并探索质子与电子方向之间的角度关系

Result: 在轻子运动学方面与大多数模型表现出良好一致性，但在强子系统建模特别是质子角度方面存在一些差异，表明现有模型在描述强子系统方面需要改进

Conclusion: 该测量为电子中微子-氩相互作用提供了重要的截面数据，揭示了当前模型在强子系统描述上的局限性，对改进中微子相互作用模型和未来中微子实验设计具有重要意义

Abstract: We present a new measurement of the electron neutrino charged current cross section on argon without pions in the final state. This measurement uses the full MicroBooNE Booster Neutrino Beam dataset of $1.3\times 10^{21}$ protons on target collected at Fermi National Accelerator Laboratory. Events are considered both with and without protons above the kinetic energy visibility threshold. Differential cross sections are extracted in proton and electron kinematics, including energy and angle relative to the neutrino beam direction. The relationship between the hadronic and leptonic systems is explored through the angle between the proton and electron directions. The resulting cross sections are compared to a variety of available generator predictions using different models of neutrino interactions. We find good agreement with most models in lepton kinematics and some discrepancies in the hadronic system modeling, particularly in proton angle.

</details>


### [192] [AMD Versal AI-Engines for fixed latency environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13852)
*Ioannis Xiotidis,Noah Clarke Hall,Tianjia Du,Nikos Konstantinidis,David Miller*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该论文研究了AMD Xilinx Versal架构中的AI Engine（AIE）协处理器在高能物理实验等固定延迟环境中的机器学习应用部署能力，评估了BDT和CNN向量化实现的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能物理实验等复杂高吞吐量数据处理系统需要将模式识别和数据压缩算法部署到传感器附近，而多芯片封装中的专用协处理器为此提供了解决方案。

Method: 对AMD Xilinx Versal架构中的AI Engine（AIE）协处理器进行技术研究，重点关注其在固定延迟环境中的部署能力，实现了Boosted Decision Tree（BDT）和Convolutional Neural Network（CNN）的向量化实现。

Result: 评估了AIE在机器学习应用中的性能表现，证明了其在固定延迟环境（如大型强子对撞机实验）中部署的可行性，可作为传统可编程逻辑实现的替代方案。

Conclusion: AI Engine协处理器能够有效支持高能物理实验等固定延迟环境中的机器学习应用部署，为传感器近端数据处理提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: Complex, high-throughput data acquisition and processing systems, such as those used in high-energy physics experiments, are increasingly moving sophisticated pattern recognition and data compression algorithms closer to the sensors themselves. To meet these needs, programmable device manufacturers offer multi-silicon die packages that commonly include dedicated co-processors within the same package. We present a technical study of a new family of such co-processors from AMD Xilinx, the Adaptive Intelligence (AI) Engine, or AIE, as part of the Versal architecture. Specifically, we focus on the deployment capabilities of AIEs in fixed latency environments such as those typically found in colliding beam experiments like those at the Large Hadron Collider. We evaluate the performance of a vectorised implementation of both a Boosted Decision Tree (BDT) and a Convolutional Neural Network (CNN), thereby demonstrating the feasibility of deploying AIEs for ML applications in such environments and their use as possible alternatives to traditional programmable logic-based implementations.

</details>
