### [225] [Critical slowing down and bulk viscosity in binary neutron star mergers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13474)
*Jamie M. Karthein,Maneesha Sushama Pradeep,Rachel Steinhorst*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 中子星并合模拟中，QCD临界点附近的临界动力学可显著增强体粘滞，与弱相互作用贡献相当，可能影响并合演化观测特征


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统中子星并合模拟假设强相互作用、弱相互作用和流体动力学时间尺度清晰分离，其中弱Urca过程是最慢的微观过程，决定体粘滞耗散。但这一耗散机制层次可能在物质轨迹通过低温QCD临界点附近时被根本改变。

Method: 分析QCD临界点附近增强的密度涨落导致的临界减速和输运系数（特别是体粘滞）快速增长。考虑有限时间效应、有限尺寸效应和流体动力学尺度分离的破坏对关联长度的限制，评估QCD临界点对体粘滞的贡献。

Result: QCD临界点对体粘滞的贡献在现实条件下可与电弱贡献相当，临界动力学可能在流体动力学演化中留下可观测的印记。

Conclusion: 中子星并合中物质轨迹通过QCD临界点附近时，临界动力学可显著改变体粘滞耗散机制，这一效应可能影响并合演化的观测特征，需要在模拟中予以考虑。

Abstract: Hydrodynamic simulations of neutron star mergers rely on the clear separation between the strong-interaction, weak-interaction, and hydrodynamic timescales. In this effective framework, weak Urca interactions are typically the slowest microscopic processes, and therefore the Urca rate determines the bulk-viscous dissipation. This assumed hierarchy of dissipative mechanisms can be decisively altered, without invalidating hydrodynamics, if the trajectory of the matter in a neutron star merger passes through the vicinity of a possible low temperature QCD critical point. The enhanced density fluctuations lead to critical slowing down and rapid growth of transport coefficients including bulk viscosity. While this growth is regulated by finite-time effects, finite-size effects, and the breakdown of hydrodynamic scale separation, which bound the correlation length, we demonstrate that the QCD contribution to bulk viscosity can rival the electroweak contribution in realistic conditions. Thus, critical dynamics could leave observable imprints on the hydrodynamic evolution of neutron star mergers.

</details>


### [226] [Implications of a Cosmogenic Origin of KM3-230213A for Ultra-High-Energy Protons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13476)
*A. R. Alhebsi,Arjen van Vliet,Domenik Ehlert,Satyendra Thoudam*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: KM3NeT观测到72PeV-2.6EeV高能中微子事件，用于约束超高能宇宙线质子源参数。研究发现：仅KM3NeT数据支持强演化的质子源（如高光度活动星系核），但加入Pierre Auger和IceCube的零观测结果后则不支持强演化。UHECR的质子分数在20EeV处约为20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用KM3NeT观测到的超高能中微子事件（KM3-230213A）来约束超高能宇宙线（UHECR）质子源的现象学参数。该中微子事件若解释为宇宙线起源，可为UHECR源模型提供重要限制。

Method: 采用两成分UHECR模型：混合成分群体和次主导的UHE质子群体。通过拟合UHECR能谱和成分数据，结合多信使约束（包括KM3NeT单中微子事件），获得模型参数的68%置信水平限制。对比仅使用KM3NeT数据与加入Pierre Auger和IceCube零观测结果两种情况。

Result: 1. 仅KM3NeT曝光要求强演化的UHE质子源，与高光度活动星系核一致；2. 加入Pierre Auger和IceCube零观测结果后，强演化被排除；3. UHECR的质子分数在20EeV处主要受成分数据约束，约为20%。

Conclusion: 单中微子事件KM3-230213A对UHECR质子源演化提供了重要约束。仅KM3NeT数据支持强演化源，但多观测台联合分析表明实际演化可能较弱。UHECR中质子成分在最高能区占比有限，约为20%。

Abstract: A significant neutrino event with an estimated energy between $72\,\mathrm{PeV}$ and $2.6\,\mathrm{EeV}$ was recently observed by the KM3NeT experiment (KM3-230213A). When interpreted as cosmogenic in origin, this event can provide constraints on several phenomenological parameters of UHE proton sources. In this study, we present the best fit to the spectrum and composition of UHECRs that is consistent with multi-messenger constraints, including the detection of a single neutrino event by the KM3NeT detector in the energy range of KM3-230213A. From the best fit, we obtain the 68\% CL constraints on the parameters of a two-population model of UHECRs, comprising a mixed-composition population and a subdominant UHE proton population. Our results indicate that the detection of a single neutrino event in the energy range of KM3-230213A solely with the KM3NeT exposure requires strongly evolving UHE proton sources, consistent with high-luminosity active galactic nuclei. On the other hand, including the null observations from the Pierre Auger and IceCube observatories disfavors such strong evolution. In both cases, the observed proton fraction of UHECRs is primarily constrained by the composition data to be $\sim 20\%$ at $20\,\mathrm{EeV}$.

</details>


### [227] [Distinguishing Kilonovae from Binary Neutron Star and Neutron Star-Black Hole Mergers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13484)
*Ish Gupta,Yugesh Bhoge,Rahul Kashyap,Mukul Bhattacharya*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 通过模拟双中子星（BNS）和中子星-黑洞（NSBH）合并的千新星光变曲线，发现峰值后不同波段的光变演化可用于区分这两种前身星类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来千新星事件可能在没有引力波信号的情况下被发现，因此需要仅凭光变曲线来区分BNS和NSBH合并事件，以确定前身星类型。

Method: 使用模拟的BNS和NSBH种群，结合半解析光变曲线模型，比较它们在光学ugrizy波段峰值后的演化特征。

Result: 在峰值后2天的u波段，BNS千新星仅下降约1星等，而NSBH千新星下降≥3星等；在峰值后10天的i波段，趋势反转，NSBH演化更慢。这种差异源于抛射物质量、不透明度和扩散时标的不同。

Conclusion: 尽管定量重叠程度依赖于模型，但定性差异在各种模型变化中持续存在，表明峰值后光变下降可作为区分BNS和NSBH合并的有效诊断工具。

Abstract: Kilonovae from compact binary mergers are most informative when accompanied by a gravitational-wave signal, which can help identify the source as a binary neutron star (BNS) or a neutron star-black hole (NSBH) merger. However, future events will also be discovered serendipitously or through follow-up of other transients, without a confident identification of the progenitor. Hence, we ask whether the kilonova light curve alone can distinguish between these two progenitor channels. Using simulated BNS and NSBH populations together with semi-analytic light curve models, we compare their post-peak evolution across the optical $ugrizy$ bands. The strongest contrast appears in the blue $u$ band 2 days after peak and in the redder $i$ band 10 days after peak. In the $u$ band, typical BNS kilonovae decline by only $\sim 1$ mag within 2 days of peak, whereas NSBH kilonovae typically decline by $\gtrsim 3$ mag over the same interval. In the $i$ band, the trend reverses for most of the population, with NSBH kilonovae evolving more slowly than BNS kilonovae. We attribute this behavior to differences in ejecta mass, opacity, and diffusion timescale between the two merger classes. Although the quantitative overlap is model-dependent, the qualitative distinction persists across model variations, identifying post-peak decline to be a viable diagnostic for inferring whether the source was a BNS or an NSBH merger.

</details>


### [228] [Pulsar timing arrays: the emerging gravitational-wave landscape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13643)
*C. M. F. Mingarelli,J. A. Casey-Clyde,Y. T. Chang,E. Eisenberg,F. Hutchison,N. Khusid,B. Larsen,A. Moran,F. Semenzato,L. Willson,Q. Zheng*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 脉冲星计时阵列(PTA)实验已进入新纪元，探测到纳赫兹引力波背景(GWB)。本文综述了探测物理、噪声模型和互相关技术，讨论了天体物理意义、多信使连续引力波搜索、新物理探测潜力，并展望了未来发展方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着PTA实验首次探测到纳赫兹引力波背景，需要全面综述该领域的物理基础、技术方法、天体物理意义以及未来发展方向，为研究界提供系统的知识框架和前沿展望。

Method: 采用综述方法，详细描述PTA探测的物理原理、噪声建模、互相关技术以分离Hellings-Downs曲线；介绍多信使连续引力波搜索框架、目标搜索活动和严格探测协议；分析各向异性搜索中的小尺度泄漏偏差问题以及确定性信号与GWB和脉冲星噪声的分离方法。

Result: 当前振幅与标准合并模型之间的感知张力通过超大质量黑洞双星种群的新见解得到解决；建立了完整的PTA探测和分析框架；识别了关键技术挑战并提出了解决方案；展望了SKAO和DSA-2000等未来设施带来的灵敏度提升。

Conclusion: PTA领域已进入成熟阶段，纳赫兹引力波背景的探测开启了宇宙学研究新窗口。通过改进数据处理、噪声建模和信号分离技术，结合未来大型射电望远镜的灵敏度提升，该领域将在天体物理、宇宙学和新物理探测方面取得突破性进展。

Abstract: Pulsar Timing Array (PTA) experiments have entered a new era with evidence for a nanoHertz gravitational wave background (GWB). This review describes the physics of detection, detailing the noise models and cross-correlation techniques required to isolate the Hellings-Downs curve. We discuss astrophysical implications, arguing that the perceived tension between current amplitudes and standard merger models is largely resolved by new insights into supermassive black hole binary populations. Beyond the stochastic background, we review the framework for multi-messenger continuous gravitational-wave searches, highlighting targeted search campaigns and rigorous detection protocols. We also examine the potential to probe New Physics, including cosmic strings and ultralight dark matter. Critical challenges are addressed, including small-scale leakage bias in anisotropy searches and the separation of deterministic signals from the GWB and pulsar noise. Finally, we outline the field's future, from rapid data combination strategies to the sensitivity gains expected from the Square Kilometre Array Observatory (SKAO) and DSA-2000.

</details>


### [229] [Ultra-compact X-ray Binaries: A Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13813)
*Bo Wang,Dongdong Liu,Yunlang Guo,Hailiang Chen,Wencong Chen,Zhanwen Han*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 综述了超致密X射线双星的形成通道、演化理论、观测特征及其在引力波天文学等领域的意义


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超致密X射线双星作为轨道周期极短的低质量X射线双星，在引力波探测、多信使天文学等领域具有重要意义，但其形成和演化机制仍存在很大不确定性，需要系统梳理现有理论和观测进展

Method: 综述性研究方法，系统回顾了四种经典形成通道（白矮星供体、氦星供体、演化主序星供体、吸积诱导坍缩通道），比较了观测样本与理论预期，分析了供体化学组成、质量转移率等约束条件

Result: 总结了各形成通道的演化情景、初始参数空间和关联天体，提供了观测UCXBs与理论预期的对比，讨论了黑洞-UCXB候选体的观测现状，指出UCXBs的起源可通过供体化学组成、质量转移率-轨道周期图等约束

Conclusion: UCXBs是未来空间引力波探测的关键目标，其形成机制可通过多波段观测、更多样本发现和详细理论模拟进一步约束，对引力波天文学、多信使天文学、双星演化和极端条件下中子星物理研究具有重要意义

Abstract: Ultra-compact X-ray binaries (UCXBs) are a subclass of low-mass X-ray binaries (LMXBs) characterized by ultra-short orbital periods, typically less than $60-80\,$min. They consist of a compact mass-accretor and a hydrogen-poor mass-donor, in which the mass-accretor could be a neuron star (NS) or even a black hole (BH). UCXBs play an important role in multiple areas of astrophysics. In particular, they are considered strong, continuous gravitational wave (GW) sources in the low-frequency band, making them key targets for future space-based GW observatories such as LISA, TianQin and Taiji. As the most compact binaries, the formation and evolution of UCXBs remain highly uncertain. In this article, we review four classic formation channels: the white dwarf donor channel, the He star donor channel, the evolved main-sequence donor channel, and the accretion-induced collapse channel. We also discuss recent progress in these channels, covering evolutionary scenarios, the initial parameter space for UCXB formation, and associated objects. A comparison between observed UCXBs and theoretical expectations is provided, along with a discussion on the observed BH-UCXB candidates. The origin of UCXBs can be constrained by the chemical composition of mass-donors and their locations in diagrams of mass-transfer rate and X-ray luminosity versus orbital period. We also examine the implications of UCXBs for several astrophysical fields, including GW astronomy, multi-messenger astronomy, binary evolution, and NS physics under extreme conditions. Further progress will depend on multi-wavelength observations, the discovery of more UCXB samples, and more detailed theoretical simulations.

</details>


### [230] [Comparative analysis of BL Lacertae in flaring and non-flaring states: timing and spectral studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14036)
*A. Priyana Noel,Alicja Wierzcholska,Raj Prince*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 利用XMM-Newton观测数据研究BL Lacertae在2020-2021年耀发期间的X射线时变和谱特性，发现高变异性、最短时标1.24ks、软变亮趋势，谱能用断幂律最佳拟合，断点能量与流量正相关，表明同步辐射峰随亮度增加向X射线波段移动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BL Lacertae作为耀变体，具有高流量变异性，其宽带耀发的物理起源尚不明确。该源在2020年7月至2021年底处于持续耀发状态，为研究不同流量状态下的光谱和时变特性提供了理想机会。

Method: 分析五组XMM-Newton EPIC观测数据，使用时变分析方法（分数变异性、最小变异性时标、离散相关函数），对两个最易变的观测进行详细光谱建模，包括软硬波段相关性分析，采用单幂律、对数抛物线和断幂律模型拟合光谱。

Result: 五组观测中有两组显示高变异性（F_var=19.16±0.32和6.27±0.43），2021年观测对应最高流量状态。0.3-10 keV波段最短变异性时标为1.24 ks，在磁能与辐射粒子能量均分假设下对应磁场强度B≈0.4 G。发现软变亮光谱趋势，断幂律模型在多数情况下提供最佳拟合，断点能量与流量呈强正相关。部分观测显示热黑体成分温度与流量正相关。

Conclusion: BL Lacertae在耀发期间表现出典型的软变亮行为，光谱断点（解释为同步辐射冷却断点）随流量增加向高能移动，表明同步辐射峰随源亮度增加进入或穿过X射线波段，这支持了同步辐射主导X射线发射的物理图像。

Abstract: BL Lacertae is a blazar known for its high flux variability and occasional broadband flares of unknown origin. It was in an extended flaring state from July 2020 until the end of 2021, making it an ideal candidate to study spectral and temporal properties during different flux states. We analysed five XMM--Newton EPIC observations of BL Lacertae taken up to the end of 2021. Temporal properties were investigated using fractional variability, minimum variability timescale, and the discrete correlation function. Detailed spectral modeling was performed on the two most variable observations, including correlation analysis between the soft (0.3--2.0 keV) and hard (2.0--10.0 keV) bands. Two of five observations were found to be highly variable with $F_{\mathrm{var}} = 19.16 \pm 0.32$ and $6.27 \pm 0.43$. The 2021 observation corresponds to the highest flux state. The shortest variability timescale in the 0.3--10 keV band is 1.24 ks. Assuming synchrotron-dominated X-ray emission, this timescale constrains the emission region size. Under equipartition between the magnetic field and radiating particles, this implies $B \approx 0.4\,\mathrm{G}$. A softer-when-brighter spectral trend was found, as commonly seen in blazars. Spectra were modeled with single power-law, log-parabola, and broken power-law models; the broken power-law gave the best fit by Akaike Information Criterion in most cases, with a strong break energy--flux correlation. A thermal blackbody component showed a positive temperature--flux correlation in some observations. The spectral break, interpreted as the synchrotron cooling break, shifts to higher energies with increasing flux. The source consistently showed softer-when-brighter behavior. Only one observation showed significant soft--hard band correlation. The data suggest the synchrotron peak moves into or across the X-ray band as the source brightens.

</details>


### [231] [Evidence for Atomic Absorption Features in the High Resolution X-ray Spectrum of the Neutron Star in Puppis A](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14146)
*John Groger,Frits Paerels,Slavko Bogdanov,Eric V. Gotthelf,David J. Helfand,Ivan Hubeny,Thierry Lanz,Thomas A. Gomez*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 首次通过X射线光谱分析获得中子星质量和半径的纯光谱学估计


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究超新星遗迹Puppis A中的中子星RX J0822-4300，通过高分辨率X射线光谱分析其表面物理特性，特别是获取中子星质量和半径的光谱学估计

Method: 使用高分辨率4-30 Å X射线光谱观测，通过与大气模型计算比较，识别红移和压力展宽的氧和氖离子跃迁，同时通过线性和二次塞曼效应确认表面磁场强度

Result: 检测到高度电离氧和氖的原子吸收线，确认了表面磁偶极场强度，首次通过纯光谱学方法推导出中子星的引力红移和表面重力加速度，从而得到其半径和质量估计

Conclusion: 成功实现了对中子星RX J0822-4300的首次纯光谱学参数测定，为理解中子星物理特性提供了新的观测方法

Abstract: We present evidence for atomic absorption lines in the high-resolution 4-30 A X-ray spectrum of the neutron star RX J0822-4300 in the supernova remnant Puppis A. Comparison with model atmosphere calculations shows that features in the observed spectrum can be uniquely associated with redshifted and pressure-broadened transitions in highly ionized oxygen and neon. We also spectroscopically confirm the previously estimated strength of the surface magnetic dipole field; we detect both the linear and the quadratic Zeeman effect. We derive values for both the gravitational redshift and the acceleration of gravity at the stellar surface, yielding the first purely spectroscopic estimates for the radius and mass of a neutron star.

</details>


### [232] [Reconnection-driven State Transitions in Flat Spectrum Radio Quasars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14305)
*Agniva Roychowdhury*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该研究将Roychowdhury (2026)关于FSRQs对数流量偏度变化的工作扩展到18个FSRQ样本，发现可分为三类：大耀斑后偏度持续降低、不降低、变化不显著。使用Fermo等(2010)的统计等离子体模型进行模拟，成功复现观测到的偏度变化p值分布，并发现耀斑导致系统熵减少，表明有序度增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展先前关于FSRQs对数流量偏度变化的研究，从单个源扩展到18个源的样本，以更系统地理解大GeV耀斑对流量分布偏度的影响，并为观测结果提供理论解释。

Method: 使用Fermo等(2010)的统计等离子体模型进行1500次模拟运行，通过等离子体合并产生大等离子体，当从重联事件获得能量且多普勒对齐时产生大耀斑。将模拟下采样到18次以匹配观测样本大小，计算偏度变化、p值分布、集合香农熵和功率谱密度。

Result: 1) 观测样本可分为三类：偏度持续降低(37%)、不降低(37%)、变化不显著(26%)；2) 模拟成功复现观测的偏度变化p值分布；3) 在偏度增加或减少的组中，熵以3σ水平减少，表明耀斑增加系统有序度；4) 模拟光变曲线功率谱密度为断裂幂律，符合已知耀变体变异性特征；5) 结果对多个基准参数200-300%的变化具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 耀变体喷流内的随机等离子体模拟能够自洽地复现耀变体GeV光变曲线的关键可观测统计特性，特别是大耀斑对流量分布偏度的影响以及系统有序度的增加，为耀变体耀斑的统计特性提供了理论框架。

Abstract: We extend the work of Roychowdhury (2026) on skewness variations of the logarithmic flux, driven by large GeV flares in FSRQs, to a sample of 18 FSRQs. We find that they can be categorized into three groups, one where the skewness attains a persistent lower value after a large flare, one where it does not, and those where change in skewness is not significant. To provide a theoretical ground for these results, we use the statistical plasmoid model of Fermo et al. (2010) that self-consistently produces large plasmoids through merging which, when gain energy from the reconnection event and are Doppler aligned, produce large flares. We find that a downsampling of our simulation of 1500 runs to 18 statistically reproduces the observed distribution in p-values for change in skewness. We further compute the ensemble Shannon entropy of the system and the skewness, where the entropy is found to decrease at a $3σ$ level in both the groups where skewness either increases or decreases, as a direct evidence of increase in order in the system caused by a flare. We find that the power spectral densities of the simulated light curves are broken-power-laws, resembling a white noise+red noise broken by the typical cooling timescale in our system, in accordance with known blazar variability. We find that our results are robust to a $200-300\%$ change in several fiducial parameters of the simulation. Our stochastic simulation of plasmoids inside a blazar jet self-consistently reproduces key observable statistical properties of blazar GeV light curves.

</details>


### [233] [Cosmic ray mass composition measurement in the energy range from $10^{16.5}$ eV to $10^{18.5}$ eV observed with the TALE hybrid detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14804)
*Telescope Array Collaboration,R. U. Abbasi,T. Abu-Zayyad,M. Allen,J. W. Belz,D. R. Bergman,F. Bradfield,I. Buckland,W. Campbell,B. G. Cheon,K. Endo,A. Fedynitch,T. Fujii,K. Fujisue,K. Fujita,M. Fukushima,G. Furlich,A. Gálvez Ureña,Z. Gerber,N. Globus,T. Hanaoka,W. Hanlon,N. Hayashida,H. He,K. Hibino,R. Higuchi,D. Ikeda,D. Ivanov,S. Jeong,C. C. H. Jui,K. Kadota,F. Kakimoto,O. Kalashev,K. Kasahara,Y. Kawachi,K. Kawata,I. Kharuk,E. Kido,H. B. Kim,J. H. Kim,J. H. Kim,S. W. Kim,R. Kobo,I. Komae,K. Komatsu,K. Komori,A. Korochkin,C. Koyama,M. Kudenko,M. Kuroiwa,Y. Kusumori,M. Kuznetsov,Y. J. Kwon,K. H. Lee,M. J. Lee,B. Lubsandorzhiev,J. P. Lundquist,H. Matsushita,A. Matsuzawa,J. A. Matthews,J. N. Matthews,K. Mizuno,M. Mori,S. Nagataki,K. Nakagawa,M. Nakahara,H. Nakamura,T. Nakamura,T. Nakayama,Y. Nakayama,K. Nakazawa,T. Nonaka,S. Ogio,H. Ohoka,N. Okazaki,M. Onishi,A. Oshima,H. Oshima,S. Ozawa,I. H. Park,K. Y. Park,M. Potts,M. Przybylak,M. S. Pshirkov,J. Remington,C. Rott,G. I. Rubtsov,D. Ryu,H. Sagawa,N. Sakaki,R. Sakamoto,T. Sako,N. Sakurai,S. Sakurai,D. Sato,K. Sekino,T. Shibata,J. Shikita,H. Shimodaira,H. S. Shin,K. Shinozaki,J. D. Smith,P. Sokolsky,B. T. Stokes,T. A. Stroman,H. Tachibana,K. Takahashi,M. Takeda,R. Takeishi,A. Taketa,M. Takita,Y. Tameda,K. Tanaka,M. Tanaka,M. Teramoto,S. B. Thomas,G. B. Thomson,P. Tinyakov,I. Tkachev,T. Tomida,S. Troitsky,Y. Tsunesada,S. Udo,F. R. Urban,M. Vrábel,D. Warren,K. Yamazaki,Y. Zhezher,Z. Zundel,J. Zvirzdin*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: TALE混合探测器测量宇宙线成分，发现平均Xmax的伸长率在10^17 eV附近存在转折，表明银河系与河外宇宙线源在此能量附近发生转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究宇宙线质量成分在10^16.5-10^18.5 eV能量范围内的变化，特别是探索银河系与河外宇宙线源之间的过渡区域（第二膝附近）。

Method: 使用TALE混合探测器（荧光探测器+表面探测器阵列）收集5年数据，分析Xmax分布，并与使用QGSJet II-04强子相互作用模型的质子、氦、氮、铁初级宇宙线的蒙特卡洛模拟进行比较。

Result: 平均Xmax的伸长率在约10^17 eV处出现转折：在此能量以下，成分逐渐变重（重核比例增加，轻初级粒子比例下降）；在此能量以上，质子比例随能量显著增加。

Conclusion: 观测结果表明在第二膝附近存在从银河系到河外宇宙线源的过渡，这为理解宇宙线起源和传播机制提供了重要线索。

Abstract: We report on the cosmic ray mass composition measured by the Telescope Array Low-energy Extension (TALE) hybrid detector. The TALE detector consists of a fluorescence detector (FD) station with 10 FD telescopes located at the Telescope Array (TA) Middle Drum FD Station (itself made up of 14 FD telescopes), and a surface detector (SD) array of scintillators. The array consists of 40 SDs with 400 m spacing and 40 SDs with 600 m spacing. In this paper, we present results on the measurement of the depth of shower maxima ($X_\mathrm{max}$) in the energy range from $10^{16.5}$ eV to $10^{18.5}$ eV collected over five years of the TALE hybrid detector. The $X_\mathrm{max}$ distributions were analyzed and compared with Monte Carlo simulations of proton, helium, nitrogen, and iron primaries, using the QGSJet II-04 hadronic interaction model. Our results indicate that the elongation rate of the mean $X_\mathrm{max}$, which is defined as the slope of $\langle X_\mathrm{max} \rangle$ versus cosmic ray energy, exhibits a break around $10^{17}$ eV. Up to this energy, the composition becomes increasingly heavy, characterized by a growing dominance of heavy nuclei and a steadily decreasing fraction of light primaries. Beyond this energy, the proton fraction increases significantly with energy. These findings suggest a transition from Galactic to extra-Galactic cosmic ray sources around the so-called second knee.

</details>


### [234] [Constraining Spin and Inclination Angle of XTE J2012+381 using AstroSat and NICER](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14834)
*Vaibhav Sharma,Ranjeev Misra,Shivani Chaudhary,J S Yadav,Pankaj Jain*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 对黑洞X射线双星XTE J2012+381在2022年爆发期间的光谱分析显示，反射-康普顿化模型最佳拟合数据，黑洞自旋参数主要位于高自旋区间(0.67-0.998)，而吸积盘倾角在多数参数组合下稳定在50°-65°之间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究黑洞X射线双星XTE J2012+381在2022年爆发期间的光谱特性，特别是在黑洞质量、源距离和光谱硬化因子等参数不确定性条件下，探索黑洞自旋和吸积盘倾角的稳定性。

Method: 使用NICER和AstroSat(LAXPC20和SXT)观测数据，提取0.7-10.0 keV能量谱。采用反射-康普顿化模型进行拟合，系统性地改变黑洞质量(7.26、11、16.5 M⊙)、源距离(3.3、5.4、7.5 kpc)和光谱硬化因子(1.5、1.7、1.9)等参数，分析这些参数变化对推断的光谱参数稳定性的影响。

Result: 在大多数参数组合下，黑洞自旋解一致位于高自旋区间，范围在~0.67到~0.998之间，仅少数配置支持较低自旋。吸积盘倾角在探索的参数空间内约束良好，通常范围在~50°到~65°之间，只有少数参数组合产生更高的倾角值。

Conclusion: XTE J2012+381的黑洞自旋参数主要表现出高自旋特性，而吸积盘倾角相对稳定，表明即使在黑洞质量和距离等参数存在不确定性的情况下，这些关键物理参数的推断结果仍然具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: We present a spectral analysis of a black hole X-ray binary XTE J2012+381 during its 2022 outburst, using data from NICER and AstroSat. Combining data from NICER, LAXPC20, and SXT, we extract energy spectra covering the 0.7-10.0 keV range. We model the energy spectra using a series of physical models and find that a reflection-Comptonization model provides the best fit. Given the uncertainties in the black hole mass and source distance, we investigate the stability of the inferred spectral parameters by systematically varying the black hole mass (7.26, 11, and 16.5 M$_\odot$), source distance (3.3, 5.4, and 7.5 kpc), and spectral hardening factor (1.5, 1.7, and 1.9). We find that, across most combinations of these parameters, the spin solutions consistently lie in the high-spin regime, spanning values between $\sim$0.67 and $\sim$0.998, with only a limited subset of configurations favoring lower spins. In contrast, the disk inclination angle remains well constrained over the majority of the explored parameter space, typically ranging between $\sim$50° and $\sim$65°. Only a few parameter combinations yield higher inclination values.

</details>


### [235] [Spectral Variations of $γ$-rays in Mrk 421](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14896)
*Rui-Qi Huang,Xin-Ke Hu,Yu-Wei Yu,Ji-Shun Lian,Jin Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 基于17年Fermi-LAT观测数据对Mrk 421的γ射线能谱变化进行综合分析，发现显著的光变和谱变，提出复杂积分谱形源于不同流量状态下谱形的叠加，GeV-TeV联合谱分析挑战单区轻子模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Mrk 421在γ射线波段的能谱变化特性，特别是其光变与谱变的关系，以及通过GeV-TeV联合谱分析探讨辐射起源，检验传统单区轻子模型的适用性。

Method: 使用17年Fermi-LAT观测数据，分析0.1-1000 GeV波段光变曲线（14天时间分档），研究日级和数日级时间分辨谱，生成与存档TeV观测同时的GeV谱，构建GeV-TeV联合谱，分析流量和谱指数的统计分布。

Result: 发现显著光变（>5σ）伴随谱变（越亮越硬），日流量变化可达一个量级；17年积分谱需复杂模型，但时间分辨谱可用幂律拟合；GeV-TeV联合谱显示GeV和TeV辐射可能起源不同；流量服从对数正态分布，谱指数可用对数正态或高斯函数拟合。

Conclusion: Mrk 421的复杂γ射线谱形源于不同流量状态下谱形的叠加，GeV和TeV辐射可能具有不同起源，挑战了传统单区轻子模型，为理解该源的辐射机制提供了新线索。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive analysis of the 17-year Fermi-LAT observational data of Mrk 421 to investigate the spectral variations in the $γ$-ray bands. The light curve of the source in the 0.1--1000 GeV band with a 14-day time bin exhibits significant variability at a confidence level exceeding 5$σ$, which is accompanied by spectral variation, displaying a {\it harder-when-brighter} behavior. Moreover, its flux variation can reach up to one order of magnitude within one day, with a daily flux up to $(1.19\pm0.84)\times10^{-8}~{\rm erg~cm^{-2}~s^{-1}}$ on MJD 56152. The 17-year integrated spectrum of Mrk 421 necessitates a complex model for explanation, whereas its time-resolved spectra over one-day or several-day time intervals can be well fitted by a power-law model. We propose that the complex spectral shape of the 17-year integrated spectrum stems from the superposition of different spectral shapes in different flux states. By generating the GeV spectra that are simultaneously observed with the archived TeV observations and constructing the combined GeV--TeV spectra, we find that some combined GeV--TeV spectral shapes clearly imply different radiation origins for the GeV and TeV emissions, challenging the one-zone leptonic model. It is found that the flux follows a lognormal distribution, while the photon spectral index distributions can be well fitted by either a lognormal or a Gaussian functions. The possible nature of the $γ$-ray variability in Mrk 421 is discussed.

</details>


### [236] [Investigating the Temporal Evolution of Gamma-Ray Burst Central Engine Parameters Based on Numerical Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15028)
*Wei-Hua Lei*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该论文使用GRMHD HARM-COOL代码模拟了磁化超吸积黑洞模型，研究了伽马射线暴中心引擎的时间演化特性，特别是中微子湮灭功率和Blandford-Znajek功率的演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超吸积恒星质量黑洞被认为是伽马射线暴的中心引擎候选者。将中心引擎模型的预测与伽马射线暴的时间行为进行比较具有重要意义，这有助于理解伽马射线暴的复杂光谱行为。

Method: 使用开源GRMHD HARM-COOL代码，在固定弯曲时空背景下演化多个2D磁化超吸积黑洞模型，采用真实状态方程。扩展代码以包含中微子湮灭功率计算，研究黑洞中心引擎参数的时间演化。

Result: 中微子功率与先前解析结果基本一致。中微子湮灭过程倾向于产生热"火球"，而BZ喷流则是坡印廷通量主导的。初始磁化参数σ₀的演化特征可能有助于理解伽马射线暴的复杂光谱行为。

Conclusion: 通过数值模拟获得了超吸积黑洞中心引擎参数的时间演化特性，特别是中微子湮灭功率和Blandford-Znajek功率的演化，这些结果有助于解释伽马射线暴观测到的复杂光谱行为。

Abstract: A hyperaccreting stellar-mass black hole (BH) has been proposed as the candidate central engine of gamma-ray bursts (GRBs). Comparing the predictions from the central engine models with the temporal behavior of GRBs is of great interest. In this paper, using the open-source GRMHD HARM-COOL code, we evolve several 2D magnetized hyperaccreting BH models with realistic equation of state in a fixed curved space-time background. We extend the code to include the calculation of neutrino annihilation power. We then study the time evolution of BH central engine parameters, i.e., the neutrino annihilation power, the Blandford-Znajke (BZ) power, and the initial magnetization $σ_0$. We find that the neutrino power is generally consistent with previous analytical results. Usually, the neutrino annihilation process tends to launch a thermal ``fireball'', while the BZ jet is Poynting-flux-dominated. Our results, especially the evolution characteristics of $σ_0$ may help to understand the complex GRB spectral behavior.

</details>


### [237] [Do the sources of the 511 keV excess explain the anomalous CMZ ionization?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15114)
*Pedro De la Torre Luque,Francesca Calore*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 研究探讨511keV正电子注入能否解释银河系中心分子区异常电离率，发现正电子注入虽能产生比以往候选源更强的电离，但仍不足以完全解释观测到的异常电离水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 银河系中心分子区观测到异常高的电离率，远超出宇宙射线能解释的范围，且空间分布均匀。511keV过剩信号可能与这种异常电离相关，研究旨在验证正电子注入假设能否解释这一现象。

Method: 基于511keV观测数据，构建符合恒星核球分布的正电子源模型（放射性核素或脉冲星），计算MeV正电子注入速率，模拟其在CMZ中产生的电离效应，并与观测电离水平对比。

Result: 正电子注入产生的电离效应比以往研究的任何候选源都强，但仍不足以完全解释CMZ观测到的异常电离率，无法单独解释电离异常现象。

Conclusion: 虽然正电子注入能产生显著电离效应，但仅凭此机制无法解释银河系中心分子区的异常电离现象，需要探索其他机制或多种机制共同作用。

Abstract: The anomalous rate of molecules ionization observed at the Central Molecular zone (CMZ) challenges known mechanisms of ionization observed in molecular clouds across the Galaxy, due to the exceptionally high levels of ionization measured (orders of magnitude above what cosmic rays can explain) and its uniform spatial distribution within the CMZ. Recent studies suggest that the source of the $511$~keV excess can be correlated with this anomalous ionization rate or contribute significantly to the ionization in the Galactic Centre (GC). One of the leading hypotheses attributes the $511$~keV signal to positron injection from radionuclides or pulsars distributed following the stellar bulge, which is rather flat around the GC and, hence, could help explaining the uniform ionization profile. In this work, we investigate whether such a population of sources, injecting MeV positrons at rates consistent with the $511$~keV observations, can account for the ionization levels and distribution observed in the CMZ. Our results indicate that positron injection alone falls short at explaining the anomaly, although their expected ionization is larger than expected from any previously studied candidates.

</details>


### [238] [Informational corrections to the early-Universe radiation sector: CET Omega, WIMP freeze-out, and implications for a possible 20 GeV gamma-ray excess](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15205)
*Christian Balfagon*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该论文研究Fermi-LAT伽马射线过剩信号对CET Omega理论的影响，该理论预测早期宇宙辐射能量密度的双重对数修正，影响暗物质热冻结过程，导致遗迹丰度和伽马射线形态的微小修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fermi-LAT观测到的伽马射线过剩信号可能源于暗物质湮灭，这为探测早期宇宙膨胀率的非标准修正提供了直接探针。作者旨在研究这一信号对CET Omega理论的启示，该理论是相对论量子场论和宇宙学的信息模块化扩展。

Method: 从模块化两点函数推导修正项，论证信息扇区相关起始尺度，通过数值解计算其对冻结过程的影响。分析信息场Φ_Ω(x)的演化，证明其在冻结前"冻结进入"并在引力对流下存活至今。与早期暗能量、kination和变化N_eff模型进行比较。

Result: 修正导致遗迹丰度的百分比水平偏移和伽马射线通量形态的亚百分比修正。参数范围10^{-4} < α_log < 10^{-2}与Planck、BBN和BAO约束一致，同时预测下一代MeV-GeV任务可观测的伽马射线形态偏差。

Conclusion: CET Omega理论预测的宇宙学修正与现有观测约束兼容，并为Fermi-LAT伽马射线过剩信号提供可能的理论解释框架，其预测的形态修正可通过未来伽马射线观测进行检验。

Abstract: Recent analyses of Fermi-LAT data have identified a nearly spherical, halo-like excess of gamma rays peaking at E_gamma ~ 20 GeV. If interpreted as dark matter annihilation, the excess directly probes the thermal freeze-out epoch and therefore any non-standard corrections to the early-Universe expansion rate. In this work we examine the implications of this tentative signal for CET Omega, an informational and modular extension of relativistic quantum field theory and cosmology. CET Omega predicts a universal state-dependent modification to the radiation energy density of the early Universe, characterized by a doubly logarithmic correction originating from renormalized modular fluctuations in the spectral triple of the theory. The correction is negligible during Big Bang nucleosynthesis and recombination but becomes relevant during thermal WIMP freeze-out. We derive the correction from the modular two-point function, justify the onset scale associated with the informational sector, and compute its quantitative impact on freeze-out through numerical solutions. We also analyze the evolution of the informational field Phi_Omega(x) and show that it freezes in before the freeze-out epoch and survives to the present time under gravitational advection. The resulting modification induces percent-level shifts in the relic abundance and sub-percent morphological corrections to the annihilation gamma-ray flux. We compare the scenario with Early Dark Energy, kination, and varying N_eff models, and show that the parameter range 10^{-4} < alpha_log < 10^{-2} remains consistent with Planck, BBN, and BAO constraints while predicting potentially observable deviations in the gamma-ray morphology accessible to next-generation MeV-GeV missions.

</details>


### [239] [Cosmic rays: constraints from future MeV detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15266)
*Giada Peron,Sarah Recchia,Stefano Menchiari,Alexandre Marcowith,Andrei Bykov,Martin Pohl,Elena Amato*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文综述了银河系宇宙射线的当前理解，特别关注低能（MeV-GeV）宇宙射线的重要性，并探讨了利用未来MeV伽马射线任务研究宇宙射线物理性质的新前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙射线在银河系演化中扮演关键角色，特别是低能宇宙射线因其丰度高、电离截面大，对星际介质动力学和化学过程（如外流启动、恒星和行星形成）至关重要。然而，对银河系宇宙射线的全面理解仍需通过多波段观测来完善。

Method: 文章首先回顾了直接测量获得的银河系宇宙射线当前理解，然后系统介绍了宇宙射线的直接可观测量、加速过程以及在银河系盘中传播的模型。接着概述了当前伽马射线观测的现状，最后提出了利用未来MeV任务观测能力研究宇宙射线物理性质的新方法。

Result: 文章综合了当前对银河系宇宙射线的认识，包括其能谱、成分、加速机制和传播特性。特别强调了伽马射线天文学作为研究宇宙射线的重要工具，因为高能光子是宇宙射线与星际等离子体相互作用的主要产物。未来MeV任务将提供更精确的观测数据来约束宇宙射线模型。

Conclusion: 银河系宇宙射线研究是一个多学科交叉领域，结合了直接测量、理论模型和伽马射线观测。未来MeV伽马射线任务将开启研究宇宙射线物理性质的新时代，特别是在低能区域，这将深化我们对宇宙射线在银河系演化中作用的理解，并为星际介质动力学和天体化学过程提供关键见解。

Abstract: Cosmic rays are charged energetic particles that permeate the interstellar medium. Their sizeable energy share and penetration power makes them essential players in the dynamical and chemical processes that rule Galactic evolution, such as the launching of outflows and the formation of star and planets. For these processes low-energy (MeV-GeV) CRs are particularly important, both because they are the most abundant and because they have the largest cross-section for ionization. The study of cosmic rays naturally connects with gamma-ray astronomy, as high-energy photons are the principal products of their interaction with the interstellar plasma. In this article, after reviewing our current understanding of Galactic cosmic rays as derived from direct measurements, we present the state of the art regarding Galactic cosmic rays covering their direct observables, their acceleration processes and models for their propagation in the Galactic Disk. We present then an excursus on the current state of gamma-ray observations, and propose new prospects for investigating the physical properties of Galactic cosmic rays, exploiting the observational capability of future MeV missions.

</details>


### [240] [The hidden population of long gamma-ray bursts from compact object mergers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15531)
*R. Maccary,C. Guidorzi,L. Amati,M. Bulla,S. Kobayashi,M. Maistrello,A. Rossi,G. Stratta,A. Tsvetkova*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该研究通过分析Fermi/GBM伽马暴目录，识别出具有特定时间特性的长时标致密天体并合候选体，这些候选体在Ep,i-Eiso关系上存在异常，且最小变时标更符合致密天体并合而非塌缩星伽马暴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRB 230307A和其他长时标致密天体并合候选体的光变曲线显示出独特的确定性演化特征，研究者希望寻找更多具有类似时间特性的未识别长时标致密天体并合候选体，以区分致密天体并合与塌缩星伽马暴。

Method: 1. 筛选Fermi/GBM目录中至少包含8个光变峰值的伽马暴；2. 分析候选体的时间和光谱特性；3. 在未知红移情况下，分析候选体在Ep,i-Eiso平面上的轨迹；4. 评估候选体与长伽马暴Ep,i-Eiso关系的兼容性；5. 计算最小变时标并与已知致密天体并合和塌缩星群体比较。

Result: 识别出9个未知红移的致密天体并合候选体，其中至少2个在Ep,i-Eiso关系上存在3.1σ置信水平的异常值；这些候选体的最小变时标更符合致密天体并合群体而非塌缩星伽马暴。

Conclusion: 特定时间特性可作为区分长时标致密天体并合与塌缩星伽马暴的诊断工具；未识别的致密天体并合在长伽马暴中的比例可能高于先前估计。

Abstract: Context. The prompt-emission time profiles of GRB 230307A and other long-duration compact object merger (COM) candidates exhibit a unique set of temporal properties, characterised by a deterministic evolution of waiting times and pulse widths.
  Aims. We searched the Fermi/GBM catalogue for other unidentified long COM candidates exhibiting temporal properties similar to those observed in GRB 230307A.
  Methods. We examined the temporal and spectral prompt-emission properties of GRBs featuring at least eight light-curve peaks. For candidates, all with unknown redshifts, that exhibited properties similar to GRB 230307A, we analysed their trajectories in the Ep,i-Eiso plane as a function of redshift. We then evaluated the joint likelihood of their compatibility with the Ep,i-Eiso relation satisfied by the bulk of long GRBs. Furthermore, we calculated their minimum variability timescales (MVTs) for comparison against known COM and collapsar populations.
  Results. We identified 9 COM candidates with unknown redshifts and demonstrated that there are at least two outliers of the Ep,i-Eiso relation with 3.1 sigma (Gaussian) confidence level. Furthermore, their MVTs are more consistent with those of COM than with collapsar GRBs.
  Conclusions. These results indicate that this specific set of temporal properties can serve as a diagnostic tool to distinguish long-duration COMs from the broader collapsar population. Furthermore, our findings suggest that the fraction of unidentified COMs among long GRBs may be larger than previously assumed.

</details>


### [241] [An extreme particle accelerator powered by PSR J1849-0001](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15537)
*The LHAASO Collaboration*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: LHAASO发现与PSR J1849-0001脉冲星风星云相关的超高能伽马射线源，其PeV光度比蟹状星云高数倍，尽管脉冲星自转功率仅为蟹状星云的1/50，挑战了现有粒子加速理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究脉冲星风星云作为极端粒子加速器的物理机制，特别是探索低自转功率脉冲星如何产生超高能伽马射线辐射，这对理解粒子加速效率和磁流体动力学条件具有重要意义。

Method: 使用LHAASO观测站探测超高能（>100 TeV）伽马射线源，结合X射线观测数据，通过光谱分析和磁约束计算，评估粒子加速效率和磁场条件。

Result: 发现与PSR J1849-0001相关的点状超高能伽马射线源，光谱延伸至PeV能量，PeV光度比蟹状星云高数倍；X射线观测约束星云内平均磁场约3μG，粒子加速效率接近或超过1。

Conclusion: 该结果挑战了脉冲星风星云粒子加速理论，表明存在非理想磁流体动力学条件，可能涉及终止激波上游的磁重联过程，需要新的理论模型解释极端加速效率。

Abstract: Pulsar wind nebulae (PWNe) are bubbles of relativistic particles, powered by the rotational energy loss of the central pulsars. The Crab Nebula, powered by the Milky Way's most energetic pulsar, was discovered by the Large High Altitude Air Shower Observatory (LHAASO) as a PeV gamma-ray emitter, thereby establishing it as an extreme particle accelerator along with multiwavelength observations. Here we report LHAASO's detection of a point-like ultrahigh-energy (UHE, photon energy $E>100\,$TeV) gamma-ray source associated with the PWN powered by PSR~J1849-0001, a pulsar of spindown power 50 times lower than the Crab pulsar. The measured gamma-ray spectrum extends to PeV energies following a power-law distribution, with the PeV luminosity a few times higher than that of the Crab Nebula. Combined X-ray observations constrain the average magnetic field within the PWN to about $3μ\,$G, and reveal an extreme particle acceleration efficiency approaching or even exceeding unity. The result challenges the particle acceleration theory in PWN and implies non-ideal magnetohydrodynamics (MHD) conditions within the accelerator, potentially involving magnetic reconnection upstream of the termination shock.

</details>


### [242] [Probing the potential high-energy messengers of the anticipated T Coronae Borealis outburst](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15570)
*O. Petruk,T. Kuzyo,S. Orlando,L. Chomiuk,F. Bocchino,M. Miceli,S. Ustamujic*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该研究通过结合三维流体动力学模拟和详细的扩散激波加速模型，预测了T CrB新星爆发后的伽马射线和中微子产生，评估了当前观测设备的探测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: T CrB是即将爆发的近邻再发新星，为实时研究新星粒子加速和高能辐射提供了独特机会。研究者旨在预测其爆发后的伽马射线和中微子信号，为观测做准备。

Method: 结合三维流体动力学模拟和扩散激波加速模型，考虑复杂的双星周介质（红巨星风、赤道密度增强、吸积盘），计算加速质子和电子的空间分辨谱，采用多区方法合成强子伽马射线（质子-质子相互作用）、轻子伽马射线（逆康普顿散射）及相关中微子发射。

Result: 早期高能辐射由抛射物主导，吸积盘在爆发后数小时内显著提升伽马射线通量和粒子归一化；考虑激波后速度梯度后，粒子最大能量可达PeV量级；GeV伽马射线是主要信使，而中微子探测仅在爆炸能量高、环境密度高的模型中可行。

Conclusion: 该研究为T CrB爆发的高能观测提供了详细预测，表明伽马射线观测前景良好，中微子探测条件较为苛刻，为利用当前伽马射线和中微子观测设备监测该事件奠定了基础。

Abstract: T Coronae Borealis (T CrB) is a nearby recurrent nova expected to erupt in the near future, offering a unique opportunity to study particle acceleration and high-energy emission from novae in real time. We investigate the production of gamma-rays and neutrinos following the T CrB outburst by combining three-dimensional hydrodynamical simulations with a detailed diffusive shock acceleration model. Our simulations account for the complex circumbinary medium, including the red giant wind, equatorial density enhancement, and accretion disk. We compute spatially resolved spectra of accelerated protons and electrons at the forward shock, accounting for downstream velocity gradients and variations in shock properties. Using a multi-zone approach, we synthesize hadronic gamma- ray emission from proton-proton interactions, leptonic gamma-rays from inverse-Compton scattering, and the associated neutrino emission. We present predicted gamma-ray spectra, light curves, and images from our numerical models of T CrB, and assess their detectability with current gamma-ray and neutrino observatories. We find that the early high-energy emission is dominated by the ejecta, with the accretion disk significantly boosting the gamma-ray flux and particle normalization during the first hours after the outburst. By incorporating velocity gradients in the post-shock flow, we demonstrate that maximum particle energies can reach the PeV scale in high-energy explosion scenarios. We show that while GeV gamma-rays are prominent messengers, neutrino detection is feasible primarily in models with high explosion energy and high ambient density.

</details>
